	if type(value) ~= "table" then return value end
	local kind = value[1]
	if kind == 1 then return environment.Color3.new(value[2], value[3], value[4]) end
	if kind == 2 then return environment.Vector3.new(value[2], value[3], value[4]) end
	if kind == 3 then return environment.Vector2.new(value[2], value[3]) end
	if kind == 4 then return environment.NumberRange.new(value[2], value[3]) end
	if kind == 5 then
		local points = {}
		for index = 2, #value, 3 do table.insert(points, environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(value[index], value[index + 1], value[index + 2])) end
		return environment.NumberSequence.new(points)
	end
	if kind == 6 then
		local points = {}
		for index = 2, #value, 4 do
			table.insert(points, environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(value[index], environment.Color3.new(value[index + 1], value[index + 2], value[index + 3])))
		end
		return environment.ColorSequence.new(points)
	end
	if kind == 7 then return environment.CFrame.new(table.unpack(value, 2, 13)) end
	if kind == 8 then
		local name = tostring(value[2]):gsub("^Enum%.", "")
		local ok, item = pcall(function() return environment.Enum[name][value[3]] end)
		return ok and item or nil
	end
	return nil
end

function AuraEngine.sequence(environment, sequence, scale, fade)
	local points = {}
	for _, point in sequence.Keypoints do
		local value = fade and 1 - (1 - point.Value) * scale or point.Value * scale
		table.insert(points, environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point.Time, value, point.Envelope * scale))
	end
	return environment.NumberSequence.new(points)
end

function AuraEngine.recolor(environment, sequence, tint)
	if environment.typeof(sequence) ~= "ColorSequence" then return environment.ColorSequence.new(tint) end
	local points = {}
	for _, point in sequence.Keypoints do
		local original = point.Value
		local high, low = math.max(original.R, original.G, original.B), math.min(original.R, original.G, original.B)
		local brightness = 0.3 + 0.7 * high
		local color = environment.Color3.new(tint.R * brightness, tint.G * brightness, tint.B * brightness)
		if low > 0.72 then color = color:Lerp(environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), (low - 0.72) / 0.28 * 0.75) end
		table.insert(points, environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(point.Time, color))
	end
	return environment.ColorSequence.new(points)
end

function AuraEngine.tune(environment, object, options)
	local size, density, bright, alpha = options.size or 1, options.density or 1, options.bright or 1, options.alpha or 1
	pcall(function()
		if object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			if size ~= 1 then
				object.Size = AuraEngine.sequence(environment, object.Size, size, false)
				object.Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(object.Speed.Min * size, object.Speed.Max * size)
				object.Acceleration *= size
			end
			if density ~= 1 then object.Rate *= density end
			if bright ~= 1 then object.Brightness *= bright end
			if alpha ~= 1 then object.Transparency = AuraEngine.sequence(environment, object.Transparency, alpha, true) end
			return
		end
		if object:IsA("Beam") then
			if size ~= 1 then
				object.Width0, object.Width1 = object.Width0 * size, object.Width1 * size
				object.CurveSize0, object.CurveSize1 = object.CurveSize0 * size, object.CurveSize1 * size
			end
			if bright ~= 1 then object.Brightness *= bright end
			if alpha ~= 1 then object.Transparency = AuraEngine.sequence(environment, object.Transparency, alpha, true) end
			return
		end
		if object:IsA("Trail") then
			if bright ~= 1 then object.Brightness *= bright end
			if alpha ~= 1 then object.Transparency = AuraEngine.sequence(environment, object.Transparency, alpha, true) end
			return
		end
		if object:IsA("Light") then
			if size ~= 1 then object.Range *= size end
			if bright ~= 1 then object.Brightness *= bright end
		end
	end)
end

function AuraEngine.build(environment, data, parent, build)
	if data.g and build.off[data.g] then return nil end
	local class = AURA_CLASSES[data.c]
	if not class then return nil end
	local object = environment.Instance.new(class)
	object.Name = data.n or class
	for property, value in data.p or {} do pcall(function() object[property] = AuraEngine.decode(environment, value) end) end
	if data.c == "A" then
		local position = object.Position
		if build.hatHost then
			object.Position = position * ((build.hatK or 1) * build.size)
		else
			local fit = build.fit
			if build.mapA then
				object.Position = build.mapA(position)
			else
				if fit then position = environment.Vector3.new(position.X * fit.x, fit.f + (position.Y + 3) * fit.y, position.Z * fit.z) end
				object.Position = position * build.size + build.liftV
			end
		end
	end
	if data.c == "PT" then
		object.Anchored, object.CanCollide, object.CanQuery, object.CanTouch, object.Massless, object.CastShadow = false, false, false, false, true, false
		local scale = (build.hatK or 1) * build.size
		object.Size *= scale
		local frame = AuraEngine.decode(environment, data.r) or environment.CFrame.new()
		frame = environment.CFrame.new((frame.Position - environment.Vector3.new(0, build.hatCrown or 0, 0)) * scale) * frame.Rotation
		if build.hatHost then
			object.CFrame = build.hatHost.CFrame * frame
			local weld = environment.Instance.new("Weld")
			weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.C0, weld.Parent = build.hatHost, object, frame, object
		end
		local alpha = build.options.alpha or 1
		if alpha ~= 1 then object.Transparency = 1 - (1 - object.Transparency) * alpha end
	end
	if data.c == "SH" then
		local scale = (build.hatK or 1) * build.size
		object.Scale, object.Offset = object.Scale * scale, object.Offset * scale
	end
	if data.c == "HL" then
		local alpha, bright = build.options.alpha or 1, build.options.bright or 1
		object.FillTransparency = 1 - math.clamp((1 - object.FillTransparency) * alpha * bright, 0, 1)
		object.OutlineTransparency = 1 - math.clamp((1 - object.OutlineTransparency) * alpha, 0, 1)
	end
	if data.c == "FI" and build.hatHost then object.Size = math.clamp(object.Size * (build.hatK or 1) * build.size, 2, 30) end
	if data.c == "P" then object.LockedToPart = true end
	if data.c == "T" then object.Lifetime = math.min(object.Lifetime, 0.3) end
	if data.i then build.ids[data.i] = object end
	if data.a or data.b then table.insert(build.links, {object, data.a, data.b}) end
	if data.g then AuraEngine.tune(environment, object, build.options) end
	if not data.g and build.hatTune and AURA_HAT_EFFECTS[data.c] then AuraEngine.tune(environment, object, build.hatTune) end
	object:SetAttribute("PulseVFX", true)
	object:SetAttribute("PulseVFXKey", build.key)
	for _, child in data.k or {} do AuraEngine.build(environment, child, object, build) end
	object.Parent = parent
	return object
end

function AuraEngine.link(build)
	for _, link in build.links do
		pcall(function()
			if link[2] and build.ids[link[2]] then link[1].Attachment0 = build.ids[link[2]] end
			if link[3] and build.ids[link[3]] then link[1].Attachment1 = build.ids[link[3]] end
		end)
	end
end

local AURA_R15 = {HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true, LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
	RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true, LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true, RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true}
local AURA_R6 = {HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, Torso = true, ["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true, ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true}
local AURA_LIMBS = {["Left Arm"] = "LeftUpperArm", ["Right Arm"] = "RightUpperArm", ["Left Leg"] = "LeftUpperLeg", ["Right Leg"] = "RightUpperLeg"}

function AuraEngine.fit(environment, character, root)
	local head, foot, torso = character:FindFirstChild("Head"), character:FindFirstChild("LeftFoot"), character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	if not head or not foot or not torso or not head:IsA("BasePart") or not foot:IsA("BasePart") or not torso:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
	local rest, links = {[root] = environment.CFrame.new()}, {}
	for _, joint in character:GetDescendants() do
		if joint:IsA("Motor6D") and joint.Part0 and joint.Part1 then
			table.insert(links, {joint.Part0, joint.Part1, joint.C0, joint.C1})
			continue
		end
		if not joint:IsA("AnimationConstraint") or not joint.Attachment0 or not joint.Attachment1 then continue end
		local first, second = joint.Attachment0, joint.Attachment1
		if first.Parent and second.Parent and first.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and second.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
			table.insert(links, {first.Parent, second.Parent, first.CFrame, second.CFrame})
		end
	end
	local changed = true
	while changed do
		changed = false
		for _, link in links do
			if not rest[link[1]] or rest[link[2]] then continue end
			rest[link[2]] = rest[link[1]] * link[3] * link[4]:Inverse()
			changed = true
		end
	end
	if not rest[head] or not rest[foot] then return nil end
	local floor = rest[foot].Position.Y - foot.Size.Y / 2
	local height = (rest[head].Position.Y - floor) / 4.5
	if floor > -1 or height < 0.4 or height > 2 then return nil end
	return {x = math.clamp(torso.Size.X / 2, 0.45, 1.8), y = height, z = math.clamp(torso.Size.Z, 0.45, 1.8), f = floor, rest = rest}
end

function AuraEngine.put(state, character, root, key, options)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local data = state.data[key]
	if not data then return end
	options = options or {}
	if data.hat then return AuraEngine.putHat(state, character, data, key, options) end
	local scale, lift = options.size or 1, options.lift or 0
	local isR15 = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") ~= nil
	local fit = isR15 and AuraEngine.fit(environment, character, root) or nil
	local build = {key = key, options = options, off = options.off or {}, size = scale, ids = {}, links = {}, liftV = environment.Vector3.zero}
	local rigScale, mapPosition
	if data.rig and fit then
		local torso, head = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"), character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local torsoRest, headRest = torso and fit.rest[torso], head and fit.rest[head]
		if torsoRest and headRest and headRest.Position.Y > torsoRest.Position.Y + 0.2 then
			local rig = data.rig
			local y = (headRest.Position.Y - torsoRest.Position.Y) / (rig.hy - rig.ty)
			local x, z = math.clamp(torso.Size.X / rig.tx, 0.3, 2.5), math.clamp(torso.Size.Z / rig.tz, 0.3, 2.5)
			local originY = torsoRest.Position.Y
			rigScale = environment.Vector3.new(x, y, z)
			mapPosition = function(position) return environment.Vector3.new(position.X * x, originY + (position.Y - rig.ty) * y, position.Z * z) end
		end
	end
	for _, hostData in data.h or {} do
		build.mapA = nil
		local part = character:FindFirstChild(hostData.n)
		local allowed = isR15 and AURA_R15[hostData.n] or not isR15 and AURA_R6[hostData.n]
		local host
		if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and allowed then
			build.liftV = part.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(environment.Vector3.new(0, lift, 0))
			build.fit = part == root and fit or nil
			if mapPosition then
				local localLift = build.liftV
				if part == root then
					build.mapA = function(position) return mapPosition(position) * scale + localLift end
				else
					local reference = AuraEngine.decode(environment, hostData.s) or part.Size
					local ratios = environment.Vector3.new(part.Size.X / math.max(reference.X, 0.01), part.Size.Y / math.max(reference.Y, 0.01), part.Size.Z / math.max(reference.Z, 0.01))
					build.mapA = function(position) return environment.Vector3.new(position.X * ratios.X, position.Y * ratios.Y, position.Z * ratios.Z) * scale + localLift end
				end
			end
			host = part
		else
			build.liftV, build.fit = environment.Vector3.zero, nil
			local original = AuraEngine.decode(environment, hostData.cf) or environment.CFrame.new()
			local position = original.Position
			if mapPosition then position = mapPosition(position) end
			if not mapPosition and fit then position = environment.Vector3.new(position.X * fit.x, fit.f + (position.Y + 3) * fit.y, position.Z * fit.z) end
			local frame = environment.CFrame.new(position * scale + environment.Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)) * original.Rotation
			if mapPosition then
				build.mapA = function(localPosition)
					return frame:PointToObjectSpace(mapPosition(original:PointToWorldSpace(localPosition)) * scale + environment.Vector3.new(0, lift, 0))
				end
			end
			host = environment.Instance.new("Part")
			host.Name = "PulseAuraHost"
			host.Size = (AuraEngine.decode(environment, hostData.s) or environment.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)) * scale
			if rigScale then host.Size = environment.Vector3.new(host.Size.X * rigScale.X, host.Size.Y * rigScale.Y, host.Size.Z * rigScale.Z) end
			host.Transparency, host.CanCollide, host.CanQuery, host.CanTouch, host.CastShadow, host.Massless = 1, false, false, false, false, true
			local limb = fit and AURA_LIMBS[hostData.n] and character:FindFirstChild(AURA_LIMBS[hostData.n])
			local anchor, offset = root, frame
			if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") and fit.rest[limb] then anchor, offset = limb, fit.rest[limb]:Inverse() * frame end
			host.CFrame = anchor.CFrame * offset
			local weld = environment.Instance.new("Weld")
			weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.C0, weld.Parent = anchor, host, offset, host
			host:SetAttribute("PulseVFX", true)
			host:SetAttribute("PulseVFXKey", key)
			host.Parent = character
		end
		for _, child in hostData.k or {} do AuraEngine.build(environment, child, host, build) end
	end
	AuraEngine.link(build)
end

local AURA_HAT_DEFAULTS = { ph_wire = { "Spin", "Wave" }, ph_galaxy = { "Float" }, ph_galaxyflame = { "Float" }, ph_phantom = { "Float" }, ph_glass = { "Spin" },
                ph_rainbowedge = { "Spin" }, ph_reddevil = { "Float" }, ph_evilrainbow = { "Float" }, ph_amethyst = { "Float" }, ph_molten = { "Float" },
                ph_toonblue = { "Float" }, ph_toonneon = { "Float" }, ph_goldflame = { "Float" }, ph_raidenglass = { "Spin" }, ph_pinkgold = { "Sway" },
                ph_pastel = { "Sway" }, ph_immember = { "Spin" }, ph_immtoxic = { "Spin" }, ph_lantred = { "Spin" }, ph_lantgreen = { "Spin" }, ph_jade = { "Sway" },
                ph_cyberoni = { "Float" }, ph_cyberronin = { "Float" }, ph_crane = { "Spin" }, ph_redglow = { "Spin" }, ph_bells = { "Sway" }, ph_bluekasa = { "Spin" } }
local AURA_HAT_FIT = { ph_amethyst = 0.773, ph_bamboo = 0.701, ph_bells = 0.711, ph_bluekasa = 0.793, ph_crane = 0.704, ph_cyberoni = 0.694, ph_cyberronin = 0.708,
                ph_dragon = 0.723, ph_evilrainbow = 0.773, ph_galaxy = 0.682, ph_galaxyflame = 0.682, ph_glass = 0.731, ph_goldflame = 1.017, ph_goldiron = 0.578,
                ph_immblack = 0.745, ph_immember = 0.745, ph_immpurple = 0.745, ph_immred = 0.745, ph_immtoxic = 0.745, ph_jade = 0.78, ph_lantgreen = 0.729,
                ph_lantred = 0.729, ph_molten = 0.773, ph_mossy = 1.024, ph_panda = 0.93, ph_pastel = 0.724, ph_phantom = 0.581, ph_pinkgold = 0.93, ph_purple = 0.719,
                ph_raidenglass = 0.902, ph_rainbowedge = 0.705, ph_reddevil = 0.705, ph_redglow = 0.797, ph_redronin = 0.723, ph_ronin = 0.776, ph_shadowkasa = 0.748,
                ph_toonblue = 0.701, ph_toonneon = 0.812, ph_veil = 0.794, ph_wire = 0.579 }
local AuraHat = {}

function AuraHat.accessoryKind(environment, accessory)
	local kind
	pcall(function()
		if accessory.AccessoryType == environment.Enum.AccessoryType.Hair then kind = "hair" end
		if accessory.AccessoryType == environment.Enum.AccessoryType.Hat then kind = "hat" end
	end)
	if kind then return kind end
	local handle = accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
	if handle and handle:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment") then return "hair" end
	if handle and handle:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment") then return "hat" end
	return nil
end

function AuraHat.hiddenParts(environment, character, hide)
	local result = {}
	for _, accessory in character:GetChildren() do
		if not accessory:IsA("Accessory") then continue end
		local kind = AuraHat.accessoryKind(environment, accessory)
		if not (kind == "hat" and hide.hat or kind == "hair" and hide.hair) then continue end
		for _, object in accessory:GetDescendants() do if object:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(result, object) end end
	end
	return result
end

function AuraHat.hairLift(environment, character, head, crown)
	local top
	for _, accessory in character:GetChildren() do
		if not accessory:IsA("Accessory") or AuraHat.accessoryKind(environment, accessory) ~= "hair" then continue end
		local handle = accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if not handle or not handle:IsA("MeshPart") or handle.Transparency >= 0.95 then continue end
		local frame, size = head.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(handle.CFrame), handle.Size
		if math.abs(frame.X) >= head.Size.X or math.abs(frame.Z) >= head.Size.Z then continue end
		local height = frame.Y + math.abs(frame.XVector.Y) * size.X / 2 + math.abs(frame.YVector.Y) * size.Y / 2 + math.abs(frame.ZVector.Y) * size.Z / 2
		if not top or height > top then top = height end
	end
	return top and math.clamp((top - crown.Y) * 0.7, 0, 0.9) or 0
end

function AuraHat.collect(state, hat, roots)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local objects = {}
	for _, root in roots do
		table.insert(objects, root)
		for _, object in root:GetDescendants() do table.insert(objects, object) end
	end
	for _, object in objects do
		local record
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then record = {object = object, kind = "part", color = object.Color, transparency = object.Transparency} end
		if object:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
			local material = object.Parent and object.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and object.Parent.Material
			record = {object = object, kind = "mesh", color = object.VertexColor, multiplier = material == environment.Enum.Material.ForceField and 0 or (material == environment.Enum.Material.Neon and 0.9 or 1.3)}
		end
		if object:IsA("Highlight") then record = {object = object, kind = "highlight", color = object.OutlineColor, second = object.FillColor, transparency = object.FillTransparency, outline = object.OutlineTransparency} end
		if object:IsA("Fire") then record = {object = object, kind = "fire", color = object.Color, second = object.SecondaryColor} end
		if object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Beam") or object:IsA("Trail") then record = {object = object, kind = "sequence", color = object.Color} end
		if object:IsA("Light") then record = {object = object, kind = "light", color = object.Color} end
		if not record then continue end
		local part = object:IsA("BasePart") and object or object:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
		local position = part and hat.root.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(part.Position) or environment.Vector3.zero
		record.phase = math.atan2(position.Z, position.X) / (2 * math.pi) % 1
		table.insert(hat.colors, record)
	end
end

function AuraHat.restore(hat)
	for _, record in hat.colors do
		pcall(function()
			local object = record.object
			if record.kind == "part" then object.Color, object.Transparency = record.color, record.transparency; return end
			if record.kind == "mesh" then object.VertexColor = record.color; return end
			if record.kind == "highlight" then
				object.OutlineColor, object.FillColor, object.FillTransparency, object.OutlineTransparency = record.color, record.second, record.transparency, record.outline
				return
			end
			if record.kind == "fire" then object.Color, object.SecondaryColor = record.color, record.second; return end
			object.Color = record.color
		end)
	end
end

function AuraHat.paint(state, hat, phase, wave)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	for _, record in hat.colors do
		local object = record.object
		local color = environment.Color3.fromHSV((phase + (wave and record.phase or 0)) % 1, 0.78, 1)
		if record.kind == "part" then object.Color = color; continue end
		if record.kind == "mesh" then
			if record.multiplier > 0 then object.VertexColor = environment.Vector3.new(color.R, color.G, color.B) * record.multiplier end
			continue
		end
		if record.kind == "highlight" then object.OutlineColor, object.FillColor = color, color; continue end
		if record.kind == "fire" then object.Color, object.SecondaryColor = color, color:Lerp(environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.35); continue end
		if record.kind == "sequence" then object.Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(color); continue end
		object.Color = color
	end
end

function AuraHat.pulse(hat, value)
	for _, record in hat.colors do
		if record.kind == "part" and record.transparency < 1 then record.object.Transparency = record.transparency + (1 - record.transparency) * 0.6 * value end
		if record.kind == "highlight" then record.object.FillTransparency = record.transparency + (1 - record.transparency) * 0.8 * value end
	end
end

function AuraHat.step(state)
	local environment, now = state.context.environment, state.context.environment.os.clock()
	local hats, hidden = {}, {}
	for _, hat in state.hats do
		if not hat.root.Parent or not hat.weld.Parent then continue end
		table.insert(hats, hat)
		local elapsed, motion, scale, frame = (now - hat.started) * hat.speed, hat.motion, hat.scale, hat.base
		if motion == "Spin" then frame *= environment.CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 1.5, 0) end
		if motion == "Float" then frame *= environment.CFrame.new(0, (math.sin(elapsed * 2.2) + 1) * 0.08 * scale, 0) end
		if motion == "Spin + Float" then frame *= environment.CFrame.new(0, (math.sin(elapsed * 2.2) + 1) * 0.08 * scale, 0) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 1.5, 0) end
		if motion == "Sway" then frame *= environment.CFrame.Angles(math.sin(elapsed * 1.7) * 0.1, 0, math.sin(elapsed * 1.3 + 1.1) * 0.1) end
		if motion == "Wobble" then frame *= environment.CFrame.fromAxisAngle(environment.Vector3.new(math.cos(elapsed * 2.6), 0, math.sin(elapsed * 2.6)), 0.13) end
		if motion == "Hover" then frame *= environment.CFrame.new(0, (0.45 + math.sin(elapsed * 1.8) * 0.1) * scale, 0) * environment.CFrame.Angles(0, elapsed * 0.9, 0) end
		if motion == "Bounce" then frame *= environment.CFrame.new(0, math.abs(math.sin(elapsed * 3.2)) * 0.2 * scale, 0) end
		if motion ~= "None" or not hat.still then hat.weld.C0, hat.still = frame, motion == "None" end
		if hat.fx ~= "None" and now - (hat.fxAt or 0) >= 0.025 then
			hat.fxAt = now
			if hat.fx == "Rainbow" or hat.fx == "Wave" then AuraHat.paint(state, hat, elapsed * 0.22 % 1, hat.fx == "Wave") end
			if hat.fx == "Pulse" then AuraHat.pulse(hat, 0.5 - 0.5 * math.cos(elapsed * 3.2)) end
		end
		if hat.hide then
			if now - (hat.hideAt or 0) > 0.5 then hat.hideAt, hat.hideList = now, AuraHat.hiddenParts(environment, hat.character, hat.hide) end
			for _, part in hat.hideList or {} do
				hidden[part] = true
				if part.LocalTransparencyModifier < 1 then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
			end
		end
	end
	state.hats = hats
	for part in state.hidden do if not hidden[part] then pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end) end end
	state.hidden = hidden
end

function AuraEngine.putHat(state, character, data, key, options)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then return end
	local size = options.size or 1
	local width = head:IsA("MeshPart") and (head.Size.X + head.Size.Z) / 2 or math.min(head.Size.X, head.Size.Z) * 1.2
	local fit = math.clamp(width / 1.196, 0.5, 2.5) * (AURA_HAT_FIT[key] or 1)
	local attachment = head:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment")
	local crown = attachment and attachment:IsA("Attachment") and attachment.CFrame or environment.CFrame.new(0, head.Size.Y / 2, 0)
	local hair = 0
	if not (options.mine and options.hideHair == true) then
		local ok, value = pcall(AuraHat.hairLift, environment, character, head, crown)
		if ok then hair = value end
	end
	local base = crown * environment.CFrame.new(0, (options.lift or 0) + hair, 0)
	local root = environment.Instance.new("Part")
	root.Name, root.Size, root.Transparency, root.Anchored = "PulseHatRoot", environment.Vector3.new(0.05, 0.05, 0.05), 1, false
	root.CanCollide, root.CanQuery, root.CanTouch, root.Massless, root.CastShadow = false, false, false, true, false
	root.CFrame = head.CFrame * base
	local weld = environment.Instance.new("Weld")
	weld.Name, weld.Part0, weld.Part1, weld.C0, weld.Parent = "PulseHatWeld", head, root, base, root
	root:SetAttribute("PulseVFX", true)
	root:SetAttribute("PulseVFXKey", key)
	root.Parent = character
	local build = {key = key, options = options, off = options.off or {}, size = size, ids = {}, links = {}, liftV = environment.Vector3.zero,
		hatHost = root, hatK = fit, hatCrown = data.crown or 0.6,
		hatTune = {size = fit * size, density = options.density or 1, bright = options.bright or 1, alpha = options.alpha or 1}}
	local roots = {}
	for _, host in data.h or {} do
		for _, child in host.k or {} do
			local built = AuraEngine.build(environment, child, character, build)
			if built then table.insert(roots, built) end
		end
	end
	AuraEngine.link(build)
	local defaults = AURA_HAT_DEFAULTS[key] or {}
	local hat = {character = character, root = root, weld = weld, base = base, scale = fit * size, key = key, started = environment.os.clock(), still = true,
		colors = {}, motion = options.motion or defaults[1] or "None", fx = options.cfx or defaults[2] or "None", speed = math.clamp(tonumber(options.aspd) or 1, 0.1, 3)}
	if options.mine and (options.hideHat ~= false or options.hideHair == true) then hat.hide = {hat = options.hideHat ~= false, hair = options.hideHair == true} end
	AuraHat.collect(state, hat, roots)
	table.insert(state.hats, hat)
end

local AuraTint = {}

function AuraTint.roots(state, character)
	local roots = {}
	if not character then return roots end
	table.insert(roots, character)
	local context, globals = state.context, state.context.environment.getgenv()
	local model
	if character == context.player.Character then
		if type(globals.__pulseSkinModel) == "function" then
			local ok, result = pcall(globals.__pulseSkinModel)
			if ok then model = result end
		end
	else
		local player = context.players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
		local remote = rawget(globals, "__pulseRemoteSkin")
		if player and type(remote) == "table" and type(remote.liveModel) == "function" then
			local ok, result = pcall(remote.liveModel, player)
			if ok then model = result end
		end
	end
	if model then table.insert(roots, model) end
	return roots
end

function AuraTint.applyObject(state, object, color, glow, remote)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local sequence = object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Beam") or object:IsA("Trail")
	local light = object:IsA("PointLight") or object:IsA("SpotLight") or object:IsA("SurfaceLight")
	if sequence then
		if state.colors[object] == nil then state.colors[object] = object.Color end
		if state.emission[object] == nil then state.emission[object] = object.LightEmission end
		object.Color = color and AuraEngine.recolor(environment, state.colors[object], color) or state.colors[object]
		object.LightEmission = color and state.emission[object] * glow or state.emission[object]
		return
	end
	if light then
		if state.colors[object] == nil then state.colors[object] = object.Color end
		if state.secondary[object] == nil then state.secondary[object] = object.Brightness end
		object.Color, object.Brightness = color or state.colors[object], color and state.secondary[object] * glow or state.secondary[object]
		return
	end
	if remote then return end
	if object:IsA("BasePart") and object.Name ~= "PulseAuraHost" or object:IsA("Fire") then
		if state.colors[object] == nil then state.colors[object] = object.Color end
		object.Color = color or state.colors[object]
		return
	end
	if object:IsA("Highlight") then
		if state.colors[object] == nil then state.colors[object] = object.OutlineColor end
		if state.secondary[object] == nil then state.secondary[object] = object.FillColor end
		object.OutlineColor, object.FillColor = color or state.colors[object], color or state.secondary[object]
		return
	end
	if object:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
		local material = object.Parent and object.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and object.Parent.Material
		if material == environment.Enum.Material.ForceField then return end
		if state.colors[object] == nil then state.colors[object] = object.VertexColor end
		object.VertexColor = color and environment.Vector3.new(color.R, color.G, color.B) * (material == environment.Enum.Material.Neon and 1 or 1.6) or state.colors[object]
	end
end

function AuraTint.apply(state, character, remoteOptions)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	character = character or context.player.Character
	if not character then return end
	local remote = remoteOptions ~= nil
	local globals = environment.getgenv()
	local common = not remote and globals.__pulseFxTintOn and globals.__pulseFxColor and globals.__pulseFxColor() or nil
	local options = context.configuration.vfxCol or {}
	for _, root in AuraTint.roots(state, character) do
		for _, object in root:GetDescendants() do
			if not object:GetAttribute("PulseVFX") then continue end
			pcall(function()
				local key = object:GetAttribute("PulseVFXKey")
				local color, glow = common, 1
				if remote then
					local packed = remoteOptions[key]
					if type(packed) == "number" and packed > 1 then
						local brightness, rgb = math.floor(packed / 16777216), packed % 16777216
						color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(math.floor(rgb / 65536) % 256, math.floor(rgb / 256) % 256, rgb % 256)
						if brightness > 0 then glow = brightness / 100 end
					end
				else
					local custom = key and options[key]
					if custom and custom.custom then
						color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(custom.r, custom.g, custom.b)
						glow = custom.glow and custom.glow / 100 or 1
					end
				end
				AuraTint.applyObject(state, object, color, glow, remote)
				for _, child in object:GetDescendants() do AuraTint.applyObject(state, child, color, glow, remote) end
			end)
		end
	end
end

function AuraTint.install(context)
	local environment, globals = context.environment, context.environment.getgenv()
	local state = {context = context, colors = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), emission = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), secondary = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})}
	local apply = function() AuraTint.apply(state) end
	local applyFor = function(character, options) if character and type(options) == "table" then AuraTint.apply(state, character, options) end end
	globals.__pulseVFXTint, globals.__pulseVFXTintFor = apply, applyFor
	context.connect(context.player.CharacterAdded, function()
		environment.task.wait(0.8)
		pcall(apply)
	end)
	return state, function()
		for object in state.colors do pcall(AuraTint.applyObject, state, object, nil, 1, false) end
		if globals.__pulseVFXTint == apply then globals.__pulseVFXTint = nil end
		if globals.__pulseVFXTintFor == applyFor then globals.__pulseVFXTintFor = nil end
	end
end

local AuraFeature = {}

function AuraFeature.options(state, key)
	local value = state.options[key]
	if type(value) ~= "table" then return {mine = true} end
	return {size = tonumber(value.size) or 1, lift = tonumber(value.lift) or 0, density = tonumber(value.density) or 1,
		bright = tonumber(value.bright) or 1, alpha = tonumber(value.alpha) or 1, off = type(value.off) == "table" and value.off or {},
		mine = true, motion = type(value.motion) == "string" and value.motion or nil, cfx = type(value.cfx) == "string" and value.cfx or nil,
		aspd = tonumber(value.aspd), hideHat = value.hideHat, hideHair = value.hideHair}
end

function AuraFeature.clear(state, character)
	character = character or state.context.player.Character
	for _, root in AuraTint.roots(state, character) do
		for _, object in root:GetDescendants() do
			if object:GetAttribute("PulseVFX") then pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
		end
	end
end

function AuraFeature.apply(state, character, selected)
	if state.alive == false then return end
	local own = selected == nil
	character = character or state.context.player.Character
	if not character then return end
	selected = selected or state.selected
	AuraFeature.clear(state, character)
	local roots = AuraTint.roots(state, character)
	local target = roots[2] or roots[1]
	local root = target and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return end
	for _, item in AURA_CATALOG do
		if not selected[item.key] then continue end
		local options = own and AuraFeature.options(state, item.key) or nil
		local ok, raised = pcall(AuraEngine.put, state, target, root, item.key, options)
		if not ok then state.context.environment.warn("[Pulse] aura " .. item.key .. ": " .. tostring(raised)) end
	end
	if own then
		local tint = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseVFXTint
		if tint then pcall(tint) end
	end
end

function AuraFeature.groups(state, key)
	local data, found = state.data[key], {}
	local function collect(node)
		if node.g then found[node.g] = true end
		for _, child in node.k or {} do collect(child) end
	end
	for _, host in data and data.h or {} do for _, child in host.k or {} do collect(child) end end
	local result = {}
	for name in found do table.insert(result, name) end
	table.sort(result)
	return result
end

function AuraFeature.mine(state)
	local environment, globals = state.context.environment, state.context.environment.getgenv()
	local common = globals.__pulseFxTintOn and globals.__pulseFxColor and globals.__pulseFxColor() or nil
	local result, count = nil, 0
	for _, item in AURA_CATALOG do
		if not state.selected[item.key] then continue end
		count += 1
		if count > 6 then break end
		result = result or {}
		local options = state.options[item.key]
		local custom = type(options) == "table" and options.custom and options or nil
		local color = custom and environment.Color3.fromRGB(custom.r, custom.g, custom.b) or common
		if not color then result[item.key] = true; continue end
		local glow = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(custom and custom.glow) or 100), 0, 250)
		result[item.key] = math.floor(color.R * 255) * 65536 + math.floor(color.G * 255) * 256 + math.floor(color.B * 255) + glow * 16777216
	end
	return result
end

function AuraFeature.filtered(state)
	local result, query = {}, state.query:lower()
	for _, item in AURA_CATALOG do
		if state.category ~= "All" and not (state.category == "Worn" and state.selected[item.key]) and item.category ~= state.category then continue end
		if query ~= "" and not item.label:lower():find(query, 1, true) then continue end
		table.insert(result, {code = item.key, label = item.label})
	end
	return result
end

function AuraFeature.controls(state)
	local context, environment, section = state.context, state.context.environment, state.context.section
	local binding = true
	local handles = {}
	local function record()
		local current = state.options[state.key]
		if type(current) ~= "table" then current = {}; state.options[state.key] = current end
		return current
	end
	local function reapply()
		if state.selected[state.key] then environment.task.spawn(function() AuraFeature.apply(state) end) end
	end
	local function set(field, value)
		if binding or not state.key then return end
		record()[field] = value
		reapply()
	end
	local function synchronize()
		if not state.key then return end
		local saved = state.options[state.key] or {}
		binding = true
		if handles.enabled then handles.enabled.SetSilent(state.selected[state.key] == true) end
		for _, spec in {{"size", 100, 100}, {"lift", 1, 0}, {"density", 100, 100}, {"bright", 100, 100}, {"alpha", 100, 100}, {"aspd", 100, 100}, {"glow", 1, 100}} do
			local handle = handles[spec[1]]
			if handle then handle.Set((tonumber(saved[spec[1]]) or spec[3] / spec[2]) * spec[2]) end
		end
		if handles.custom then handles.custom.SetSilent(saved.custom == true) end
		if handles.color then handles.color.Set(environment.Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(saved.r) or 255, tonumber(saved.g) or 255, tonumber(saved.b) or 255)) end
		local defaults = AURA_HAT_DEFAULTS[state.key] or {}
		if handles.motion then handles.motion.Set(saved.motion or defaults[1] or "None") end
		if handles.fx then handles.fx.Set(saved.cfx or defaults[2] or "None") end
		if handles.hideHat then handles.hideHat.SetSilent(saved.hideHat ~= false) end
		if handles.hideHair then handles.hideHair.SetSilent(saved.hideHair == true) end
		local groups = AuraFeature.groups(state, state.key)
		local options = {}
		for _, group in groups do table.insert(options, {code = group, label = group}) end
		state.group = groups[1]
		if handles.parts then handles.parts.SetOptions(options); if state.group then handles.parts.Set(state.group) end end
		if handles.partEnabled then handles.partEnabled.SetSilent(not (saved.off and saved.off[state.group])) end
		binding = false
	end
	local function updateList()
		local options = AuraFeature.filtered(state)
		if not handles.items then return end
		handles.items.SetOptions(options)
		local found = false
		for _, item in options do if item.code == state.key then found = true; break end end
		if not found and options[1] then state.key = options[1].code; handles.items.Set(state.key) end
		synchronize()
	end
	local categories = {"All", "Worn", "Flames", "Energy", "Wings & Halos", "Mystic", "Dark", "Ice & Love", "Hats"}
	local options = {}
	for _, name in categories do table.insert(options, {code = name, label = name}) end
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Category", NoSave = true, Default = "All", Options = options, Callback = function(value) state.category = value; updateList() end})
	local native = section:LastSection()
	if native and native.Input then native:Input({Text = "Search auras", Live = true, Callback = function(value) state.query = tostring(value or ""); updateList() end}) end
	handles.items = section:Dropdown({Text = "Aura", NoSave = true, Default = state.key, Options = AuraFeature.filtered(state), Callback = function(key)
		state.key = key
		if not binding then synchronize() end
	end})
	handles.enabled = section:Toggle({Text = "Equip aura", NoSave = true, Default = state.selected[state.key] == true, Callback = function(value)
		if binding or not state.key then return end
		state.selected[state.key] = value and true or nil
		environment.task.spawn(function() AuraFeature.apply(state) end)
		if state.category == "Worn" then updateList() end
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Remove all", Callback = function()
		for _, item in AURA_CATALOG do state.selected[item.key] = nil end
		environment.task.spawn(function() AuraFeature.apply(state) end)
		updateList()
	end})
	for _, spec in {{"Size", "size", 30, 200, 100, 100, "%"}, {"Height", "lift", -3, 3, 0, 1, ""},
		{"Density", "density", 10, 200, 100, 100, "%"}, {"Brightness", "bright", 0, 300, 100, 100, "%"}, {"Opacity", "alpha", 10, 100, 100, 100, "%"}} do
		handles[spec[2]] = section:Slider({Text = spec[1], NoSave = true, Min = spec[3], Max = spec[4], Default = spec[5], Decimals = spec[2] == "lift" and 1 or 0, Suffix = spec[7], Callback = function(value) set(spec[2], value / spec[6]) end})
	end
	handles.custom = section:Toggle({Text = "Custom Color", NoSave = true, Default = false, Callback = function(value)
		if binding or not state.key then return end
		local saved = record()
		if value then saved.r, saved.g, saved.b = tonumber(saved.r) or 255, tonumber(saved.g) or 255, tonumber(saved.b) or 255 end
		saved.glow = saved.glow or 100
		set("custom", not not value)
	end})
	handles.color = section:Colorpicker({Text = "Color", NoSave = true, Default = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Callback = function(color)
		if binding or not state.key or environment.typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
		local saved = record()
		saved.r, saved.g, saved.b = math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)
		saved.custom, saved.glow = true, saved.glow or 100
		if handles.custom then handles.custom.SetSilent(true) end
		local tint = environment.getgenv().__pulseVFXTint
		if tint then pcall(tint) end
	end})
	handles.glow = section:Slider({Text = "Glow", NoSave = true, Min = 0, Max = 250, Default = 100, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value) set("glow", value) end})
	local function choices(values)
		local result = {}
		for _, value in values do table.insert(result, {code = value, label = value}) end
		return result
	end
	handles.motion = section:Dropdown({Text = "Hat Animation", NoSave = true, Default = "None", Options = choices({"None", "Spin", "Float", "Spin + Float", "Sway", "Wobble", "Hover", "Bounce"}), Callback = function(value) set("motion", value) end})
	handles.fx = section:Dropdown({Text = "Hat Effect", NoSave = true, Default = "None", Options = choices({"None", "Rainbow", "Wave", "Pulse"}), Callback = function(value) set("cfx", value) end})
	handles.aspd = section:Slider({Text = "Hat Speed", NoSave = true, Min = 10, Max = 300, Default = 100, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value) set("aspd", value / 100) end})
	handles.hideHat = section:Toggle({Text = "Hide Hats", NoSave = true, Default = true, Callback = function(value) set("hideHat", not not value) end})
	handles.hideHair = section:Toggle({Text = "Hide Hair", NoSave = true, Default = false, Callback = function(value) set("hideHair", not not value) end})
	handles.parts = section:Dropdown({Text = "Parts", NoSave = true, Default = "", Options = {}, Callback = function(value)
		state.group = value
		if binding then return end
		if handles.partEnabled then
			local saved = state.options[state.key] or {}
			binding = true; handles.partEnabled.SetSilent(not (saved.off and saved.off[value])); binding = false
		end
	end})
	handles.partEnabled = section:Toggle({Text = "Show part", NoSave = true, Default = true, Callback = function(value)
		if binding or not state.key or not state.group then return end
		local saved = record()
		saved.off = type(saved.off) == "table" and saved.off or {}
		saved.off[state.group] = value and nil or true
		reapply()
	end})
	binding = false
	synchronize()
	state.synchronize = updateList
end

function AuraFeature.install(context)
	local environment, globals = context.environment, context.environment.getgenv()
	context.configuration.vfx = type(context.configuration.vfx) == "table" and context.configuration.vfx or {}
	context.configuration.vfxCol = type(context.configuration.vfxCol) == "table" and context.configuration.vfxCol or {}
	local state = {context = context, data = createAuraData(), selected = context.configuration.vfx, options = context.configuration.vfxCol,
		hats = {}, hidden = {}, alive = true, key = AURA_CATALOG[1].key, group = nil, category = "All", query = ""}
	local apply = function(character, selected) AuraFeature.apply(state, character, selected) end
	local clear = function(character) AuraFeature.clear(state, character) end
	local mine = function() return AuraFeature.mine(state) end
	local pending = false
	local changed = function(player)
		if not state.alive then return end
		if player and player ~= context.player then
			local notify = rawget(globals, "__pulseMirDirty")
			if type(notify) == "function" then pcall(notify, player) end
			return
		end
		if pending then return end
		pending = true
		environment.task.delay(0.1, function() pending = false; pcall(apply) end)
	end
	globals.__pulseVFXApply, globals.__pulseVFXClear, globals.__pulseVFXMine, globals.__pulseSkinChanged = apply, clear, mine, changed
	AuraFeature.controls(state)
	if context.applyHooks then table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		if not state.alive then return end
		state.synchronize()
		environment.task.spawn(function() pcall(apply) end)
	end) end
	context.connect(context.player.CharacterAdded, function()
		environment.task.wait(0.8)
		pcall(apply)
	end)
	context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function()
		if state.alive and (#state.hats > 0 or next(state.hidden)) then pcall(AuraHat.step, state) end
	end)
	environment.task.spawn(function() pcall(apply) end)
	return state, function()
		state.alive = false
		pcall(clear)
		pcall(AuraHat.step, state)
		if globals.__pulseVFXApply == apply then globals.__pulseVFXApply = nil end
		if globals.__pulseVFXClear == clear then globals.__pulseVFXClear = nil end
		if globals.__pulseVFXMine == mine then globals.__pulseVFXMine = nil end
		if globals.__pulseSkinChanged == changed then globals.__pulseSkinChanged = nil end
	end
end

local SELF_CHAM_STYLES = {
	ForceField = {material = "ForceField"}, Hologram = {material = "ForceField", clean = true, edge = true},
	Flat = {material = "SmoothPlastic", clean = true}, Neon = {material = "Neon", clean = true},
	Glow = {material = "Neon", clean = true, edge = true}, Glass = {material = "Glass", clean = true, reflectance = 0.25, transparency = 0.35},
	Crystal = {material = "Glass", clean = true, reflectance = 0.5, transparency = 0.55, edge = true},
	Chrome = {material = "SmoothPlastic", clean = true, reflectance = 0.6}, Metallic = {material = "Foil", clean = true, reflectance = 0.3},
	Ice = {material = "Ice", clean = true, reflectance = 0.2, transparency = 0.15}, Lava = {material = "CrackedLava", clean = true},
	Ghost = {material = "SmoothPlastic", clean = true, reflectance = 0.1, transparency = 0.6, edge = true},
	Wireframe = {material = "ForceField", clean = true, transparency = 0.85, wire = true},
	Silhouette = {fill = 0, edge = true}, Tint = {fill = 0.5}, Outline = {edge = true},
}
local SELF_CHAM_ORDER = {"ForceField", "Hologram", "Flat", "Neon", "Glow", "Glass", "Crystal", "Chrome", "Metallic", "Ice", "Lava", "Ghost", "Wireframe", "Silhouette", "Tint", "Outline"}
local SELF_CHAM_ANIMATIONS = {"None", "Pulse", "Breathe", "Fade", "Flash", "Rainbow", "Wave", "Gradient"}
local SelfChams = {}

function SelfChams.color(environment, value, fallback)
	if type(value) == "table" and tonumber(value.r) and tonumber(value.g) and tonumber(value.b) then
		return environment.Color3.fromRGB(value.r, value.g, value.b)
	end
	return fallback
end

function SelfChams.saveColor(color)
	return {r = math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), g = math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), b = math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)}
end

function SelfChams.settings(context)
	local configuration, environment = context.configuration, context.environment
	configuration.chamsCfg = type(configuration.chamsCfg) == "table" and configuration.chamsCfg or {}
	local fallback = SelfChams.color(environment, configuration.chamsCol, environment.Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255))
	local settings = {}
	for _, kind in {"self", "tool"} do
		local saved = type(configuration.chamsCfg[kind]) == "table" and configuration.chamsCfg[kind] or {}
		configuration.chamsCfg[kind] = saved
		settings[kind] = {on = false, col = SelfChams.color(environment, saved.c, fallback), col2 = SelfChams.color(environment, saved.c2, environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)),
			style = SELF_CHAM_STYLES[tostring(saved.style)] and saved.style or "ForceField",
			anim = table.find(SELF_CHAM_ANIMATIONS, tostring(saved.anim)) and saved.anim or "None",
			speed = math.clamp(tonumber(saved.speed) or 100, 10, 300), alpha = math.clamp(tonumber(saved.alpha) or 0, 0, 90), saved = saved}
	end
	return settings
end

function SelfChams.save(state, kind)
	local settings = state.settings[kind]
	local saved = settings.saved
	saved.c, saved.c2 = SelfChams.saveColor(settings.col), SelfChams.saveColor(settings.col2)
	saved.style, saved.anim, saved.speed, saved.alpha = settings.style, settings.anim, settings.speed, settings.alpha
	if kind == "self" then state.context.configuration.chamsCol = SelfChams.saveColor(settings.col) end
end

function SelfChams.same(environment, first, second)
	if environment.typeof(first) == "Color3" and environment.typeof(second) == "Color3" then
		return math.abs(first.R - second.R) < 0.004 and math.abs(first.G - second.G) < 0.004 and math.abs(first.B - second.B) < 0.004
	end
	if type(first) == "number" and type(second) == "number" then return math.abs(first - second) < 0.002 end
	return first == second
end

function SelfChams.write(state, object, record, property, desired)
	local current, previous = object[property], record.written[property]
	if previous == nil or not SelfChams.same(state.context.environment, current, previous) then record.original[property] = current end
	if type(desired) == "function" then desired = desired(record.original[property]) end
	if desired == nil then record.written[property] = nil; return end
	if not SelfChams.same(state.context.environment, current, desired) then object[property] = desired end
	record.written[property] = desired
end

function SelfChams.restore(state, object, record)
	if record.clothing then
		if not object.Parent and record.parent and record.parent.Parent then object.Parent = record.parent end
		return
	end
	if not object.Parent then return end
	for property, original in record.original do
		local written = record.written[property]
		if written ~= nil then
			pcall(function() if SelfChams.same(state.context.environment, object[property], written) then object[property] = original end end)
		end
	end
end

function SelfChams.animated(state, settings, time, height)
	local color, second, animation = settings.col, settings.col2, settings.anim
	local phase = time * settings.speed / 100
	if animation == "Pulse" then
		local hue, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()
		return state.context.environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue, saturation, value * (0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(phase * 4)))), 0
	end
	if animation == "Breathe" then return color, 0.7 * (0.5 - 0.5 * math.cos(phase * 3)) end
	if animation == "Fade" then return color:Lerp(second, 0.5 - 0.5 * math.cos(phase * 2.5)), 0 end
	if animation == "Flash" then return math.floor(phase * 5) % 2 == 0 and color or second, 0 end
	if animation == "Rainbow" or animation == "Wave" then
		local _, saturation, value = color:ToHSV()
		local hue = animation == "Wave" and phase * 0.35 - height * 0.08 or phase * 0.25
		return state.context.environment.Color3.fromHSV(hue % 1, math.max(saturation, 0.55), math.max(value, 0.8)), 0
	end
	if animation == "Gradient" then return second:Lerp(color, 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(phase * 2.2 - height * 0.8)), 0 end
	return color, 0
end

function SelfChams.transparency(original, style, settings, animation)
	return 1 - (1 - original) * (1 - (style or 0)) * (1 - settings.alpha / 100) * (1 - animation)
end

function SelfChams.folder(state)
	local camera = state.context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	if state.folder and state.folder.Parent == camera then return state.folder end
	if state.folder then pcall(function() state.folder:Destroy() end) end
	local folder = state.context.environment.Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name, folder.Parent, state.folder = "PulseSelfChams", camera, folder
	return folder
end

function SelfChams.appearance(state)
	local camera = state.context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local model = camera and camera:FindFirstChild("PulseSkin")
	return model and model:IsA("Model") and model or nil
end

function SelfChams.displays(state, character)
	local result = {}
	for _, kind in {"Knife", "Gun"} do
		local reference = character:FindFirstChild("DisplayRef" .. kind)
		local display = reference and reference:IsA("ObjectValue") and reference.Value
		if display and display.Parent then table.insert(result, display) end
	end
	local folder = state.context.workspace:FindFirstChild("WeaponDisplays")
	for _, display in folder and folder:GetChildren() or {} do
		if display.Name:sub(1, 9) == "PulseSkin" then table.insert(result, display) end
	end
	return result
end

function SelfChams.visible(object)
	return not object:IsA("BasePart") or object.Transparency < 0.99 and object.LocalTransparencyModifier < 0.99
end

function SelfChams.collect(state, root, kind, style, clothing, wanted, boxes)
	local function add(object)
		if object:GetAttribute("PulseVFX") then return end
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then
			if object.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then return end
			if style.material and (state.saved[object] or object.Transparency < 0.99) then wanted[object] = kind end
			if style.wire and object.Transparency < 0.99 then boxes[object] = kind end
			return
		end
		if not style.clean then return end
		if object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("SpecialMesh") or clothing and (object:IsA("Clothing") or object:IsA("ShirtGraphic")) then wanted[object] = kind end
	end
	add(root)
	for _, object in root:GetDescendants() do add(object) end
end

function SelfChams.targets(state)
	local wanted, highlights, boxes = {}, {}, {}
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	if not character then return wanted, highlights, boxes end
	local own, tool = state.settings.self, state.settings.tool
	if own.on then
		local style, appearance = SELF_CHAM_STYLES[own.style], SelfChams.appearance(state)
		for _, child in character:GetChildren() do if not child:IsA("Tool") then SelfChams.collect(state, child, "self", style, true, wanted, boxes) end end
		if appearance then SelfChams.collect(state, appearance, "self", style, true, wanted, boxes) end
		if style.fill ~= nil or style.edge then
			highlights[appearance or character] = "self"
			if not appearance then for _, child in character:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Tool") then highlights[child] = "block" end end end
		end
	end
	if tool.on then
		local style = SELF_CHAM_STYLES[tool.style]
		local targets = SelfChams.displays(state, character)
		for _, child in character:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(targets, child) end end
		for _, target in targets do
			if not SelfChams.visible(target) then continue end
			SelfChams.collect(state, target, "tool", style, false, wanted, boxes)
			if style.fill ~= nil or style.edge then highlights[target] = "tool" end
		end
	end
	return wanted, highlights, boxes
end

function SelfChams.reconcile(state, wanted, highlights, boxes)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	for object, record in state.saved do
		local keep = wanted[object] == record.kind
		if not keep and record.clothing then
			local settings = state.settings[record.kind]
			keep = settings.on and SELF_CHAM_STYLES[settings.style].clean == true and record.parent and record.parent.Parent ~= nil
		end
		if not keep then state.saved[object] = nil; pcall(SelfChams.restore, state, object, record) end
	end
	for object, kind in wanted do
		if state.saved[object] then continue end
		local record = {kind = kind, original = {}, written = {}}
		if object:IsA("Clothing") or object:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
			record.clothing, record.parent = true, object.Parent
			pcall(function() object.Parent = nil end)
		else
			record.part, record.meshPart, record.decal, record.mesh = object:IsA("BasePart"), object:IsA("MeshPart"), object:IsA("Decal"), object:IsA("SpecialMesh")
		end
		state.saved[object] = record
	end
	for object, record in state.highlights do
		if highlights[object] ~= record.kind or not object.Parent then state.highlights[object] = nil; pcall(function() record.object:Destroy() end) end
	end
	for object, kind in highlights do
		if state.highlights[object] then continue end
		local highlight = environment.Instance.new("Highlight")
		highlight.Name, highlight.DepthMode, highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = "PulseChamsHL", environment.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded, 1, 1
		highlight.Adornee, highlight.Parent = object, SelfChams.folder(state)
		state.highlights[object] = {object = highlight, kind = kind}
	end
	for object, record in state.boxes do
		if boxes[object] ~= record.kind or not object.Parent then state.boxes[object] = nil; pcall(function() record.object:Destroy() end) end
	end
	for object, kind in boxes do
		if state.boxes[object] then continue end
		local box = environment.Instance.new("SelectionBox")
		box.Name, box.LineThickness, box.SurfaceTransparency, box.Transparency = "PulseChamsWire", 0.025, 1, 1
		box.Adornee, box.Parent = object, SelfChams.folder(state)
		state.boxes[object] = {object = box, kind = kind}
	end
end

function SelfChams.paint(state, full)
	local environment, character = state.context.environment, state.context.player.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local origin = root and root.Position.Y
	local appearance = SelfChams.appearance(state)
	local time = environment.os.clock() - state.started
	for object, record in state.saved do
		local settings = state.settings[record.kind]
		local style = SELF_CHAM_STYLES[settings.style]
		if record.part and object.Parent and style.material and (full or settings.anim ~= "None") then
			pcall(function()
				local color, animation = SelfChams.animated(state, settings, time, origin and object.Position.Y - origin or 0)
				SelfChams.write(state, object, record, "Material", environment.Enum.Material[style.material])
				SelfChams.write(state, object, record, "Color", color)
				SelfChams.write(state, object, record, "Reflectance", style.reflectance or 0)
				if full and style.clean and record.meshPart then SelfChams.write(state, object, record, "TextureID", "") end
				local transparency = style.transparency or 0
				if transparency > 0 or settings.alpha > 0 or animation > 0 or record.written.Transparency ~= nil then
					SelfChams.write(state, object, record, "Transparency", function(original)
						if original >= 0.99 then return nil end
						return SelfChams.transparency(original, transparency, settings, animation)
					end)
				end
			end)
			continue
		end
		if full and object.Parent and record.decal then pcall(SelfChams.write, state, object, record, "Transparency", 1) end
		if full and object.Parent and record.mesh then pcall(SelfChams.write, state, object, record, "TextureId", "") end
	end
	for object, record in state.highlights do
		pcall(function()
			local highlight = record.object
			if record.kind == "block" then highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency, highlight.Enabled = 1, 1, true; return end
			local settings, style = state.settings[record.kind], SELF_CHAM_STYLES[state.settings[record.kind].style]
			local color, animation = SelfChams.animated(state, settings, time, 0)
			local hidden = 0
			if object == character or object == appearance then
				local head = object:FindFirstChild("Head")
				if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then hidden = head.LocalTransparencyModifier end
			end
			highlight.FillColor = color
			highlight.OutlineColor = style.fill == 0 and color:Lerp(environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.4) or color
			highlight.FillTransparency = style.fill ~= nil and 1 - (1 - SelfChams.transparency(0, style.fill, settings, animation)) * (1 - hidden) or 1
			highlight.OutlineTransparency = style.edge and 1 - (1 - SelfChams.transparency(0, 0, settings, animation)) * (1 - hidden) or 1
			highlight.Enabled = hidden < 0.95 and SelfChams.visible(object)
		end)
	end
	for object, record in state.boxes do
		pcall(function()
			local settings = state.settings[record.kind]
			local color, animation = SelfChams.animated(state, settings, time, origin and object.Position.Y - origin or 0)
			local hidden = object.LocalTransparencyModifier
			record.object.Color3 = color
			record.object.Transparency = 1 - (1 - SelfChams.transparency(0, 0, settings, animation)) * (1 - hidden)
			record.object.Visible = hidden < 0.95 and object.Transparency < 0.99
		end)
	end
end

function SelfChams.refresh(state)
	local wanted, highlights, boxes = SelfChams.targets(state)
	SelfChams.reconcile(state, wanted, highlights, boxes)
	SelfChams.paint(state, true)
end

function SelfChams.clearKind(state, kind)
	for object, record in state.saved do
		if record.kind == kind then state.saved[object] = nil; pcall(SelfChams.restore, state, object, record) end
	end
	for object, record in state.highlights do
		if record.kind == kind or kind == "self" and record.kind == "block" then state.highlights[object] = nil; pcall(function() record.object:Destroy() end) end
	end
	for object, record in state.boxes do
		if record.kind == kind then state.boxes[object] = nil; pcall(function() record.object:Destroy() end) end
	end
end

function SelfChams.synchronize(state)
	local restored = SelfChams.settings(state.context)
	for _, kind in {"self", "tool"} do
		local current, values = state.settings[kind], restored[kind]
		for _, key in {"col", "col2", "style", "anim", "speed", "alpha", "saved"} do current[key] = values[key] end
		for key, handle in state.controls[kind] do pcall(handle.SetSilent, current[key]) end
		SelfChams.clearKind(state, kind)
	end
	pcall(SelfChams.refresh, state)
	local tint = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseVFXTint
	if tint then pcall(tint) end
end

function SelfChams.install(context)
	local environment, globals = context.environment, context.environment.getgenv()
	local state = {context = context, settings = SelfChams.settings(context), saved = {}, highlights = {}, boxes = {},
		controls = {self = {}, tool = {}}, started = environment.os.clock(), scanAt = 0, paintAt = 0}
	local function options(values)
		local result = {}
		for _, value in values do table.insert(result, {code = value, label = value}) end
		return result
	end
	local function tint()
		if globals.__pulseVFXTint then pcall(globals.__pulseVFXTint) end
	end
	local function refresh() pcall(SelfChams.refresh, state) end
	local function controls(kind)
		return function()
			local settings = state.settings[kind]
			state.controls[kind].col = context.section:Colorpicker({Text = "Chams Color", NoSave = true, Default = settings.col, Callback = function(color)
				if environment.typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
				settings.col = color; SelfChams.save(state, kind); pcall(SelfChams.paint, state, true); if kind == "self" then tint() end
			end})
			state.controls[kind].style = context.section:Dropdown({Text = "Chams Style", NoSave = true, Default = settings.style, Options = options(SELF_CHAM_ORDER), Callback = function(value)
				if not SELF_CHAM_STYLES[value] or value == settings.style then return end
				settings.style = value; SelfChams.save(state, kind); SelfChams.clearKind(state, kind); refresh()
			end})
			state.controls[kind].anim = context.section:Dropdown({Text = "Animation", NoSave = true, Default = settings.anim, Options = options(SELF_CHAM_ANIMATIONS), Callback = function(value)
				if not table.find(SELF_CHAM_ANIMATIONS, value) then return end
				settings.anim = value; SelfChams.save(state, kind); refresh()
			end})
			state.controls[kind].speed = context.section:Slider({Text = "Animation Speed", NoSave = true, Min = 10, Max = 300, Default = settings.speed, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value)
				settings.speed = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 100, 10, 300); SelfChams.save(state, kind)
			end})
			state.controls[kind].col2 = context.section:Colorpicker({Text = "Second Color", NoSave = true, Default = settings.col2, Callback = function(color)
				if environment.typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
				settings.col2 = color; SelfChams.save(state, kind); pcall(SelfChams.paint, state, true)
			end})
			state.controls[kind].alpha = context.section:Slider({Text = "Chams Transparency", NoSave = true, Min = 0, Max = 90, Default = settings.alpha, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value)
				settings.alpha = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, 90); SelfChams.save(state, kind); pcall(SelfChams.paint, state, true)
			end})
		end
	end
	for _, kind in {"self", "tool"} do
		context.section:Toggle({Text = kind == "self" and "Self Chams" or "Tool Chams", Default = false, Callback = function(value)
			state.settings[kind].on = not not value; refresh()
		end, Settings = controls(kind)})
	end
	context.section:Toggle({Text = "Tint Auras / VFX", Default = false, Callback = function(value) globals.__pulseFxTintOn = not not value; tint() end})
	if context.applyHooks then table.insert(context.applyHooks, function() SelfChams.synchronize(state) end) end
	local mine = function()
		local own, tool = state.settings.self, state.settings.tool
		if not own.on and not tool.on then return nil end
		local color, style = own.on and own.col or tool.col, own.on and own.style or tool.style
		local result = {s = own.on and 1 or nil, t = tool.on and 1 or nil, c = math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5) * 65536 + math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5) * 256 + math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)}
		if style ~= "ForceField" and SELF_CHAM_STYLES[style].material then result.y = style end
		return result
	end
	local material = function(style)
		local spec = SELF_CHAM_STYLES[tostring(style)]
		return spec and spec.material and environment.Enum.Material[spec.material] or nil
	end
	local color = function() return state.settings.self.col end
	globals.__pulseChamsMine, globals.__pulseChamsMat, globals.__pulseSelfChams, globals.__pulseFxColor = mine, material, state, color
	state.K, state.hls, state.apply = state.settings, state.highlights, refresh
	context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function()
		local now = environment.os.clock()
		if now - state.scanAt >= 0.4 and (state.settings.self.on or state.settings.tool.on or next(state.saved) or next(state.highlights) or next(state.boxes)) then
			state.scanAt = now; refresh()
		end
		if now - state.paintAt < 1 / 30 then return end
		if state.settings.self.anim == "None" and state.settings.tool.anim == "None" and not next(state.highlights) and not next(state.boxes) then return end
		state.paintAt = now
		pcall(SelfChams.paint, state, false)
	end)
	return state, function()
		state.settings.self.on, state.settings.tool.on = false, false
		SelfChams.clearKind(state, "self"); SelfChams.clearKind(state, "tool")
		if state.folder then pcall(function() state.folder:Destroy() end) end
		if globals.__pulseChamsMine == mine then globals.__pulseChamsMine = nil end
		if globals.__pulseChamsMat == material then globals.__pulseChamsMat = nil end
		if globals.__pulseSelfChams == state then globals.__pulseSelfChams = nil end
		if globals.__pulseFxColor == color then globals.__pulseFxColor = nil end
	end
end
