local WALK_STYLE_ORDER = {"Aurora", "Comet", "Inferno", "Frost", "Rainbow", "Neon", "Shadow", "Sakura", "Galaxy", "Electric"}
local WalkTrail = {}

function WalkTrail.settings(configuration)
	configuration.trail = type(configuration.trail) == "table" and configuration.trail or {}
	local settings = configuration.trail
	local migrations = {Matrix = "Aurora", Fire = "Inferno", Ghost = "Frost"}
	settings.mode = migrations[settings.mode] or settings.mode
	if not WALK_STYLES[settings.mode] then settings.mode = "Aurora" end
	local defaults = {on = false, life = 3, scale = 100, alpha = 100, custom = false, r = 0, g = 200, b = 255}
	for key, value in defaults do
		if settings[key] == nil or type(value) == "number" and tonumber(settings[key]) == nil then settings[key] = value end
	end
	return settings
end

function WalkTrail.numberSequence(environment, values)
	local points = {}
	for _, point in values do table.insert(points, environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], point[2], point[3] or 0)) end
	return environment.NumberSequence.new(points)
end

function WalkTrail.colors(environment, values, tint)
	local points = {}
	for index, rgb in values do
		local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3])
		if tint then
			local high, low = math.max(color.R, color.G, color.B), math.min(color.R, color.G, color.B)
			local brightness = 0.3 + 0.7 * high
			color = environment.Color3.new(tint.R * brightness, tint.G * brightness, tint.B * brightness)
			if low > 0.72 then color = color:Lerp(environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), (low - 0.72) / 0.28 * 0.75) end
		end
		local position = #values == 1 and 0 or (index - 1) / (#values - 1)
		table.insert(points, environment.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(position, color))
	end
	if #points == 1 then return environment.ColorSequence.new(points[1].Value) end
	return environment.ColorSequence.new(points)
end

function WalkTrail.clear(state)
	for _, object in state.objects do pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
	if state.folder then pcall(function() state.folder:Destroy() end) end
	state.objects, state.emitters, state.stamps = {}, {}, {}
	state.folder, state.character, state.signature, state.lastPosition, state.moving, state.stamp = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
end

function WalkTrail.createObject(state, class)
	local object = state.context.environment.Instance.new(class)
	table.insert(state.objects, object)
	return object
end

function WalkTrail.particle(state, parent, spec, scale, opacity, life, tint, sizeScale)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local particle = WalkTrail.createObject(state, "ParticleEmitter")
	particle.Texture, particle.Color = spec.tex, WalkTrail.colors(environment, spec.color, tint)
	particle.LightEmission, particle.LightInfluence = spec.le or 1, spec.li or 0
	particle.Size = WalkTrail.numberSequence(environment, {{0, spec.size[1] * scale * sizeScale}, {1, spec.size[2] * scale * sizeScale}})
	local transparency = 1 - (1 - (spec.tr or 0)) * opacity
	particle.Transparency = WalkTrail.numberSequence(environment, {{0, spec.flat and 1 or transparency}, {0.15, transparency}, {1, 1}})
	if type(spec.life) == "table" then
		particle.Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(spec.life[1] * life, spec.life[2] * life)
	else
		particle.Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(spec.life * life)
	end
	local speed = spec.speed or {0.001, 0.001}
	particle.Rate, particle.Speed = 0, environment.NumberRange.new(speed[1] * scale, speed[2] * scale)
	particle.SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(spec.spread or 0, spec.spread or 0)
	local acceleration = spec.acc and environment.Vector3.new(table.unpack(spec.acc)) or environment.Vector3.zero
	particle.Acceleration, particle.Drag = acceleration * scale, spec.drag or 0
	particle.Rotation, particle.RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360), environment.NumberRange.new(-(spec.rot or 0), spec.rot or 0)
	if spec.upright then particle.Orientation, particle.Rotation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCameraWorldUp, environment.NumberRange.new(-8, 8) end
	if spec.par then particle.Orientation, particle.Rotation = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel, environment.NumberRange.new(0) end
	pcall(function() particle.Brightness = 3 end)
	particle.Parent = parent
	return particle
end

function WalkTrail.ribbon(state, foot, left, right, color, transparency, width, lifetime, brightness)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local trail = WalkTrail.createObject(state, "Trail")
	trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1, trail.Color = left, right, color
	trail.Transparency, trail.WidthScale = WalkTrail.numberSequence(environment, transparency), WalkTrail.numberSequence(environment, width)
	trail.Lifetime, trail.LightEmission, trail.LightInfluence, trail.FaceCamera, trail.MinLength = lifetime, 1, 0, false, 0.05
	pcall(function() trail.Brightness = brightness end)
	trail.Parent = foot
end

function WalkTrail.build(state, character)
	WalkTrail.clear(state)
	local context, environment, settings = state.context, state.context.environment, state.settings
	local spec = WALK_STYLES[settings.mode] or WALK_STYLES.Aurora
	local scale = math.clamp((tonumber(settings.scale) or 100) / 100, 0.3, 3)
	local opacity = math.clamp((tonumber(settings.alpha) or 100) / 100, 0, 1)
	local life = math.clamp((tonumber(settings.life) or 3) / 3, 0.4, 3)
	local tint = settings.custom and environment.Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(tonumber(settings.r) or 0, 0, 255), math.clamp(tonumber(settings.g) or 200, 0, 255), math.clamp(tonumber(settings.b) or 255, 0, 255)) or nil
	local color = WalkTrail.colors(environment, spec.rib, tint)
	for _, side in {"Left", "Right"} do
		local foot = character:FindFirstChild(side .. "Foot") or character:FindFirstChild(side .. " Leg")
		if not foot then continue end
		local height, width = -foot.Size.Y / 2 + 0.08, spec.width * scale
		local attachments = {}
		for _, horizontal in {-width / 2, width / 2, -width * (spec.glow or 2.6) / 2, width * (spec.glow or 2.6) / 2, 0} do
			local attachment = WalkTrail.createObject(state, "Attachment")
			attachment.Position, attachment.Parent = environment.Vector3.new(horizontal, height + (#attachments == 4 and 0.15 or 0), 0), foot
			table.insert(attachments, attachment)
		end
		local duration = spec.ribLife * life
		WalkTrail.ribbon(state, foot, attachments[1], attachments[2], color, {{0, 1 - 0.95 * opacity}, {0.5, 1 - 0.55 * opacity}, {1, 1}}, {{0, 1}, {1, 0.2}}, duration, 3)
		WalkTrail.ribbon(state, foot, attachments[3], attachments[4], color, {{0, 1 - 0.3 * opacity}, {0.6, 1 - 0.12 * opacity}, {1, 1}}, {{0, 1}, {1, 0.3}}, duration * 0.8, 2)
		for _, particleSpec in spec.foot or {} do
			local particle = WalkTrail.particle(state, attachments[5], particleSpec, scale, opacity, life, tint, 1)
			table.insert(state.emitters, {object = particle, rate = particleSpec.rate or 10})
		end
	end
	local folder = environment.Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name, folder.Parent, state.folder = "PulseTrail", context.workspace, folder
	local stamp = WalkTrail.createObject(state, "Part")
	stamp.Name, stamp.Size, stamp.Transparency, stamp.Anchored = "PulseTrailStamp", environment.Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2), 1, true
	stamp.CanCollide, stamp.CanQuery, stamp.CanTouch, stamp.CastShadow = false, false, false, false
	stamp.Parent = folder
	local attachment = WalkTrail.createObject(state, "Attachment")
	attachment.Parent = stamp
	for _, particleSpec in spec.step or {} do
		local particle = WalkTrail.particle(state, attachment, particleSpec, scale, opacity, life, tint, 0.5)
		particle.Orientation, particle.Speed = environment.Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular, environment.NumberRange.new(0.001)
		particle.SpreadAngle, particle.Acceleration = environment.Vector2.new(0, 0), environment.Vector3.zero
		table.insert(state.stamps, {object = particle, amount = particleSpec.n or 1})
	end
	state.stamp = stamp
end

function WalkTrail.signature(settings)
	return table.concat({settings.mode, tostring(settings.scale), tostring(settings.alpha), tostring(settings.life), tostring(settings.custom), tostring(settings.r), tostring(settings.g), tostring(settings.b)}, "|")
end

function WalkTrail.step(state)
	local context, environment, settings = state.context, state.context.environment, state.settings
	if not settings.on then if state.character or state.folder then WalkTrail.clear(state) end; return end
	local character = context.player.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
	local signature = WalkTrail.signature(settings)
	if state.character ~= character or state.signature ~= signature or not state.stamp or not state.stamp.Parent then
		local ok, raised = pcall(WalkTrail.build, state, character)
		if not ok then environment.warn("[Pulse] trail: " .. tostring(raised)) end
		state.character, state.signature = character, signature
	end
	local grounded, velocity = humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= environment.Enum.Material.Air, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local moving = grounded and velocity.X * velocity.X + velocity.Z * velocity.Z > 4
	if moving ~= state.moving then
		state.moving = moving
		for _, emitter in state.emitters do pcall(function() emitter.object.Rate = moving and emitter.rate or 0 end) end
	end
	if not grounded then state.lastPosition = nil; return end
	if not moving then return end
	local position = root.Position
	if state.lastPosition and (position - state.lastPosition).Magnitude < 3 then return end
	state.lastPosition = position
	local hit
	pcall(function()
		state.raycast.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character, state.folder}
		hit = context.workspace:Raycast(position, environment.Vector3.new(0, -7, 0), state.raycast)
	end)
	if not hit or not state.stamp then return end
	state.stamp.CFrame = environment.CFrame.new(hit.Position + hit.Normal * 0.06)
	for _, effect in state.stamps do pcall(function() effect.object:Emit(effect.amount) end) end
end

function WalkTrail.install(context)
	local environment = context.environment
	local settings = WalkTrail.settings(context.configuration)
	local colorPicker
	local raycast = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	raycast.FilterType, raycast.IgnoreWater = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
	local state = {context = context, settings = settings, raycast = raycast, objects = {}, emitters = {}, stamps = {}}
	context.section:Header("Trail")
	context.section:Toggle({Text = "Walk Trail", Default = settings.on, Callback = function(value)
		settings.on = not not value
		if not settings.on then WalkTrail.clear(state) end
	end, Settings = function()
		local options = {}
		for _, mode in WALK_STYLE_ORDER do table.insert(options, {code = mode, label = mode}) end
		context.section:Dropdown({Text = "Trail Style", Default = settings.mode, Options = options, Callback = function(value) if WALK_STYLES[value] then settings.mode = value end end})
		context.section:Slider({Text = "Trail Fade (s)", Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = settings.life, Decimals = 0, Callback = function(value) settings.life = value end})
		context.section:Slider({Text = "Trail Size", Min = 30, Max = 300, Default = settings.scale, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value) settings.scale = value end})
		context.section:Slider({Text = "Trail Opacity", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = settings.alpha, Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value) settings.alpha = value end})
		context.section:Toggle({Text = "Trail Custom Color", Default = settings.custom, Callback = function(value) settings.custom = not not value end, Settings = function()
			colorPicker = context.section:Colorpicker({Text = "Trail Color", NoSave = true, Default = environment.Color3.fromRGB(settings.r, settings.g, settings.b), Callback = function(value)
				if environment.typeof(value) ~= "Color3" then return end
				settings.r, settings.g, settings.b = math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5)
			end})
		end})
	end})
	if context.applyHooks then table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		if colorPicker then colorPicker.SetSilent(environment.Color3.fromRGB(settings.r or 255, settings.g or 255, settings.b or 255)) end
	end) end
	context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function() WalkTrail.step(state) end)
	return state, function() WalkTrail.clear(state) end
end

local Optimizer = {}
local OPTIMIZER_EFFECTS = {ParticleEmitter = true, Beam = true, Trail = true, Fire = true, Smoke = true, Sparkles = true}
local OPTIMIZER_PROPERTIES = {shadow = "CastShadow", lshadow = "Shadows", light = "Enabled", fx = "Enabled",
	wrap = "Enabled", mtex = "TextureID", acc = "LocalTransparencyModifier"}

function Optimizer.install(context)
	local settings = Optimizer.initialize(context.configuration)
	local state = {
		context = context,
		settings = settings,
		quality = context.environment.UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings"),
		saved = {},
		global = {},
	}

	for _, name in {"shadow", "lshadow", "light", "fx", "tex", "mtex", "plastic", "acc", "wrap", "post"} do
		state.saved[name] = {}
	end

	Optimizer.installControls(state)
	Optimizer.connect(state)
	if context.applyHooks then table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		for name in state.saved do Optimizer.restore(state, name) end
		Optimizer.applyGlobals(state)
		Optimizer.sweep(state)
	end) end
	context.environment.task.defer(function()
		Optimizer.applyGlobals(state)
		Optimizer.sweep(state)
	end)

	return function() Optimizer.stop(state) end
end

function Optimizer.initialize(configuration)
	local settings = configuration.optim or {}
	configuration.optim = settings
	settings.fpsunlock = settings.fpsunlock or false

	if settings.darken == nil then settings.darken = 45 end
	if settings.on == true and settings.migrated2 == nil then
		settings.gfxLow = settings.lowquality ~= false
		settings.noShadows = settings.shadows ~= false
		settings.noFx = settings.particles ~= false
		settings.noTex = settings.textures ~= false
		settings.stopAnims = settings.anims ~= false
		settings.plastic = settings.materials == true
		settings.antiGlare = true
	end

	settings.migrated2, settings.on = true, false
	for _, key in {"gfxLow", "noShadows", "noLights", "noPost", "noFx", "noTex", "plastic", "simplePlayers", "stopAnims", "bgSaver", "antiGlare"} do
		if settings[key] == nil then settings[key] = false end
	end

	return settings
end

function Optimizer.save(state, name, object, value)
	if state.saved[name][object] == nil then state.saved[name][object] = value end
end

function Optimizer.restore(state, name)
	local objects = state.saved[name]
	state.saved[name] = {}

	for object, value in objects do
		pcall(function()
			if not object.Parent then return end
			if OPTIMIZER_PROPERTIES[name] then
				object[OPTIMIZER_PROPERTIES[name]] = value
				return
			end
			if name == "post" then
				local property = object:IsA("Atmosphere") and "Density" or "Enabled"
				object[property] = value
				return
			end
			if name == "tex" then
				local property = object.ClassName == "SpecialMesh" and "TextureId" or "Transparency"
				object[property] = value
				return
			end
			if name == "plastic" then object.Material, object.Reflectance = value[1], value[2] end
		end)
	end
end

function Optimizer.skip(state, object)
	local context = state.context
	local workspace, lighting = context.workspace, context.lighting
	if workspace.CurrentCamera and object:IsDescendantOf(workspace.CurrentCamera) then return true end
	if context.player.Character and object:IsDescendantOf(context.player.Character) then return true end

	local ancestor = object
	while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace and ancestor ~= lighting do
		if ancestor.Name:sub(1, 5) == "Pulse" then return true end
		ancestor = ancestor.Parent
	end

	return false
end

function Optimizer.otherCharacter(state, object)
	local context = state.context
	local model = object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

	while model do
		if model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
			local player = context.players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
			return player ~= nil and player ~= context.player
		end
		model = model.Parent and model.Parent:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	end

	return false
end

function Optimizer.touchPart(state, object)
	local settings, enum = state.settings, state.context.environment.Enum
	if settings.noShadows and object.CastShadow then
		Optimizer.save(state, "shadow", object, true)
		object.CastShadow = false
	end
	if settings.plastic and (object.Material ~= enum.Material.SmoothPlastic or object.Reflectance ~= 0)
		and object.Material ~= enum.Material.ForceField and object.Material ~= enum.Material.Neon then
		Optimizer.save(state, "plastic", object, {object.Material, object.Reflectance})
		object.Material, object.Reflectance = enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, 0
	end
	if settings.noTex and object.ClassName == "MeshPart" and object.TextureID ~= "" then
		Optimizer.save(state, "mtex", object, object.TextureID)
		object.TextureID = ""
	end
	if settings.simplePlayers and object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") and Optimizer.otherCharacter(state, object) then
		Optimizer.save(state, "acc", object, object.LocalTransparencyModifier)
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
	end
end

function Optimizer.touch(state, object)
	local settings, class = state.settings, object.ClassName
	local isPart, isLight = object:IsA("BasePart"), object:IsA("Light")
	if not (isPart or isLight or OPTIMIZER_EFFECTS[class] or class == "Decal" or class == "Texture" or class == "SpecialMesh" or class == "WrapLayer") then return end
	if Optimizer.skip(state, object) then return end

	pcall(function()
		if isPart then
			Optimizer.touchPart(state, object)
			return
		end
		if isLight then
			if settings.noLights and object.Enabled then
				Optimizer.save(state, "light", object, true)
				object.Enabled = false
			end
			if settings.noShadows and object.Shadows then
				Optimizer.save(state, "lshadow", object, true)
				object.Shadows = false
			end
			return
		end
		if OPTIMIZER_EFFECTS[class] then
			if settings.noFx and object.Enabled then
				Optimizer.save(state, "fx", object, true)
				object.Enabled = false
			end
			return
		end
		if class == "WrapLayer" then
			if settings.simplePlayers and object.Enabled and Optimizer.otherCharacter(state, object) then
				Optimizer.save(state, "wrap", object, true)
				object.Enabled = false
			end
			return
		end
		if not settings.noTex then return end
		if class == "SpecialMesh" and object.TextureId ~= "" then
			Optimizer.save(state, "tex", object, object.TextureId)
			object.TextureId = ""
		end
		if class ~= "SpecialMesh" and object.Transparency < 1 then
			Optimizer.save(state, "tex", object, object.Transparency)
			object.Transparency = 1
		end
	end)
end

function Optimizer.sweep(state)
	local settings, context = state.settings, state.context
	if not (settings.noShadows or settings.noLights or settings.noFx or settings.noTex or settings.plastic or settings.simplePlayers) then return end

	context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		for index, object in context.workspace:GetDescendants() do
			Optimizer.touch(state, object)
			if index % 600 == 0 then context.environment.task.wait() end
		end
	end)
end

function Optimizer.glare(state)
	local environment, lighting = state.context.environment, state.context.lighting
	local settings, global = state.settings, state.global
	if environment.getgenv().__pulseVisOwns == true then return end

	pcall(function()
		if not settings.antiGlare then
			if global.light then
				lighting.Brightness, lighting.Ambient, lighting.OutdoorAmbient = global.light[1], global.light[2], global.light[3]
				global.light = nil
			end
			return
		end

		global.light = global.light or {lighting.Brightness, lighting.Ambient, lighting.OutdoorAmbient}
		local darken = math.clamp((tonumber(settings.darken) or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
		local black = environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		lighting.Brightness = global.light[1] * (1 - darken * 0.8)
		lighting.Ambient = global.light[2]:Lerp(black, darken)
		lighting.OutdoorAmbient = global.light[3]:Lerp(black, darken)
	end)
end

function Optimizer.applyGlobals(state)
	local context, settings, global = state.context, state.settings, state.global
	local quality, lighting = state.quality, context.lighting

	pcall(function()
		if settings.gfxLow then
			if global.quality == nil then global.quality = quality.SavedQualityLevel end
			quality.SavedQualityLevel = context.environment.Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1
			return
		end
		if global.quality ~= nil then quality.SavedQualityLevel, global.quality = global.quality, nil end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		if settings.noShadows then
			if global.shadows == nil then global.shadows = lighting.GlobalShadows end
			lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			return
		end
		if global.shadows ~= nil then lighting.GlobalShadows, global.shadows = global.shadows, nil end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		if not settings.noPost then Optimizer.restore(state, "post"); return end
		for _, object in lighting:GetChildren() do
			if object.Name:sub(1, 5) == "Pulse" then continue end
			if object:IsA("PostEffect") and object.Enabled then
				Optimizer.save(state, "post", object, true)
				object.Enabled = false
			end
			if object:IsA("Atmosphere") and object.Density > 0 then
				Optimizer.save(state, "post", object, object.Density)
				object.Density = 0
			end
		end
	end)
	Optimizer.glare(state)
end

function Optimizer.setFps(state, value)
	local setfpscap = state.context.environment.setfpscap
	if type(setfpscap) == "function" then pcall(setfpscap, value) end
end

function Optimizer.render3d(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	local environment = context.environment
	pcall(function() context.runService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(not settings.no3d) end)
	if type(environment.setfpscap) ~= "function" then return end

	pcall(function()
		if settings.no3d then
			if state.renderingCap == nil and type(environment.getfpscap) == "function" then state.renderingCap = environment.getfpscap() end
			Optimizer.setFps(state, math.clamp(tonumber(settings.no3dFps) or 15, 5, 60))
			return
		end
		Optimizer.setFps(state, settings.fpsunlock and 9999 or state.renderingCap or 240)
		state.renderingCap = nil
	end)
end

function Optimizer.installControls(state)
	local section, settings = state.context.section, state.settings
	section:Header("Optimizer")

	local function toggle(text, key, categories, globals)
		section:Toggle({Text = text, Default = settings[key] == true, Callback = function(enabled)
			local previous = settings[key] == true
			settings[key] = not not enabled
			if globals then Optimizer.applyGlobals(state) end
			if categories and enabled and not previous then Optimizer.sweep(state) end
			if categories and not enabled and previous then
				for _, category in categories do Optimizer.restore(state, category) end
			end
		end})
	end

	toggle("No Shadows", "noShadows", {"shadow", "lshadow"}, true)
	toggle("No Map Lights", "noLights", {"light"})
	toggle("No Post Effects", "noPost", nil, true)
	toggle("No Particles", "noFx", {"fx"})
	toggle("No Textures", "noTex", {"tex", "mtex"})
	toggle("Plastic Materials", "plastic", {"plastic"})
	section:Toggle({Text = "Anti-Glare", Default = settings.antiGlare == true, Callback = function(enabled)
		settings.antiGlare = not not enabled
		Optimizer.glare(state)
	end, Settings = function()
		section:Slider({Text = "Darken", Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = settings.darken, Decimals = 0, Callback = function(value)
			settings.darken = value
			Optimizer.glare(state)
		end})
	end})

	section:Header("Performance")
	section:Toggle({Text = "FPS Unlocker (9999)", Default = settings.fpsunlock, Callback = function(enabled)
		settings.fpsunlock = not not enabled
		if enabled then Optimizer.setFps(state, 9999) end
	end})
	toggle("Low Graphics", "gfxLow", nil, true)
	toggle("Simple Players", "simplePlayers", {"acc", "wrap"})
	toggle("Stop Animations (others)", "stopAnims")
	toggle("Background Saver", "bgSaver")
	section:Toggle({Text = "Disable 3D Rendering", Default = settings.no3d == true, Callback = function(enabled)
		local previous = settings.no3d == true
		settings.no3d = not not enabled
		if enabled or previous then Optimizer.render3d(state) end
	end, Settings = function()
		section:Slider({Text = "FPS Cap", Min = 5, Max = 60, Default = math.clamp(tonumber(settings.no3dFps) or 15, 5, 60), Decimals = 0, Callback = function(value)
			settings.no3dFps = value
			if settings.no3d then Optimizer.setFps(state, value) end
		end})
	end})
end

function Optimizer.connect(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	local environment, quality = context.environment, state.quality
	context.connect(context.workspace.DescendantAdded, function(object)
		if settings.noShadows or settings.noLights or settings.noFx or settings.noTex or settings.plastic or settings.simplePlayers then
			environment.task.defer(Optimizer.touch, state, object)
		end
	end)
	context.connect(context.lighting.ChildAdded, function()
		if settings.noPost then environment.task.defer(Optimizer.applyGlobals, state) end
	end)
	context.connect(quality:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SavedQualityLevel"), function()
		if settings.gfxLow and quality.SavedQualityLevel ~= environment.Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1 then
			state.global.quality = quality.SavedQualityLevel
			environment.task.defer(function()
				if settings.gfxLow then pcall(function() quality.SavedQualityLevel = environment.Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1 end) end
			end)
		end
	end)
	context.connect(context.inputService.WindowFocusReleased, function() Optimizer.focusReleased(state) end)
	context.connect(context.inputService.WindowFocused, function() Optimizer.focused(state) end)
	environment.task.spawn(function() Optimizer.stopAnimations(state) end)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		while context.isAlive() do
			environment.task.wait(2)
			if settings.fpsunlock and not state.backgroundOff then Optimizer.setFps(state, 9999) end
		end
	end)
end

function Optimizer.focusReleased(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	if not settings.bgSaver or state.backgroundOff then return end

	state.backgroundOff = true
	pcall(function()
		local getfpscap = context.environment.getfpscap
		state.backgroundCap = type(getfpscap) == "function" and getfpscap() or (settings.fpsunlock and 9999 or 240)
	end)
	pcall(function() context.runService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(false) end)
	Optimizer.setFps(state, 10)
end

function Optimizer.focused(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	if not state.backgroundOff then return end

	state.backgroundOff = false
	pcall(function() context.runService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(not settings.no3d) end)
	local fps = settings.fpsunlock and 9999 or state.backgroundCap or 240
	if settings.no3d then fps = math.clamp(tonumber(settings.no3dFps) or 15, 5, 60) end
	Optimizer.setFps(state, fps)
end

function Optimizer.stopAnimations(state)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.settings
	while context.isAlive() do
		context.environment.task.wait(0.3)
		if not settings.stopAnims then continue end
		for _, player in context.players:GetPlayers() do
			local humanoid = player ~= context.player and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
			if not animator then continue end
			for _, track in animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end) end
		end
	end
end

function Optimizer.stop(state)
	local context, settings, global = state.context, state.settings, state.global
	for name in state.saved do Optimizer.restore(state, name) end
	pcall(function() if global.quality ~= nil then state.quality.SavedQualityLevel = global.quality end end)
	pcall(function() if global.shadows ~= nil then context.lighting.GlobalShadows = global.shadows end end)
	pcall(function()
		if global.light then context.lighting.Brightness, context.lighting.Ambient, context.lighting.OutdoorAmbient = global.light[1], global.light[2], global.light[3] end
	end)
	if state.backgroundOff then
		pcall(function() context.runService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(true) end)
		if state.backgroundCap then Optimizer.setFps(state, state.backgroundCap) end
	end
	if settings.no3d then
		pcall(function() context.runService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(true) end)
		Optimizer.setFps(state, settings.fpsunlock and 9999 or state.renderingCap or 240)
	end
end

local CustomCursor = {}
local CURSOR_SHAPES = {{"Dot", "Dot"}, {"Ring", "Ring"}, {"Crosshair", "Cross"}, {"Gap", "Gap Cross"}, {"GapDot", "Gap Dot"},
	{"RingDot", "Ring Dot"}, {"Diag", "Diagonal"}, {"Brackets", "Brackets"}, {"Tri", "Tri"}, {"Diamond", "Diamond"},
	{"Square", "Square"}, {"Heart", "Heart"}, {"PP", "PP"}}
local CURSOR_ASSETS = {11719595104, 11810229301, 12233185690, 12275825245, 6239163275, 11830989304, 11747999946, 11723817205,
	11988231127, 8390115370, 5143056985, 3259050989, 51838212, 8360620314, 11867337948, 11915656516, 11867351018, 12232793816,
	11747026230, 10696045527, 7051824015, 11718192673, 11121461899, 11756692076, 11756696859}
local CURSOR_THUMBS = {316279305, 128575217347464, 12275825310, 749187669, 2106032409, 10490937536, 115013535365912, 74659778634567,
	3612989007, 12614416526, 16599119961, 18415342958, 942455758, 11716577756, 11720549778, 13414078000, 11724945237, 1458996342,
	15956684491, 17602789776, 17128019382, 11754490336, 101154782718053, 130901098960945, 87104527, 437291573, 8050581238,
	9988796437, 102603903772193, 129587060006634, 12170156046, 12679500589, 446710860, 135488882163722, 11756694103, 966597671,
	10831379367, 11770890261, 13206317085, 129602324246246, 358948794}

function CustomCursor.images()
	local result = {}
	for _, id in ipairs(CURSOR_ASSETS) do table.insert(result, "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)) end
	for _, id in ipairs(CURSOR_THUMBS) do table.insert(result, "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. tostring(id) .. "&w=150&h=150") end
	return result
end

function CustomCursor.initialize(settings, env)
	local defaults = {cursor = "Off", cursorSize = 100, cursorFade = 0, cursorOutline = true, cursorTint = false,
		cursorRainbow = false, cursorRainbowSpd = 3, cursorPulse = false, cursorPulseSpd = 3, cursorPulseAmt = 20,
		cursorSpin = false, cursorSpinSpd = 180, cursorSpinRev = false, cursorClick = false, cursorClickAmt = 30}
	for key, value in pairs(defaults) do if settings[key] == nil then settings[key] = value end end
	if not tonumber(settings.cursorR) then
		local color = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		if settings.cursor ~= "Off" and tonumber(settings.cursorHue) then
			local brightness = tonumber(settings.cursorBright) or 100
			color = env.Color3.fromHSV(tonumber(settings.cursorHue) % 360 / 360, brightness <= 2 and 0 or 0.85, brightness / 100)
		end
		settings.cursorR, settings.cursorG, settings.cursorB = math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)
	end
end

function CustomCursor.valid(state, name)
	if name == "Off" then return true end
	for _, shape in ipairs(CURSOR_SHAPES) do if shape[1] == name then return true end end
	if type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
	local index = tonumber(name:match("^I(%d+)$") or "")
	return index ~= nil and state.images[index] ~= nil
end

function CustomCursor.color(state, now)
	local env, settings = state.context.environment, state.settings
	if settings.cursorRainbow then return env.Color3.fromHSV(now * (tonumber(settings.cursorRainbowSpd) or 3) * 0.06 % 1, 0.8, 1) end
	return env.Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(settings.cursorR) or 255, tonumber(settings.cursorG) or 255, tonumber(settings.cursorB) or 255)
end

function CustomCursor.scale(state, now, clicked, base)
	local settings, value = state.settings, base
	if settings.cursorPulse then
		local speed = (tonumber(settings.cursorPulseSpd) or 3) * 0.35
		value *= 1 + math.clamp((tonumber(settings.cursorPulseAmt) or 20) / 100, 0, 1) * math.sin(now * speed * math.pi * 2)
	end
	local elapsed = now - clicked
	if settings.cursorClick and elapsed >= 0 and elapsed < 0.24 then
		value *= 1 - math.clamp((tonumber(settings.cursorClickAmt) or 30) / 100, 0, 0.9) * math.sin(elapsed / 0.24 * math.pi)
	end
	return math.max(value, 0.05)
end

function CustomCursor.element(state, parent, x, y, width, height, rotation, rounded, zIndex)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local frame = env.Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.BorderSizePixel, frame.AnchorPoint = 0, env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	frame.Position, frame.Size = env.UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, y), env.UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
	frame.Rotation, frame.ZIndex = rotation or 0, zIndex or 3
	if rounded then
		local corner = env.Instance.new("UICorner")
		corner.CornerRadius, corner.Parent = env.UDim.new(rounded, 0), frame
	end
	frame.Parent = parent
	return frame
end

function CustomCursor.draw(state, parent, shape)
	local env = state.context.environment
	for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do if not child:IsA("UIScale") then child:Destroy() end end
	local parts = {}
	local function fill(x, y, width, height, rotation, rounded, noOutline)
		local outline
		if not noOutline then
			outline = CustomCursor.element(state, parent, x, y, width + 2, height + 2, rotation, rounded, 2)
			outline.BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		end
		local frame = CustomCursor.element(state, parent, x, y, width, height, rotation, rounded, 3)
		table.insert(parts, {kind = "fill", frame = frame, outline = outline})
	end
	local function ring(x, y, size, thickness, rotation, square)
		local rounded = not square and 0.5 or nil
		local outline = CustomCursor.element(state, parent, x, y, size - 2, size - 2, rotation, rounded, 2)
		outline.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		local outerStroke = env.Instance.new("UIStroke")
		outerStroke.Color, outerStroke.Thickness, outerStroke.Parent = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), thickness + 2, outline
		local frame = CustomCursor.element(state, parent, x, y, size, size, rotation, rounded, 3)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		local stroke = env.Instance.new("UIStroke")
		stroke.Thickness, stroke.Parent = thickness, frame
		table.insert(parts, {kind = "ring", frame = frame, stroke = stroke, outline = outline, outerStroke = outerStroke})
	end
	if shape == "Dot" then fill(0, 0, 6, 6, 0, 0.5) end
	if shape == "Ring" then ring(0, 0, 14, 2) end
	if shape == "Crosshair" then fill(0, 0, 18, 2); fill(0, 0, 2, 18) end
	if shape == "Gap" or shape == "GapDot" then
		fill(7, 0, 8, 2); fill(-7, 0, 8, 2); fill(0, 7, 2, 8); fill(0, -7, 2, 8)
		if shape == "GapDot" then fill(0, 0, 2, 2) end
	end
	if shape == "RingDot" then ring(0, 0, 16, 2); fill(0, 0, 4, 4, 0, 0.5) end
	if shape == "Diag" then fill(5, 5, 8, 2, 45); fill(-5, -5, 8, 2, 45); fill(5, -5, 8, 2, -45); fill(-5, 5, 8, 2, -45) end
	if shape == "Brackets" then
		for _, x in ipairs({-1, 1}) do for _, y in ipairs({-1, 1}) do fill(x * 7, y * 9, 6, 2); fill(x * 9, y * 7, 2, 6) end end
		fill(0, 0, 2, 2)
	end
	if shape == "Tri" then fill(0, -8, 8, 2, 90); fill(7, 4, 8, 2, 30); fill(-7, 4, 8, 2, 150) end
	if shape == "Diamond" then ring(0, 0, 12, 2, 45, true) end
	if shape == "Square" then ring(0, 0, 14, 2, 0, true); fill(0, 0, 2, 2) end
	if shape == "Heart" then fill(-4, -3, 10, 10, 0, 0.5, true); fill(4, -3, 10, 10, 0, 0.5, true); fill(0, 2, 12, 12, 45, nil, true) end
	if shape == "PP" then
		fill(0, -2, 8, 22, 0, 1, true); fill(0, -14, 10, 10, 0, 1, true)
		fill(-5, 10, 8, 8, 0, 1, true); fill(5, 10, 8, 8, 0, 1, true)
	end
	local index = type(shape) == "string" and tonumber(shape:match("^I(%d+)$") or "") or nil
	if index and state.images[index] then
		local image = env.Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		image.BackgroundTransparency, image.AnchorPoint, image.Position = 1, env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
		image.Size, image.ScaleType, image.ZIndex, image.Image = env.UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32), env.Enum.ScaleType.Fit, 3, state.images[index]
		image.Parent = parent
		table.insert(parts, {kind = "image", frame = image})
	end
	return parts
end

function CustomCursor.paint(state, parts, color, preview)
	local settings, env = state.settings, state.context.environment
	local fade = preview and 0 or math.clamp((tonumber(settings.cursorFade) or 0) / 100, 0, 0.95)
	local showOutline = preview or settings.cursorOutline ~= false
	local outlineFade = 1 - (1 - fade) * 0.6
	for _, part in ipairs(parts or {}) do
		if part.kind == "fill" then
			part.frame.BackgroundColor3, part.frame.BackgroundTransparency = color, fade
			if part.outline then part.outline.Visible, part.outline.BackgroundTransparency = showOutline, outlineFade end
			continue
		end
		if part.kind == "ring" then
			part.stroke.Color, part.stroke.Transparency = color, fade
			part.outline.Visible, part.outerStroke.Transparency = showOutline, outlineFade
			continue
		end
		part.frame.ImageColor3 = settings.cursorTint and not preview and color or env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		part.frame.ImageTransparency = fade
	end
end

function CustomCursor.native(state, hidden)
	local input = state.context.inputService
	if hidden then
		if not state.hidden or input.MouseIconEnabled then state.hidden = true; pcall(function() input.MouseIconEnabled = false end) end
		return
	end
	if state.hidden then state.hidden = false; pcall(function() input.MouseIconEnabled = true end) end
end

function CustomCursor.ensure(state)
	if state.gui and state.gui.Parent then return end
	local env, context = state.context.environment, state.context
	local gui = env.Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name, gui.ResetOnSpawn, gui.DisplayOrder = "PulseCursor", false, 2147483000
	gui.ZIndexBehavior, gui.IgnoreGuiInset = env.Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, true
	pcall(function() gui.Parent = context.coreGui end)
	if not gui.Parent then pcall(function() gui.Parent = context.player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end) end
	local frame = env.Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name, frame.BackgroundTransparency, frame.AnchorPoint = "K", 1, env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	frame.Size, frame.Visible, frame.Parent = env.UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64), false, gui
	local scale = env.Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Parent = frame
	state.gui, state.frame, state.scaleObject = gui, frame, scale
end

function CustomCursor.repaint(state)
	local color = CustomCursor.color(state, os.clock())
	CustomCursor.paint(state, state.parts, color, false)
	CustomCursor.paint(state, state.previewParts, color, false)
	if state.paintPreview then state.paintPreview() end
end

function CustomCursor.build(state)
	CustomCursor.ensure(state)
	state.parts = CustomCursor.draw(state, state.frame, state.settings.cursor)
	if state.preview then state.previewParts = CustomCursor.draw(state, state.preview, state.settings.cursor) end
	CustomCursor.repaint(state)
end

function CustomCursor.select(state, shape)
	if not CustomCursor.valid(state, shape) then shape = "Off" end
	state.settings.cursor = shape
	CustomCursor.build(state)
	if state.paintCells then state.paintCells() end
end

function CustomCursor.step(state, delta, now)
	local context, settings, env = state.context, state.settings, state.context.environment
	if settings.cursorSpin then
		state.rotation = (state.rotation + delta * (tonumber(settings.cursorSpinSpd) or 180) * (settings.cursorSpinRev and -1 or 1)) % 360
	else state.rotation = 0 end
	local color = settings.cursorRainbow and CustomCursor.color(state, now) or nil
	if state.preview and settings.cursor ~= "Off" and (not context.window or context.window.IsOpen ~= false) then
		state.preview.Rotation = state.rotation
		state.previewScale.Scale = math.min(CustomCursor.scale(state, now, state.previewClickAt, math.clamp((tonumber(settings.cursorSize) or 100) / 100, 0.4, 3)), 2.1)
		if color then CustomCursor.paint(state, state.previewParts, color, false) end
	end
	if settings.cursor == "Off" or not state.frame then
		if state.frame then state.frame.Visible = false end
		CustomCursor.native(state, false)
		return
	end
	local mouse = context.inputService.MouseEnabled
	CustomCursor.native(state, mouse)
	state.frame.Visible = mouse
	if not mouse then return end
	local location = context.inputService:GetMouseLocation()
	state.frame.Position, state.frame.Rotation = env.UDim2.fromOffset(location.X, location.Y), state.rotation
	state.scaleObject.Scale = CustomCursor.scale(state, now, state.clickAt, math.clamp((tonumber(settings.cursorSize) or 100) / 100, 0.2, 4))
	if color then CustomCursor.paint(state, state.parts, color, false) end
end

function CustomCursor.gallery(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local section, create = context.section, context.create
	local hero = create("Frame", {Name = "CursorHero", Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 92), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, section:Container())
	local previewButton = create("TextButton", {Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4), Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(84, 84),
		BorderSizePixel = 0, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "", ClipsDescendants = true}, hero)
	context.corner(previewButton, 14)
	context.nova.Theme.bind(previewButton, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
	local outline = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.86, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, previewButton)
	context.nova.Theme.bind(outline, "Color", "Glass")
	state.preview = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 2}, previewButton)
	state.previewScale = create("UIScale", {}, state.preview)
	local placeholder = context.nova.icon({Parent = previewButton, Icon = "mouse-pointer-2", Size = 24, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "Faint", Z = 2})
	local title = context.nova.label({Parent = hero, Text = "", Font = context.nova.Font.Bold(), Size = 15, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(98, 12),
		Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 20), Truncate = true, Color = "Text"})
	local detail = context.nova.label({Parent = hero, Text = "", Font = context.nova.Font.Medium(), Size = 12, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(98, 34),
		Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 16), Truncate = true, Color = "Muted"})
	local off = create("TextButton", {Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset(98, 58), Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(75, 24), BorderSizePixel = 0,
		AutoButtonColor = false, Text = context.translation.tr("Turn off")}, hero)
	context.corner(off, 8)
	context.nova.Theme.bind(off, "BackgroundColor3", "Elevated")
	context.nova.Theme.bind(off, "TextColor3", "Text")
	state.paintPreview = function()
		local shape, settings = state.settings.cursor, state.settings
		local name = "Off"
		for _, item in ipairs(CURSOR_SHAPES) do if item[1] == shape then name = item[2]; break end end
		local index = type(shape) == "string" and shape:match("^I(%d+)$")
		if index then name = context.translation.tr("Image") .. " #" .. index else name = context.translation.tr(name) end
		title.Text, placeholder.ImageTransparency, off.Visible = name, shape == "Off" and 0 or 1, shape ~= "Off"
		local details = {context.translation.tr("Size") .. " " .. tostring(math.floor((tonumber(settings.cursorSize) or 100) + 0.5)) .. "%"}
		for _, item in ipairs({{"cursorSpin", "Spin"}, {"cursorPulse", "Pulse"}, {"cursorRainbow", "Rainbow"}, {"cursorClick", "Bounce"}}) do
			if settings[item[1]] then table.insert(details, context.translation.tr(item[2])) end
		end
		detail.Text = table.concat(details, " В· ")
		context.nova.paint(outline, "Color", shape ~= "Off" and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
		outline.Transparency = shape ~= "Off" and 0.45 or 0.86
	end
	context.connect(previewButton.MouseButton1Click, function() state.previewClickAt = os.clock() end)
	context.connect(off.MouseButton1Click, function() CustomCursor.select(state, "Off") end)
	local tabs = create("Frame", {Name = "CursorCategories", Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, section:Container())
	local grids, buttons = {}, {}
	for index, category in ipairs({"Shapes", "Crosshairs"}) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Position = env.UDim2.new((index - 1) * 0.5, 0, 0, 0), Size = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 1, 0),
			Text = context.translation.tr(category), AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0}, tabs)
		context.corner(button, 8)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "TextColor3", "Text")
		context.translation.bind(button, category)
		buttons[category] = button
		local frame = create("Frame", {Name = "Cursor" .. category, Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false}, section:Container())
		local layout = create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
		grids[category] = {frame = frame, layout = layout, built = false}
	end
	state.cells = {}
	state.paintCells = function()
		for key, cell in pairs(state.cells) do
			local selected = key == state.settings.cursor
			context.nova.paint(cell.button, "BackgroundColor3", selected and "Accent" or "Surface2", "Quick")
			context.nova.paint(cell.stroke, "Color", (selected or cell.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			cell.button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.82 or 0
			cell.stroke.Transparency, cell.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0.15 or (cell.hover and 0.5 or 0.86), selected and 1.5 or 1
			cell.check.Visible = selected
		end
	end
	local function addCell(parent, order, key)
		local previewScale
		local button = create("TextButton", {Name = "C" .. key, LayoutOrder = order, AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "",
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true}, parent)
		context.corner(button, 10)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.86, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		if key == "Off" then
			context.nova.icon({Parent = button, Icon = "ban", Size = 18, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
				Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "Muted", Z = 2})
		else
			local imageIndex = tonumber(key:match("^I(%d+)$") or "")
			if imageIndex then
				create("ImageLabel", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
					Size = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.68, 0.68), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = env.Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
					Image = state.images[imageIndex], ZIndex = 2}, button)
			else
				local preview = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
					Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(64, 64), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 2}, button)
				previewScale = create("UIScale", {Scale = 0.65}, preview)
				CustomCursor.paint(state, CustomCursor.draw(state, preview, key), env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), true)
			end
		end
		local check = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = env.UDim2.new(1, -3, 0, 3),
			Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 4}, button)
		context.corner(check, 7)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(check, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
		context.nova.icon({Parent = check, Icon = "check", Size = 10, Pos = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
			Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "OnAccent", Z = 5})
		local cell = {button = button, stroke = stroke, check = check, previewScale = previewScale, hover = false}
		state.cells[key] = cell
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function() CustomCursor.select(state, key) end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() cell.hover = true; state.paintCells() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() cell.hover = false; state.paintCells() end)
	end
	local function resize()
		local grid = grids[state.category or "Shapes"]
		local scale = context.window and context.window.Scale and context.window.Scale.Scale or 1
		local width = grid.frame.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(scale, 0.1)
		if width < 60 then return end
		local columns = math.max(4, math.floor((width + 8) / 54))
		local cellSize = math.floor((width - 8 * (columns - 1)) / columns)
		for _, item in pairs(grids) do item.layout.CellSize = env.UDim2.fromOffset(cellSize, cellSize) end
		for _, item in pairs(state.cells) do
			if item.previewScale then item.previewScale.Scale = math.clamp(cellSize / 64 * 0.9, 0.3, 1.2) end
		end
	end
	for _, grid in pairs(grids) do pcall(function()
		context.connect(grid.frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"), resize)
	end) end
	local function show(category)
		state.category = category
		local grid = grids[category]
		if not grid.built then
			grid.built = true
			if category == "Shapes" then
				addCell(grid.frame, 0, "Off")
				for index, item in ipairs(CURSOR_SHAPES) do addCell(grid.frame, index, item[1]) end
			else
				for index in ipairs(state.images) do addCell(grid.frame, index, "I" .. index) end
			end
		end
		for name, value in pairs(grids) do
			value.frame.Visible = name == category
			context.nova.paint(buttons[name], "BackgroundColor3", name == category and "Accent" or "Surface2", "Quick")
		end
		state.paintCells()
		pcall(resize)
	end
	for category, button in pairs(buttons) do context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function() show(category) end) end
	show(state.settings.cursor:match("^I%d+$") and "Crosshairs" or "Shapes")
	state.paintPreview()
end

function CustomCursor.install(context)
	local env, section = context.environment, context.section
	local settings = context.configuration.optim
	CustomCursor.initialize(settings, env)
	local state = {context = context, settings = settings, images = CustomCursor.images(), parts = {}, previewParts = {},
		rotation = 0, clickAt = -9, previewClickAt = -9, hidden = false}
	if not CustomCursor.valid(state, settings.cursor) then settings.cursor = "Off" end
	section:Header("Custom Cursor")
	local galleryReady = pcall(CustomCursor.gallery, state)
	if not galleryReady then state.preview, state.previewScale, state.paintPreview, state.paintCells = nil, nil, nil, nil end
	local function slider(text, key, min, max, suffix)
		section:Slider({Text = text, Min = min, Max = max, Decimals = 0, Suffix = suffix, Default = settings[key], Callback = function(value)
			settings[key] = value
			CustomCursor.repaint(state)
		end})
	end
	local function toggle(text, key, children)
		section:Toggle({Text = text, Default = settings[key] == true, Settings = children, Callback = function(value)
			settings[key] = value == true
			CustomCursor.repaint(state)
		end})
	end
	slider("Cursor Size", "cursorSize", 40, 300, "%")
	slider("Cursor Transparency", "cursorFade", 0, 90, "%")
	section:Colorpicker({Text = "Cursor Color", Default = env.Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(settings.cursorR) or 255, tonumber(settings.cursorG) or 255, tonumber(settings.cursorB) or 255), Callback = function(color)
		if env.typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
		settings.cursorR, settings.cursorG, settings.cursorB = math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)
		CustomCursor.repaint(state)
	end})
	toggle("Cursor Outline", "cursorOutline")
	toggle("Recolor Images", "cursorTint")
	toggle("Rainbow Cursor", "cursorRainbow", function() slider("Rainbow Speed", "cursorRainbowSpd", 1, 10) end)
	toggle("Cursor Pulse", "cursorPulse", function() slider("Pulse Speed", "cursorPulseSpd", 1, 10); slider("Pulse Size", "cursorPulseAmt", 5, 80, "%") end)
	toggle("Cursor Spin", "cursorSpin", function() slider("Spin Speed", "cursorSpinSpd", 10, 1080, "В°"); toggle("Reverse Spin", "cursorSpinRev") end)
	toggle("Click Bounce", "cursorClick", function() slider("Bounce Size", "cursorClickAmt", 5, 80, "%") end)
	if context.inputService.InputBegan then context.connect(context.inputService.InputBegan, function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == env.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then state.clickAt = os.clock() end
	end) end
	if context.runService.RenderStepped then context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function(delta)
		CustomCursor.step(state, delta, os.clock())
	end) end
	table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		if not CustomCursor.valid(state, settings.cursor) then settings.cursor = "Off" end
		pcall(CustomCursor.build, state)
		if state.paintCells then state.paintCells() end
	end)
	env.task.defer(function() if context.isAlive() then pcall(CustomCursor.build, state) end end)
	return state, function()
		CustomCursor.native(state, false)
		if state.gui then pcall(function() state.gui:Destroy() end) end
	end
end
