local VisualWorld = {}

function VisualWorld.defaults(environment)
	local rgb, white, black = environment.Color3.fromRGB, environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), environment.Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	return {on = false, preset = "Original", strength = 100, quality = "Full", tech = "Future", brightScale = 1,
		ambientLift = 0, airScale = 1, skybox = "Game", lampShadows = false, ambientScale = 1, ambientTint = white,
		brightOn = false, bright = 2, timeOn = false, time = 14, ambientOn = false, ambient = rgb(150, 150, 150),
		outdoorAmbient = rgb(170, 170, 170), exposureOn = false, exposure = 0, noShadows = false, shadowSoftOn = false,
		shadowSoftness = 0.5, envOn = false, envDiffuse = 1, envSpecular = 1, shiftOn = false, shiftTop = black,
		shiftBottom = black, fogOn = false, fogStart = 0, fogEnd = 2000, fogColour = rgb(190, 190, 190),
		atmosphereOn = false, density = 0.28, offset = 0.1, haze = 1, glare = 0.1, atmosphereColour = rgb(199, 211, 230),
		decay = rgb(110, 120, 140), noAtmosphere = false, noPost = false, skyOn = false, stars = 3000, sunSize = 21,
		moonSize = 11, celestial = true, cloudsOn = false, cloudCover = 0.35, cloudDensity = 0.35, cloudColour = white,
		gradeOn = false, gradeBrightness = 0, contrast = 0, saturation = 0, tint = white, toneOn = false, tone = "Default",
		bloomOn = false, bloomIntensity = 0.3, bloomSize = 24, bloomThreshold = 1.2, raysOn = false, raysIntensity = 0.06,
		raysSpread = 0.8, vignetteOn = false, vignette = 35, dofOn = false, dofFar = 0.14, dofFocus = 50,
		dofRadius = 60, lampBoost = 1, neonGlow = false, latOn = false, lat = 41.7, mats = "Game"}
end

function VisualWorld.remember(state, object, property)
	local properties = state.saved[object]
	if properties and properties[property] then return properties[property] end
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
	if not ok then return nil end
	properties = properties or {}
	state.saved[object] = properties
	properties[property] = {value = value}
	return properties[property]
end

function VisualWorld.baseline(state, object, property)
	local saved = VisualWorld.remember(state, object, property)
	if not saved then return nil end
	local ok, current = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
	if ok and saved.written and current ~= saved.last then saved.value = current end
	return saved.value
end

function VisualWorld.release(state, object, property)
	local properties = state.saved[object]
	local saved = properties and properties[property]
	if not saved then return end
	pcall(function()
		local current = object[property]
		if saved.written and current ~= saved.last then saved.value = current end
		object[property] = saved.value
	end)
	properties[property] = nil
	if not next(properties) then state.saved[object] = nil end
end

function VisualWorld.sync(state, object, property, enabled, value)
	if not object then return end
	if not enabled then return VisualWorld.release(state, object, property) end
	local saved = VisualWorld.remember(state, object, property)
	if not saved then return end
	pcall(function()
		local current = object[property]
		if saved.written and current ~= saved.last then saved.value = current end
		saved.written, saved.last = true, value
		if current ~= value then object[property] = value end
		saved.last = object[property]
	end)
end

function VisualWorld.drop(state, name)
	local object = state.effects[name]
	state.effects[name] = nil
	if not object then return end
	state.owned[object] = nil
	pcall(function() object:Destroy() end)
end

function VisualWorld.ensure(state, name, class, parent)
	local object = state.effects[name]
	if object and object.Parent == parent then return object end
	VisualWorld.drop(state, name)
	local ok, created = pcall(function()
		local result = state.context.environment.Instance.new(class)
		state.owned[result], state.effects[name] = true, result
		result.Name, result.Parent = "PulseVisual_" .. name, parent
		return result
	end)
	if not ok then VisualWorld.drop(state, name); return nil end
	return created
end

function VisualWorld.targets(state, parent, class)
	local result, seen = {}, {}
	if not parent then return result end
	for _, object in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
		if not state.owned[object] and object:IsA(class) and object.Name:sub(1, 5) ~= "Pulse" and object.Name:sub(1, 4) ~= "Nova" then
			table.insert(result, object)
			seen[object] = true
		end
	end
	for object, properties in pairs(state.saved) do
		if not seen[object] and properties.Parent and properties.Parent.value == parent and object:IsA(class) then table.insert(result, object) end
	end
	return result
end

function VisualWorld.configure(state, name, class, enabled, properties)
	if not enabled then return VisualWorld.drop(state, name) end
	local context = state.context
	local object = VisualWorld.ensure(state, name, class, context.workspace.CurrentCamera or context.lighting)
	if not object then return end
	for property, value in pairs(properties) do pcall(function() object[property] = value end) end
	object.Enabled = true
end

function VisualWorld.apply(state)
	if not state.values.on or state.applying then return end
	state.applying = true
	local ok, message = pcall(function()
		local context, v = state.context, state.values
		local env, lighting = context.environment, context.lighting
		local sync = function(object, property, enabled, value) VisualWorld.sync(state, object, property, enabled, value) end
		local technology
		if v.tech ~= "Game" then pcall(function() technology = env.Enum.Technology[v.tech] end) end
		sync(lighting, "Technology", technology ~= nil, technology)
		local scaledBrightness = not v.brightOn and v.brightScale ~= 1
		local brightness = scaledBrightness and VisualWorld.baseline(state, lighting, "Brightness")
		sync(lighting, "Brightness", v.brightOn or scaledBrightness, v.brightOn and v.bright or (tonumber(brightness) or 3) * v.brightScale)
		sync(lighting, "ClockTime", v.timeOn, v.time % 24)
		local white = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		local scaledAmbient = not v.ambientOn and (v.ambientLift > 0 or v.ambientScale ~= 1 or v.ambientTint ~= white)
		for _, entry in ipairs({{"Ambient", v.ambient}, {"OutdoorAmbient", v.outdoorAmbient}}) do
			local base = scaledAmbient and VisualWorld.baseline(state, lighting, entry[1]) or env.Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
			local adjusted = env.Color3.new(math.clamp(base.R * v.ambientScale * v.ambientTint.R, 0, 1),
				math.clamp(base.G * v.ambientScale * v.ambientTint.G, 0, 1), math.clamp(base.B * v.ambientScale * v.ambientTint.B, 0, 1)):Lerp(white, math.clamp(v.ambientLift, 0, 1))
			sync(lighting, entry[1], v.ambientOn or scaledAmbient, v.ambientOn and entry[2] or adjusted)
		end
		for _, entry in ipairs({{"ExposureCompensation", v.exposureOn, v.exposure}, {"GlobalShadows", v.noShadows, false},
			{"ShadowSoftness", v.shadowSoftOn, v.shadowSoftness}, {"EnvironmentDiffuseScale", v.envOn, v.envDiffuse},
			{"EnvironmentSpecularScale", v.envOn, v.envSpecular}, {"ColorShift_Top", v.shiftOn, v.shiftTop},
			{"ColorShift_Bottom", v.shiftOn, v.shiftBottom}, {"FogStart", v.fogOn, math.clamp(v.fogStart, 0, 1999)},
			{"FogEnd", v.fogOn, math.clamp(math.max(v.fogEnd, v.fogStart + 1), 1, 2000)}, {"FogColor", v.fogOn, v.fogColour},
			{"GeographicLatitude", v.latOn, v.lat}}) do sync(lighting, entry[1], entry[2], entry[3]) end
		local tone = env.Enum.TonemapperPreset.Default
		if v.toneOn then pcall(function() tone = env.Enum.TonemapperPreset[v.tone] or tone end) end
		for _, parent in ipairs({lighting, context.workspace.CurrentCamera}) do
			for _, effect in ipairs(VisualWorld.targets(state, parent, "PostEffect")) do sync(effect, "Enabled", v.noPost, false) end
			for _, effect in ipairs(VisualWorld.targets(state, parent, "ColorGradingEffect")) do sync(effect, "TonemapperPreset", true, tone) end
		end
		local atmosphereOn = v.atmosphereOn and not v.noAtmosphere and not v.fogOn
		local atmospheres = VisualWorld.targets(state, lighting, "Atmosphere")
		if atmosphereOn and #atmospheres == 0 then
			local object = VisualWorld.ensure(state, "Air", "Atmosphere", lighting)
			if object then table.insert(atmospheres, object) end
		else VisualWorld.drop(state, "Air") end
		for _, object in ipairs(atmospheres) do
			if not state.owned[object] then sync(object, "Parent", v.noAtmosphere or v.fogOn, nil) end
			for property, value in pairs({Density = v.density, Offset = v.offset, Haze = v.haze, Glare = v.glare, Color = v.atmosphereColour, Decay = v.decay}) do
				if state.owned[object] then object[property] = value; continue end
				local scaled = not atmosphereOn and property == "Density" and v.airScale ~= 1
				if scaled then value = (tonumber(VisualWorld.baseline(state, object, property)) or v.density) * v.airScale end
				sync(object, property, atmosphereOn or scaled, value)
			end
		end
		local skyboxes = {['Photoreal Day'] = {SkyboxBk = '6444884337', SkyboxFt = '6444884337', SkyboxLf = '6444884337',
			SkyboxRt = '6444884337', SkyboxDn = '6444884785', SkyboxUp = '6412503613', SunTextureId = '6196665106', MoonTextureId = '6444320592'}}
		local customSky = context.skyActive and context.skyActive()
		local skybox = not customSky and skyboxes[v.skybox] or nil
		local skyDetails = v.skyOn and not customSky
		local skies = VisualWorld.targets(state, lighting, "Sky")
		if (skyDetails or skybox) and #skies == 0 then
			local object = VisualWorld.ensure(state, "Sky", "Sky", lighting)
			if object then table.insert(skies, object) end
		else VisualWorld.drop(state, "Sky") end
		for _, object in ipairs(skies) do
			for property, value in pairs({StarCount = v.stars, SunAngularSize = v.sunSize, MoonAngularSize = v.moonSize, CelestialBodiesShown = v.celestial}) do
				if state.owned[object] then object[property] = value else sync(object, property, skyDetails, value) end
			end
			for _, property in ipairs({"SkyboxBk", "SkyboxFt", "SkyboxLf", "SkyboxRt", "SkyboxDn", "SkyboxUp", "SunTextureId", "MoonTextureId"}) do
				local value = skybox and skybox[property]
				if value then value = "rbxassetid://" .. value end
				if state.owned[object] then if value then object[property] = value end else sync(object, property, value ~= nil, value) end
			end
		end
		local terrain = context.workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
		local clouds = VisualWorld.targets(state, terrain, "Clouds")
		if v.cloudsOn and terrain and #clouds == 0 then
			local object = VisualWorld.ensure(state, "Clouds", "Clouds", terrain)
			if object then table.insert(clouds, object) end
		else VisualWorld.drop(state, "Clouds") end
		for _, object in ipairs(clouds) do
			for property, value in pairs({Cover = v.cloudCover, Density = v.cloudDensity, Color = v.cloudColour, Enabled = true}) do
				if state.owned[object] then object[property] = value else sync(object, property, v.cloudsOn, value) end
			end
		end
		local strength, full = math.clamp(v.strength / 100, 0, 1), v.quality == "Full"
		VisualWorld.configure(state, "Color", "ColorCorrectionEffect", v.gradeOn, {Brightness = v.gradeBrightness * strength,
			Contrast = v.contrast * strength, Saturation = v.saturation * strength, TintColor = white:Lerp(v.tint, strength)})
		VisualWorld.configure(state, "Bloom", "BloomEffect", v.bloomOn, {Intensity = v.bloomIntensity * strength, Size = v.bloomSize, Threshold = v.bloomThreshold})
		VisualWorld.configure(state, "Rays", "SunRaysEffect", v.raysOn and full, {Intensity = v.raysIntensity * strength, Spread = v.raysSpread})
		VisualWorld.configure(state, "Tone", "ColorGradingEffect", v.toneOn, {TonemapperPreset = tone})
		VisualWorld.configure(state, "DOF", "DepthOfFieldEffect", v.dofOn and full, {FarIntensity = v.dofFar * strength,
			FocusDistance = v.dofFocus, InFocusRadius = v.dofRadius, NearIntensity = 0})
		pcall(VisualWorld.materials, state, v.mats)
		VisualWorld.lamps(state)
		VisualWorld.overlay(state)
	end)
	state.applying = false
	if not ok then state.context.environment.warn("[Pulse] shaders: " .. tostring(message)) end
end

function VisualWorld.materials(state, name)
	if state.materialSet == name then return end
	local env, service = state.context.environment, state.context.materialService
	for material, value in pairs(state.materialPrevious) do
		pcall(function() service:SetBaseMaterialOverride(env.Enum.Material[material], value) end)
	end
	for _, variant in ipairs(state.materialVariants) do pcall(function() variant:Destroy() end) end
	table.clear(state.materialPrevious)
	table.clear(state.materialVariants)
	local materials = VISUAL_MATERIALS[name]
	if materials then
		for material, maps in pairs(materials) do
			pcall(function()
				local variant = env.Instance.new("MaterialVariant")
				variant.Name, variant.BaseMaterial = "PulseHD_" .. material, env.Enum.Material[material]
				for index, property in ipairs({"ColorMap", "NormalMap", "RoughnessMap", "MetalnessMap"}) do
					if maps[index] ~= "" then variant[property] = "rbxassetid://" .. maps[index] end
				end
				variant.StudsPerTile, variant.Parent = maps[5], service
				table.insert(state.materialVariants, variant)
				state.materialPrevious[material] = service:GetBaseMaterialOverride(env.Enum.Material[material])
				service:SetBaseMaterialOverride(env.Enum.Material[material], variant.Name)
			end)
		end
	end
	state.materialSet = materials and name or "Game"
end

function VisualWorld.lamps(state)
	local context, v = state.context, state.values
	local needed = v.lampShadows or math.abs(v.lampBoost - 1) > 0.01 or v.neonGlow
	local neons = {}
	if needed then
		for _, root in ipairs(context.workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if root.ClassName ~= "Model" or not (root:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or root.Name:find("Lobby", 1, true)) then continue end
			if context.players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(root) then continue end
			for _, object in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
				local class = object.ClassName
				if (class == "PointLight" or class == "SpotLight" or class == "SurfaceLight") and object.Name ~= "PulseNeonGlow" then
					local boost = math.abs(v.lampBoost - 1) > 0.01
					local brightness = boost and VisualWorld.baseline(state, object, "Brightness")
					local range = boost and VisualWorld.baseline(state, object, "Range")
					VisualWorld.sync(state, object, "Brightness", boost, (tonumber(brightness) or 1) * v.lampBoost)
					VisualWorld.sync(state, object, "Range", boost, math.min(60, (tonumber(range) or 8) * (1 + (v.lampBoost - 1) * 0.5)))
					VisualWorld.sync(state, object, "Shadows", v.lampShadows, true)
				end
				if v.neonGlow and object:IsA("BasePart") and object.Material == context.environment.Enum.Material.Neon and object.Transparency < 0.6 then
					local size = math.max(object.Size.X, object.Size.Y, object.Size.Z)
					if size >= 0.8 and #neons < 90 then table.insert(neons, {object, size}) end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	for object, properties in pairs(state.saved) do
		if object.ClassName ~= "PointLight" and object.ClassName ~= "SpotLight" and object.ClassName ~= "SurfaceLight" then continue end
		if not v.lampShadows then VisualWorld.release(state, object, "Shadows") end
		if math.abs(v.lampBoost - 1) <= 0.01 then
			VisualWorld.release(state, object, "Brightness")
			VisualWorld.release(state, object, "Range")
		end
	end
	local keep = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(neons) do
		local part, size = entry[1], entry[2]
		keep[part] = true
		if state.neons[part] then continue end
		pcall(function()
			local light = context.environment.Instance.new("PointLight")
			light.Name, light.Color, light.Brightness = "PulseNeonGlow", part.Color, 1.4
			light.Range, light.Shadows, light.Parent = math.clamp(size * 1.6, 6, 18), false, part
			state.neons[part] = light
		end)
	end
	for part, light in pairs(state.neons) do
		if keep[part] and part.Parent and light.Parent then continue end
		pcall(function() light:Destroy() end)
		state.neons[part] = nil
	end
end

function VisualWorld.overlay(state)
	local env, v = state.context.environment, state.values
	if not v.vignetteOn then
		if state.gui then state.gui:Destroy(); state.gui = nil end
		return
	end
	if not state.gui or not state.gui.Parent then
		local gui = env.Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		gui.Name, gui.ResetOnSpawn, gui.IgnoreGuiInset, gui.DisplayOrder = "PulseVisualScreen", false, true, 5
		pcall(function() gui.ScreenInsets = env.Enum.ScreenInsets.None end)
		local ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = state.context.coreGui end)
		if not ok or not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = state.context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end
		state.gui, state.edges = gui, {}
		for index = 1, 4 do
			local frame, gradient = env.Instance.new("Frame"), env.Instance.new("UIGradient")
			frame.Name, frame.BorderSizePixel, frame.Active = "Vignette" .. index, 0, false
			frame.BackgroundColor3, frame.Parent = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), gui
			frame.Size = index <= 2 and env.UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.28) or env.UDim2.fromScale(0.23, 1)
			if index == 2 then frame.Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.72) end
			if index == 4 then frame.Position = env.UDim2.fromScale(0.77, 0) end
			gradient.Rotation, gradient.Parent = ({90, 270, 0, 180})[index], frame
			table.insert(state.edges, gradient)
		end
	end
	local transparency = env.NumberSequence.new({env.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1 - math.clamp(v.vignette / 100, 0, 0.8)), env.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
	for _, gradient in ipairs(state.edges) do gradient.Transparency = transparency end
end

function VisualWorld.restore(state)
	state.applying = true
	for object, properties in pairs(state.saved) do
		for property in pairs(properties) do VisualWorld.release(state, object, property) end
	end
	for name in pairs(state.effects) do VisualWorld.drop(state, name) end
	for part, light in pairs(state.neons) do pcall(function() light:Destroy() end); state.neons[part] = nil end
	if state.gui then pcall(function() state.gui:Destroy() end); state.gui = nil end
	pcall(VisualWorld.materials, state, "Game")
	state.applying = false
end

function VisualWorld.request(state)
	if state.batch or state.queued or not state.values.on then return end
	state.queued = true
	local epoch = state.epoch
	state.context.environment.task.defer(function()
		if epoch ~= state.epoch then return end
		state.queued = false
		if state.context.isAlive() then VisualWorld.apply(state) end
	end)
end

function VisualWorld.enabled(state, enabled)
	state.values.on = enabled == true
	state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseVisOwns = state.values.on
	if not state.values.on then
		state.epoch += 1
		state.queued = false
		VisualWorld.restore(state)
		return
	end
	VisualWorld.request(state)
end

function VisualWorld.preset(state, name)
	local v = state.values
	if name == "Original" then
		VisualWorld.enabled(state, false)
		for key, value in pairs(state.defaults) do v[key] = value end
	else
		local selected
		for _, preset in ipairs(state.presets) do if preset.name == name then selected = preset; break end end
		if not selected then return end
		for key, value in pairs(state.defaults) do
			if key ~= "on" and key ~= "preset" and key ~= "quality" and key ~= "strength" and key ~= "tech" then v[key] = value end
		end
		for key, value in pairs(selected.values) do v[key] = value end
		v.preset, v.on = name, true
	end
	state.context.configuration.visuals = state.context.configuration.visuals or {}
	state.context.configuration.visuals.look = v.preset
	if state.lookControl then pcall(state.lookControl.SetSilent, v.preset) end
	for key, control in pairs(state.controls) do if control.SetSilent then pcall(control.SetSilent, v[key]) end end
	if name ~= "Original" then VisualWorld.enabled(state, true) end
end

function VisualWorld.snapshot(state)
	local env, result = state.context.environment, {}
	for key in pairs(state.defaults) do
		local value = state.values[key]
		if env.typeof(value) == "Color3" then
			result[key] = {c3 = {math.floor(value.R * 1000000 + 0.5) / 1000000, math.floor(value.G * 1000000 + 0.5) / 1000000, math.floor(value.B * 1000000 + 0.5) / 1000000}}
		else result[key] = value end
	end
	return result
end

function VisualWorld.applySnapshot(state, snapshot)
	if type(snapshot) ~= "table" then return end
	local env = state.context.environment
	for key, value in pairs(snapshot) do
		local default = state.defaults[key]
		if default == nil then continue end
		if type(value) == "table" and type(value.c3) == "table" then value = env.Color3.new(tonumber(value.c3[1]) or 0, tonumber(value.c3[2]) or 0, tonumber(value.c3[3]) or 0) end
		if env.typeof(value) == env.typeof(default) then state.values[key] = value end
	end
	state.context.configuration.visuals = state.context.configuration.visuals or {}
	state.context.configuration.visuals.look = state.values.preset
	if state.lookControl then pcall(state.lookControl.SetSilent, state.values.preset) end
	for key, control in pairs(state.controls) do if control.SetSilent then pcall(control.SetSilent, state.values[key]) end end
	VisualWorld.enabled(state, state.values.on)
end

function VisualWorld.install(context)
	local env, section = context.environment, context.section
	local defaults = VisualWorld.defaults(env)
	local state = {context = context, defaults = defaults, values = table.clone(defaults), controls = {}, presets = visualPresets(env),
		saved = {}, effects = {}, owned = {}, neons = {}, materialPrevious = {}, materialVariants = {}, materialSet = "Game", epoch = 0, batch = true}
	local v = state.values
	local function changed(key, value)
		v[key] = value
		if state.batch then return end
		if key == "on" then VisualWorld.enabled(state, value); return end
		VisualWorld.request(state)
	end
	local function toggle(text, key, children)
		state.controls[key] = section:Toggle({Text = text, Default = v[key], Settings = children, Callback = function(value) changed(key, value) end})
	end
	local function slider(text, key, min, max, decimals, suffix)
		state.controls[key] = section:Slider({Text = text, Min = min, Max = max, Decimals = decimals or 0, Suffix = suffix,
			Default = v[key], Callback = function(value) changed(key, value) end})
	end
	local function color(text, key)
		state.controls[key] = section:Colorpicker({Text = text, Default = v[key], Callback = function(value)
			if env.typeof(value) == "Color3" then changed(key, value) end
		end})
	end
	local function dropdown(text, key, options)
		local items = {}
		for _, value in ipairs(options) do table.insert(items, {code = value, label = value}) end
		state.controls[key] = section:Dropdown({Text = text, Default = v[key], Options = items, Callback = function(value)
			if value ~= nil then changed(key, value) end
		end})
	end
	section:Sub("Shaders")
	section:Columns()
	section:Group({Title = "Shaders", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	toggle("Enabled", "on")
	slider("Strength", "strength", 0, 100, 0, "%")
	dropdown("Quality", "quality", {"Full", "Light"})
	dropdown("Engine", "tech", {"Future", "ShadowMap", "Voxel", "Game"})
	section:Group({Title = "Looks", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	local options = {"Original"}
	for _, preset in ipairs(state.presets) do if not table.find(options, preset.name) then table.insert(options, preset.name) end end
	local looks = {}
	for _, name in ipairs(options) do table.insert(looks, {code = name, label = name}) end
	state.lookControl = section:Dropdown({Text = "Look", Options = looks, Default = context.configuration.visuals and context.configuration.visuals.look or "Original", NoSave = true,
		Callback = function(name)
			if state.batch then return end
			VisualWorld.preset(state, name)
			if context.applyWeatherExtra then context.applyWeatherExtra(name) end
		end})
	section:Group({Title = "Lighting", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	toggle("Brightness", "brightOn", function() slider("Light level", "bright", 0, 6, 2) end)
	toggle("Time of day", "timeOn", function() slider("Hour", "time", 0, 24, 2) end)
	toggle("Exposure", "exposureOn", function() slider("Exposure level", "exposure", -2, 2, 2) end)
	slider("Ambient level", "ambientScale", 0.2, 1.6, 2)
	color("Ambient tint", "ambientTint")
	toggle("Ambient light", "ambientOn", function() color("Indoor", "ambient"); color("Outdoor", "outdoorAmbient") end)
	toggle("Light colors", "shiftOn", function() color("Sunlit", "shiftTop"); color("Shaded", "shiftBottom") end)
	toggle("No shadows", "noShadows")
	toggle("Soft shadows", "shadowSoftOn", function() slider("Softness", "shadowSoftness", 0, 1, 2) end)
	toggle("Lamp shadows", "lampShadows")
	slider("Lamp brightness", "lampBoost", 0.5, 3, 2)
	toggle("Neon glow", "neonGlow")
	toggle("Sun angle", "latOn", function() slider("Latitude", "lat", -60, 80) end)
	toggle("Environment", "envOn", function() slider("Diffuse light", "envDiffuse", 0, 1, 2); slider("Reflections", "envSpecular", 0, 1, 2) end)
	section:Group({Title = "Air", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	toggle("Atmosphere", "atmosphereOn", function()
		slider("Density", "density", 0, 1, 2); slider("Horizon offset", "offset", -1, 1, 2)
		slider("Haze", "haze", 0, 10, 2); slider("Sun glare", "glare", 0, 10, 2)
		color("Color", "atmosphereColour"); color("Horizon", "decay")
	end)
	toggle("No atmosphere", "noAtmosphere")
	toggle("Fog control", "fogOn", function() slider("Fog start", "fogStart", 0, 2000); slider("Fog end", "fogEnd", 100, 2000); color("Color", "fogColour") end)
	section:Group({Title = "Sky", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	dropdown("Skybox", "skybox", {"Game", "Photoreal Day"})
	toggle("Sky details", "skyOn", function()
		toggle("Sun & moon", "celestial"); slider("Star count", "stars", 0, 5000); slider("Sun size", "sunSize", 0, 50, 1); slider("Moon size", "moonSize", 0, 40, 1)
	end)
	toggle("Clouds", "cloudsOn", function() slider("Cloud cover", "cloudCover", 0, 1, 2); slider("Cloud density", "cloudDensity", 0, 1, 2); color("Color", "cloudColour") end)
	section:Group({Title = "Color", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	toggle("Color grading", "gradeOn", function()
		slider("Brightness", "gradeBrightness", -0.3, 0.3, 2); slider("Contrast", "contrast", -0.5, 0.7, 2)
		slider("Saturation", "saturation", -1, 1, 2); color("Tint", "tint")
	end)
	toggle("Tone mapping", "toneOn", function() dropdown("Style", "tone", {"Default", "Retro"}) end)
	toggle("Mute game effects", "noPost")
	section:Group({Title = "Glow", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	toggle("Bloom", "bloomOn", function() slider("Glow intensity", "bloomIntensity", 0, 3, 2); slider("Glow radius", "bloomSize", 0, 56); slider("Glow threshold", "bloomThreshold", 0, 4, 2) end)
	toggle("Sun rays", "raysOn", function() slider("Ray intensity", "raysIntensity", 0, 0.5, 3); slider("Ray spread", "raysSpread", 0, 1, 2) end)
	section:Group({Title = "Screen", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	toggle("Vignette", "vignetteOn", function() slider("Edge shade", "vignette", 0, 80, 0, "%") end)
	toggle("Depth of field", "dofOn", function() slider("Blur strength", "dofFar", 0, 0.6, 2); slider("Focus distance", "dofFocus", 5, 200); slider("Sharp zone", "dofRadius", 5, 200) end)
	section:Group({Title = "Materials", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	dropdown("Materials", "mats", {"Game", "HD Realistic", "HD Worn", "HD Wet"})
	state.batch = false
	if context.lighting.Changed then context.connect(context.lighting.Changed, function() if not state.applying then VisualWorld.request(state) end end) end
	if context.lighting.ChildAdded then context.connect(context.lighting.ChildAdded, function(object)
		if not state.applying and not state.owned[object] then VisualWorld.request(state) end
	end) end
	local function watchCamera()
		if state.cameraConnection then state.cameraConnection:Disconnect(); state.cameraConnection = nil end
		if state.camera and state.camera ~= context.workspace.CurrentCamera then
			for _, effect in ipairs(VisualWorld.targets(state, state.camera, "PostEffect")) do
				VisualWorld.release(state, effect, "Enabled")
				VisualWorld.release(state, effect, "TonemapperPreset")
			end
		end
		state.camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
		if state.camera and state.camera.ChildAdded then state.cameraConnection = context.connect(state.camera.ChildAdded, function(object)
			if not state.owned[object] then VisualWorld.request(state) end
		end) end
		VisualWorld.request(state)
	end
	local ok, cameraChanged = pcall(function() return context.workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera") end)
	if ok and cameraChanged then context.connect(cameraChanged, watchCamera) end
	watchCamera()
	local elapsed = 0
	if context.runService and context.runService.Heartbeat then context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
		if not v.on then elapsed = 0; return end
		elapsed += delta
		if elapsed < 1 then return end
		elapsed = 0
		VisualWorld.request(state)
	end) end
	context.configuration.visuals = context.configuration.visuals or {}
	local visuals = context.configuration.visuals
	local savedLook = visuals.look
	if type(savedLook) == "string" and table.find(options, savedLook) then v.preset = savedLook end
	local legacy = {pack = "Natural", tod_morning = "Golden Hour", tod_midday = "Natural", tod_evening = "Dusk",
		tod_night = "Night", spirit = "Golden Hour", storm = "Overcast", streetlife = "Neon", realistic = "Natural",
		photostudio = "Overcast", desert = "Golden Hour"}
	local legacyLook = legacy[visuals.shader]
	visuals.shader = ""
	local migrateLook = v.on and visuals.lookVer ~= 4 and v.preset ~= "Original" and v.preset or nil
	visuals.lookVer = 4
	if not v.on and legacyLook then migrateLook = legacyLook end
	if migrateLook then VisualWorld.preset(state, migrateLook) else VisualWorld.enabled(state, v.on) end
	return state, function()
		if state.cameraConnection then state.cameraConnection:Disconnect() end
		VisualWorld.enabled(state, false)
	end
end

	{name = "Realistic Sky", key = "realsky", spec = { faces = { 18603249071, 18603241716, 18603244510, 18603245276, 18603246382, 18603261859 }, celestial = true, stars = 5000, sun = 60, moon = 1.5, sunTex = "", moonTex = "" }},
	{name = "Cloudy Day", key = "cloudyday", spec = { faces = { 679786546, 680029379, 679786832, 679786679, 679786411, 679787023 }, celestial = true, stars = 3000, sun = 21, moon = 11 }},
	{name = "Mountain Valley", key = "valley", spec = { faces = { 324014980, 324015477, 324014995, 324014679, 324015013, 324015409 }, celestial = true, stars = 3000, sun = 21, moon = 11 }},

	{name = "Rain Sky", key = "rainsky", spec = { faces = { 4495864450, 4495864887, 4495865458, 4495866035, 4495866584, 4495867486 }, celestial = true, stars = 3000, sun = 1, moon = 1, sunTex = "", moonTex = "" }},

local VisualSky = {}

function VisualSky.clearObjects(state)
	for _, object in ipairs(state.objects) do pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
	table.clear(state.objects)
	if state.original and not state.original.Parent then
		pcall(function() state.original.Parent = state.context.lighting end)
	end
end

function VisualSky.create(state, class, name, parent, properties)
	local object = state.context.environment.Instance.new(class)
	table.insert(state.objects, object)
	object.Name = name
	for property, value in pairs(properties) do object[property] = value end
	object.Parent = parent
	return object
end

function VisualSky.apply(state, spec)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	if state.original then state.original.Parent = nil end
	local celestial = spec.celestial ~= false
	if spec.faces then celestial = spec.celestial == true end
	local properties = {CelestialBodiesShown = celestial,
		StarCount = spec.stars or (spec.faces and 0 or 3000)}
	if spec.sun then properties.SunAngularSize = spec.sun end
	if spec.moon then properties.MoonAngularSize = spec.moon end
	if spec.sunTex then properties.SunTextureId = spec.sunTex end
	if spec.moonTex then properties.MoonTextureId = spec.moonTex end
	if spec.faces then
		for index, property in ipairs({"SkyboxBk", "SkyboxDn", "SkyboxFt", "SkyboxLf", "SkyboxRt", "SkyboxUp"}) do
			properties[property] = "rbxassetid://" .. spec.faces[index]
		end
	end
	VisualSky.create(state, "Sky", "PulseSky", context.lighting, properties)
	if spec.atmo then
		local atmosphere = spec.atmo
		VisualSky.create(state, "Atmosphere", "PulseSkyAtmo", context.lighting, {Density = atmosphere.d or 0.3,
			Offset = 0, Color = atmosphere.c or env.Color3.fromRGB(199, 199, 199),
			Decay = atmosphere.dk or env.Color3.fromRGB(106, 112, 125), Glare = atmosphere.g or 0, Haze = atmosphere.h or 0})
	end
	if spec.clouds then
		local clouds = spec.clouds
		local terrain = context.workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
		VisualSky.create(state, "Clouds", "PulseClouds", terrain, {Cover = clouds.cv or 0.5,
			Density = clouds.d or 0.5, Color = clouds.c or env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)})
	end
	if spec.clock then context.lighting.ClockTime = spec.clock end
	if spec.bright then context.lighting.Brightness = spec.bright end
	if spec.outdoor then context.lighting.OutdoorAmbient = spec.outdoor end
end

function VisualSky.activate(state, key, enabled)
	if state.changing then return end
	if enabled and (state.active == key or not state.skies[key]) then return end
	if not enabled and state.active ~= key then return end
	state.changing = true
	VisualSky.clearObjects(state)
	state.active = nil
	if enabled then
		local ok = pcall(VisualSky.apply, state, state.skies[key].spec)
		if ok then state.active = key else VisualSky.clearObjects(state) end
	end
	state.context.configuration.visuals = state.context.configuration.visuals or {}
	state.context.configuration.visuals.sky = state.active or ""
	state.changing = false
	if state.paint then state.paint() end
	if state.context.visualWorld then VisualWorld.request(state.context.visualWorld) end
end

function VisualSky.register(state, key, spec, name)
	if state.skies[key] then return end
	state.skies[key] = {spec = spec, name = name, ui = {Set = function(enabled) VisualSky.activate(state, key, enabled == true) end}}
end

function VisualSky.gallery(state)
	local context, env, section = state.context, state.context.environment, state.context.section
	section:Group({Title = "Custom Skies", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	local container = section:Container()
	if not container then return end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, container)
	create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 66), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
	local ok, atlas = pcall(env.getcustomasset, "PulseHub/sky_gallery_v2.jpg")
	if not ok then atlas = nil end
	local indices = {animesky = 1, pinksky = 2, heavensky = 3, realsky = 4, rainsky = 5, spacesky = 6, cloudyday = 7,
		valley = 8, mirror = 9, darkstorm = 10, animeocean = 11, violet = 12, rainbow = 13, cartoon = 14,
		pastel = 15, blood = 16, moonlit = 17, starry = 18, orbit = 19, nebula = 20, galaxy = 21, mcsky = 22}
	local tiles = {}
	local entries = {{name = "Default sky", key = ""}}
	for _, item in ipairs(CUSTOM_SKIES) do table.insert(entries, item) end
	for order, item in ipairs(entries) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order,
			BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)}, frame)
		context.corner(button, 10)
		local picture = create("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
			ScaleType = env.Enum.ScaleType.Crop}, button)
		context.corner(picture, 10)
		local index = indices[item.key]
		if index and atlas then
			picture.Image = atlas
			picture.ImageRectOffset = env.Vector2.new((index - 1) % 4 * 256, math.floor((index - 1) / 4) * 160)
			picture.ImageRectSize = env.Vector2.new(256, 160)
		end
		local shade = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = env.UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -5),
			Size = env.UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}, button)
		context.corner(shade, 6)
		local label = context.nova.label({Parent = shade, Text = context.translation.tr(item.name), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(),
			Size = 11, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Truncate = true, Color = "Text"})
		context.translation.bind(label, item.name)
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.8, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		local tile = {key = item.key, stroke = stroke, shade = shade, hover = false}
		table.insert(tiles, tile)
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function()
			if item.key == "" then
				if state.active then VisualSky.activate(state, state.active, false) end
			else VisualSky.activate(state, item.key, true) end
		end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() tile.hover = true; state.paint() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() tile.hover = false; state.paint() end)
	end
	state.paint = function()
		for _, tile in ipairs(tiles) do
			local selected = tile.key == (state.active or "")
			context.nova.paint(tile.stroke, "Color", (selected or tile.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			tile.stroke.Transparency, tile.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0 or (tile.hover and 0.4 or 0.8), selected and 2 or 1
			tile.shade.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.62 or 0.4
		end
	end
	state.paint()
end

function VisualSky.install(context)
	local original
	pcall(function() original = context.lighting:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Sky") end)
	local state = {context = context, original = original, objects = {}, skies = {}}
	for _, item in ipairs(CUSTOM_SKIES) do VisualSky.register(state, item.key, item.spec, item.name) end
	local api = {skies = state.skies, register = function(key, spec) VisualSky.register(state, key, spec) end,
		activate = function(key, enabled) VisualSky.activate(state, key, enabled ~= false) end,
		active = function() return state.active end,
		clear = function() if state.active then VisualSky.activate(state, state.active, false) end end}
	context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI = api
	context.visualWorld.context.skyActive = api.active
	pcall(VisualSky.gallery, state)
	table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		local key = context.configuration.visuals and context.configuration.visuals.sky or ""
		if state.skies[key] then VisualSky.activate(state, key, true); return end
		if state.active then VisualSky.activate(state, state.active, false) end
	end)
	local saved = context.configuration.visuals and context.configuration.visuals.sky
	if saved and state.skies[saved] then context.environment.task.defer(function()
		if context.isAlive() then VisualSky.activate(state, saved, true) end
	end) end
	return state, function()
		VisualSky.clearObjects(state)
		state.active = nil
		if context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI == api then context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI = nil end
	end
end

local TexturePack = {}
local TEXTURE_ORDER = {"Off", "Minecraft", "Nether", "Classic", "Stylized", "Toon", "Realistic", "Luxury", "Urban", "Medieval",
	"Sci-Fi", "Space", "Winter", "Desert", "Beach", "Candy", "SpongeBob", "Jungle", "Lava", "Halloween"}

function TexturePack.asset(state, pack, key)
	local cacheKey = pack .. "/" .. key
	if state.assets[cacheKey] then return state.assets[cacheKey] end
	local env, settings = state.context.environment, TEXTURE_PACKS[pack]
	if not settings then return nil end
	local path = "PulseHub/texpack/" .. string.lower(pack) .. "_" .. key .. "_v1." .. settings.ext
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		if not env.isfile(path) then
			local encoded = env.__pulseData.take("tex_" .. pack .. "_" .. key)
			if not encoded then return nil end
			env.writefile(path, buffer.tostring(OfflineFiles._decodeBuffer(encoded)))
		end
		return env.getcustomasset(path)
	end)
	if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then state.assets[cacheKey] = result end
	return state.assets[cacheKey]
end

function TexturePack.eligible(state, part)
	if not part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("Terrain") or part.Transparency >= 0.95 or part.MaterialVariant ~= "" then return false end
	if part.Name:sub(1, 5) == "Pulse" then return false end
	if part:IsA("MeshPart") and part.TextureID ~= "" then return false end
	local workspace = state.context.workspace
	if workspace.CurrentCamera and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.CurrentCamera) then return false end
	local parent = part.Parent
	while parent and parent ~= workspace do
		if parent:IsA("Model") and parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return false end
		if parent:IsA("Tool") or parent:IsA("Accessory") or parent.Name == "CoinContainer" or parent.Name:sub(1, 5) == "Pulse" then return false end
		parent = parent.Parent
	end
	return true
end

function TexturePack.variant(state, material, key, pack)
	local variant = state.variants[material]
	if not variant or not variant.Parent then
		variant = state.context.environment.Instance.new("MaterialVariant")
		variant.Name, variant.BaseMaterial, variant.Parent = "PulseTP_" .. material.Name, material, state.context.materialService
		state.variants[material] = variant
	end
	local asset = TexturePack.asset(state, state.settings.pack, key)
	if asset then variant.ColorMap = asset end
	variant:SetAttribute("PulseKey", key)
	variant.StudsPerTile = math.clamp((tonumber(state.settings.scale) or 4) * (pack.tile[key] or 1), 0.5, 32)
	return variant
end

function TexturePack.applyPart(state, part, pack)
	if state.parts[part] then return end
	local key = pack.map[part.Material.Name]
	if not key or not TexturePack.eligible(state, part) then return end
	local variant = state.variants[part.Material]
	if not variant or not variant.Parent then variant = TexturePack.variant(state, part.Material, key, pack) end
	state.parts[part] = {material = part.Material, color = part.Color}
	part.MaterialVariant = variant.Name
	if state.settings.mcColors and pack.neutral[key] then part.Color = state.context.environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1) end
end

function TexturePack.clear(state)
	state.generation += 1
	table.clear(state.queue)
	for part, saved in pairs(state.parts) do
		pcall(function()
			if not part.Parent then return end
			if part.MaterialVariant:sub(1, 8) == "PulseTP_" then part.MaterialVariant = "" end
			part.Color = saved.color
		end)
	end
	table.clear(state.parts)
	for _, variant in pairs(state.variants) do pcall(function() variant:Destroy() end) end
	table.clear(state.variants)
	if state.originalMaterials ~= nil then
		pcall(function() state.context.materialService.Use2022Materials = state.originalMaterials end)
		state.originalMaterials = nil
	end
end

function TexturePack.apply(state)
	state.last = state.settings.pack
	TexturePack.clear(state)
	local pack = TEXTURE_PACKS[state.settings.pack]
	if not pack then return end
	local context = state.context
	pcall(function()
		state.originalMaterials = context.materialService.Use2022Materials
		context.materialService.Use2022Materials = true
	end)
	local generation = state.generation
	context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		local descendants = context.workspace:GetDescendants()
		for index, part in ipairs(descendants) do
			if generation ~= state.generation or not context.isAlive() then return end
			if part:IsA("BasePart") then pcall(TexturePack.applyPart, state, part, pack) end
			if index % 600 == 0 then context.environment.task.wait() end
		end
	end)
end

function TexturePack.refreshColors(state)
	local pack = TEXTURE_PACKS[state.settings.pack]
	if not pack then return end
	for part, saved in pairs(state.parts) do
		pcall(function()
			if not part.Parent then return end
			local key = pack.map[saved.material.Name]
			part.Color = state.settings.mcColors and key and pack.neutral[key] and state.context.environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or saved.color
		end)
	end
end

function TexturePack.rescale(state)
	local pack = TEXTURE_PACKS[state.settings.pack]
	if not pack then return end
	for _, variant in pairs(state.variants) do pcall(function()
		local key = variant:GetAttribute("PulseKey")
		variant.StudsPerTile = math.clamp((tonumber(state.settings.scale) or 4) * (pack.tile[key] or 1), 0.5, 32)
	end) end
end

function TexturePack.gallery(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local container = context.section:Container()
	if not container then return end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, container)
	create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 56), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
	local tiles = {}
	for index, name in ipairs(TEXTURE_ORDER) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = index}, frame)
		context.corner(button, 10)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.86, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		local pack = TEXTURE_PACKS[name]
		if pack then
			local picture = create("ImageLabel", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0),
				Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = env.Enum.ScaleType.Stretch,
				Image = TexturePack.asset(state, name, pack.icon) or ""}, button)
			if pack.ext == "png" then pcall(function() picture.ResampleMode = env.Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated end) end
			context.corner(picture, 8)
		else
			context.nova.icon({Parent = button, Icon = "ban", Size = 22, Pos = env.UDim2.new(0, 28, 0.5, 0),
				Anchor = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Color = "Muted", Z = 2})
		end
		local label = context.nova.label({Parent = button, Text = context.translation.tr(name), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(),
			Size = 13, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(56, 0), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0), Truncate = true, Color = "Muted"})
		context.translation.bind(label, name)
		local tile = {name = name, button = button, stroke = stroke, label = label, hover = false}
		table.insert(tiles, tile)
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function()
			if name == state.settings.pack then return end
			state.settings.pack = name
			state.paint()
			TexturePack.apply(state)
		end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() tile.hover = true; state.paint() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() tile.hover = false; state.paint() end)
	end
	state.paint = function()
		for _, tile in ipairs(tiles) do
			local selected = tile.name == state.settings.pack
			context.nova.paint(tile.button, "BackgroundColor3", selected and "Accent" or "Surface2", "Quick")
			tile.button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.82 or 0
			context.nova.paint(tile.stroke, "Color", (selected or tile.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			tile.stroke.Transparency, tile.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0.1 or (tile.hover and 0.45 or 0.86), selected and 1.5 or 1
			context.nova.paint(tile.label, "TextColor3", (selected or tile.hover) and "Text" or "Muted", "Quick")
		end
	end
	state.paint()
end

function TexturePack.install(context)
	local config = context.configuration
	config.texpack = config.texpack or {}
	local settings = config.texpack
	if type(settings.pack) ~= "string" then settings.pack = "Off" end
	if not tonumber(settings.scale) then settings.scale = 4 end
	if settings.mcColors == nil then settings.mcColors = true end
	local state = {context = context, settings = settings, parts = {}, variants = {}, assets = {}, queue = {}, generation = 0}
	local section = context.section
	section:Group({Title = "Texture Pack", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	pcall(TexturePack.gallery, state)
	section:Toggle({Text = "Pack Colors", Default = settings.mcColors ~= false, Callback = function(value)
		settings.mcColors = value
		TexturePack.refreshColors(state)
	end})
	section:Slider({Text = "Texture Size", Min = 1, Max = 12, Default = settings.scale, Decimals = 0, Suffix = " st", Callback = function(value)
		settings.scale = value
		TexturePack.rescale(state)
	end})
	if context.workspace.DescendantAdded then context.connect(context.workspace.DescendantAdded, function(object)
		if settings.pack ~= "Off" and object:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(state.queue, object) end
	end) end
	if context.runService.Heartbeat then context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function()
		if #state.queue == 0 then return end
		local pack = TEXTURE_PACKS[settings.pack]
		if not pack then table.clear(state.queue); return end
		for _ = 1, math.min(#state.queue, 300) do
			local part = table.remove(state.queue)
			if part.Parent then pcall(TexturePack.applyPart, state, part, pack) end
		end
		if os.clock() - (state.prunedAt or 0) > 5 then
			state.prunedAt = os.clock()
			for part in pairs(state.parts) do if not part.Parent then state.parts[part] = nil end end
		end
	end) end
	table.insert(context.applyHooks, function()
		if type(settings.pack) ~= "string" then settings.pack = "Off" end
		if not tonumber(settings.scale) then settings.scale = 4 end
		if settings.mcColors == nil then settings.mcColors = true end
		if state.paint then state.paint() end
		if state.last ~= settings.pack then TexturePack.apply(state); return end
		TexturePack.refreshColors(state)
		TexturePack.rescale(state)
	end)
	context.environment.getgenv().__pulseTexPack = state
	if settings.pack ~= "Off" then context.environment.task.defer(function() if context.isAlive() then TexturePack.apply(state) end end) end
	return state, function()
		TexturePack.clear(state)
		if context.environment.getgenv().__pulseTexPack == state then context.environment.getgenv().__pulseTexPack = nil end
	end
end

end
