local Weather = {}
local WEATHER_QUALITIES = {Light = {rate = 0.45, probeInterval = 0.6}, Balanced = {rate = 0.8, probeInterval = 0.45},
	High = {rate = 1.15, probeInterval = 0.4}, Ultra = {rate = 1.6, probeInterval = 0.35}}
local WEATHER_LIMITS = {amount = {60, 0, 100}, opacity = {80, 20, 100}, scale = {1, 0.5, 1.8}, speed = {100, 60, 150},
	wind = {5, 0, 18}, windAngle = {35, 0, 360}, soundVolume = {60, 0, 100}, stormPower = {50, 0, 100},
	wetness = {55, 0, 100}, radius = {30, 12, 50}}
local WEATHER_ORDER = {"Snow", "Blossom", "Leaves", "Fireflies", "Mist", "Embers", "Ash", "Pollen"}

function Weather.defaults(environment)
	local rgb = environment.Color3.fromRGB
	return {on = false, kind = "Snow", amount = 60, quality = "Balanced", opacity = 80, scale = 1, speed = 100,
		wind = 5, windAngle = 35, gusts = true, shelter = true, grade = true, sound = true, soundVolume = 60,
		thunder = false, stormPower = 50, wet = true, wetness = 55, radius = 30, ripples = true, matchLight = true,
		snowColour = rgb(240, 246, 255), petalColour = rgb(255, 255, 255), leafColour = rgb(255, 255, 255),
		mistColour = rgb(214, 222, 232), emberColour = rgb(255, 140, 44), ashColour = rgb(168, 164, 160),
		fireflyColour = rgb(196, 255, 96), pollenColour = rgb(255, 238, 190), scene = ""}
end

function Weather.validate(state)
	local values = state.values
	if not state.recipes[values.kind] then values.kind = "Snow" end
	if not WEATHER_QUALITIES[values.quality] then values.quality = "Balanced" end
	for key, limits in pairs(WEATHER_LIMITS) do
		local value = tonumber(values[key])
		if not value or value ~= value or math.abs(value) == math.huge then value = limits[1] end
		values[key] = math.clamp(value, limits[2], limits[3])
	end
	for _, key in ipairs({"snowColour", "petalColour", "leafColour", "mistColour", "emberColour", "ashColour", "fireflyColour", "pollenColour"}) do
		if state.context.environment.typeof(values[key]) ~= "Color3" then values[key] = state.defaults[key] end
	end
end

function Weather.stop(state)
	for _, object in ipairs({state.folder, state.grade}) do if object then pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end end
	state.folder, state.grade, state.camera, state.builtKind, state.builtQuality, state.builtSound = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
	state.character, state.position, state.params = nil, nil, nil
	table.clear(state.nodes)
	table.clear(state.voices)
	state.visibility, state.frameElapsed, state.probeElapsed = 0, 0, 1
	state.covered, state.suspended, state.dirty, state.squash = nil, nil, true, nil
	state.audioLevel, state.coverBlend, state.gradeLevel = 0, 0, 0
end

function Weather.filter(state)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local excluded = {state.camera}
	for _, player in ipairs(state.context.players:GetPlayers()) do if player.Character then table.insert(excluded, player.Character) end end
	for part in pairs(state.invisible) do if part.Parent then table.insert(excluded, part) end end
	local params = env.RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType, params.FilterDescendantsInstances, params.IgnoreWater = env.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, excluded, true
	pcall(function() params.RespectCanCollide = true end)
	state.params = params
end

function Weather.cast(state, origin, direction)
	if not state.params then return nil, false end
	for _ = 1, 6 do
		local ok, result = pcall(state.context.workspace.Raycast, state.context.workspace, origin, direction, state.params)
		if not ok then return nil, false end
		if not result then return nil, true end
		local part = result.Instance
		if not part:IsA("BasePart") or part.Transparency < 0.95 then return result, true end
		state.invisible[part] = true
		pcall(function() state.params:AddToFilter(part) end)
	end
	return nil, true
end

function Weather.volume(state, name)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local part = env.Instance.new("Part")
	part.Name, part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanTouch, part.CanQuery = name, true, false, false, false
	part.CastShadow, part.Transparency, part.Size, part.Parent = false, 1, env.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), state.folder
	return part
end

function Weather.build(state, camera)
	Weather.stop(state)
	local env, values = state.context.environment, state.values
	state.camera, state.character = camera, state.context.player.Character
	state.builtKind, state.builtQuality, state.builtSound = values.kind, values.quality, values.sound
	state.folder = env.Instance.new("Folder")
	state.folder.Name, state.folder.Parent = "PulseWeather", camera
	local recipe = state.recipes[values.kind]
	for index, layer in ipairs(recipe.layers) do
		local grid = layer.noShelter and 1 or 3
		local center = (grid + 1) / 2
		for row = 1, grid do
			for column = 1, grid do
				local part = Weather.volume(state, "WeatherVolume" .. index .. "_" .. row .. "_" .. column)
				local emitter = env.Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
				emitter.Name, emitter.Enabled, emitter.Rate, emitter.LockedToPart = "WeatherParticles", false, 0, false
				emitter.VelocityInheritance, emitter.EmissionDirection = 0, layer.lift and env.Enum.NormalId.Top or env.Enum.NormalId.Bottom
				pcall(function()
					emitter.Shape, emitter.ShapeStyle, emitter.ShapeInOut = env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box,
						env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume, env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward
				end)
				emitter.Parent = part
				table.insert(state.nodes, {part = part, emitter = emitter, layer = layer, grid = grid, slots = grid * grid,
					gx = column - center, gz = row - center, seed = math.random() * 6.2832, blocked = true})
			end
		end
	end
	state.grade = env.Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	state.grade.Name, state.grade.Parent = "PulseWeatherGrade", camera
	if values.sound then
		for _, spec in ipairs(recipe.voices or {}) do
			local sound, equalizer = env.Instance.new("Sound"), env.Instance.new("EqualizerSoundEffect")
			sound.Name, sound.SoundId, sound.Looped, sound.Volume = "Voice", spec.id, true, 0
			sound.RollOffMode, sound.Parent = env.Enum.RollOffMode.Linear, state.folder
			equalizer.LowGain, equalizer.MidGain, equalizer.HighGain, equalizer.Parent = 0, 0, 0, sound
			pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
			table.insert(state.voices, {sound = sound, equalizer = equalizer, spec = spec, level = 0})
		end
	end
	Weather.filter(state)
	state.dirty = true
end

function Weather.sequence(environment, points)
	local keypoints = {}
	for _, point in ipairs(points) do table.insert(keypoints, environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(table.unpack(point))) end
	return environment.NumberSequence.new(keypoints)
end

function Weather.configure(state)
	local env, v, recipe = state.context.environment, state.values, state.recipes[state.values.kind]
	local color, opacity, speed = v[recipe.colour], v.opacity / 100, v.speed / 100
	local white, dark = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), state.envDark
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do
		local layer, emitter = node.layer, node.emitter
		node.tile = v.radius * 2 * layer.reach / node.grid
		node.part.Size = env.Vector3.new(node.tile, layer.h, node.tile)
		local amount = v.amount / 100
		local rate = layer.from and amount < layer.from and 0 or layer.rate[1] + (layer.rate[2] - layer.rate[1]) * amount
		node.rate = rate * WEATHER_QUALITIES[v.quality].rate * (v.radius / 30) ^ 2 / node.slots
		emitter.Texture = state.textures[layer.tex]
		local base, tint, fire = layer.col or color, layer.tint or 0.2, layer.col2 and v[layer.col2] or color
		if layer.fire then
			emitter.Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, fire:Lerp(env.Color3.fromRGB(255, 246, 210), 0.65)),
				env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, fire), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, fire:Lerp(env.Color3.fromRGB(140, 20, 6), 0.65))})
		else
			emitter.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(base:Lerp(white, layer.pulse and 0.35 or tint), base)
		end
		emitter.LightInfluence = layer.light or 0.3
		emitter.LightEmission = math.clamp((layer.glow or 0) * (0.4 + 0.6 * dark), 0, 1)
		emitter.Lifetime = env.NumberRange.new(layer.life[1] / speed, layer.life[2] / speed)
		emitter.Speed = env.NumberRange.new(layer.speed[1] * speed, layer.speed[2] * speed)
		local low, high = layer.size[1] * v.scale, layer.size[2] * v.scale
		local midpoint, spread = (low + high) * 0.5, (high - low) * 0.5
		local sizes = {{0, midpoint * 0.9, spread}, {0.2, midpoint, spread}, {1, midpoint * 0.92, spread}}
		if layer.pulse then sizes = {{0, 0}, {0.12, midpoint}, {0.3, high * 0.45}, {0.5, high}, {0.72, low * 0.5}, {0.88, midpoint * 0.8}, {1, 0}} end
		if layer.fire then sizes = {{0, midpoint, spread}, {0.7, midpoint * 0.75, spread * 0.7}, {1, midpoint * 0.2, 0}} end
		emitter.Size = Weather.sequence(env, sizes)
		local alpha = math.clamp(opacity * (layer.alpha or 1) * (0.9 + 0.2 * dark), 0, 1)
		local fade = {{0, 1}, {0.1, 1 - alpha}, {0.78, 1 - alpha}, {1, 1}}
		if layer.pulse or layer.flicker then
			fade = {{0, 1}, {0.1, 1 - alpha}, {0.3, 1 - alpha * 0.15}, {0.5, 1 - alpha * 0.1},
				{0.68, 1 - alpha}, {0.85, 1 - alpha * 0.25}, {1, 1}}
		end
		emitter.Transparency = Weather.sequence(env, fade)
		emitter.SpreadAngle, emitter.Drag = env.Vector2.new(layer.spread or 5, layer.spread or 5), layer.drag or 0
		emitter.Rotation = layer.rot and env.NumberRange.new(0, 360) or env.NumberRange.new(0)
		emitter.RotSpeed = layer.rot and env.NumberRange.new(-layer.rot, layer.rot) or env.NumberRange.new(0)
		emitter.Orientation, emitter.Enabled = env.Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera, true
		pcall(function() emitter.FlipbookLayout = env.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.None end)
	end
	state.dirty = false
end

function Weather.updateSquash(state)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local stretch = state.context.configuration.ui and tonumber(state.context.configuration.ui.stretch) or 0
	local squash = stretch > 0 and math.floor(-math.log(math.max(0.1, 1 - stretch / 100 * 0.6), 2) * 100 + 0.5) / 100 or 0
	if state.squash == squash then return end
	state.squash = squash
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do
		local layer, emitter = node.layer, node.emitter
		local rotating = layer.rot and not (squash > 0 and not layer.flutter)
		emitter.Rotation = rotating and env.NumberRange.new(0, 360) or env.NumberRange.new(0)
		emitter.RotSpeed = rotating and env.NumberRange.new(-layer.rot, layer.rot) or env.NumberRange.new(0)
		pcall(function()
			if layer.flutter then
				emitter.Squash = Weather.sequence(env, {{0, 0}, {0.14, 0.75}, {0.29, -0.55}, {0.43, 0.65},
					{0.57, -0.7}, {0.71, 0.5}, {0.86, -0.6}, {1, 0.2}})
			else emitter.Squash = env.NumberSequence.new(math.clamp(squash, -3, 3)) end
		end)
	end
end

function Weather.clearParticles(state)
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do pcall(function() node.emitter:Clear() end) end
end

function Weather.covered(state, position, height)
	local vector = state.context.environment.Vector3
	local hit, valid = Weather.cast(state, position + vector.new(0, 0.4, 0), vector.new(0, height or 40, 0))
	return not valid or hit ~= nil
end

function Weather.probe(state, focus)
	local vector, v = state.context.environment.Vector3, state.values
	Weather.filter(state)
	local covered = v.shelter and Weather.covered(state, focus) or false
	if covered and not state.covered then
		for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do if not node.layer.noShelter then node.emitter:Clear() end end
	end
	state.covered = covered
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do
		local blocked = false
		if not node.layer.noShelter then
			blocked = covered or v.shelter and Weather.covered(state, vector.new(node.part.Position.X, focus.Y, node.part.Position.Z), math.max(node.layer.h, 8) + 8)
		end
		if blocked and not node.blocked then node.emitter:Clear() end
		node.blocked = blocked
	end
end

function Weather.gust(state)
	if not state.values.gusts then return 1 end
	local phase = state.phase
	return 0.8 + math.sin(phase * 0.7) * 0.2 + math.sin(phase * 1.9) * 0.08 + math.sin(phase * 0.23 + 1.3) * 0.1
end

function Weather.audio(state, delta)
	local v = state.values
	local target = v.amount > 0 and 1 or 0
	state.audioLevel += (target - state.audioLevel) * (1 - math.exp(-delta * 2.5))
	target = state.covered and 1 or 0
	state.coverBlend += (target - state.coverBlend) * (1 - math.exp(-delta * 3))
	local fade, amount = 1 - math.exp(-delta * 3.5), v.amount / 100
	for _, voice in ipairs(state.voices) do
		local spec = voice.spec
		local level = spec.lo + (spec.hi - spec.lo) * amount
		if spec.from then level *= math.clamp((amount - spec.from) / 0.2, 0, 1) end
		level *= spec.base
		if spec.wind then level *= 0.35 + 0.65 * math.clamp(v.wind / 10, 0, 1.4) * Weather.gust(state) end
		local shelter = 1 - state.coverBlend * 0.55
		if spec.roof then shelter = state.coverBlend end
		if spec.open then shelter = 1 - state.coverBlend * 0.75 end
		if spec.steady then shelter = 1 end
		level *= v.soundVolume / 100 * state.audioLevel * shelter
		voice.level += (level - voice.level) * fade
		voice.sound.Volume = voice.level
		local highGain = state.coverBlend > 0.5 and not spec.roof and -14 or 0
		voice.equalizer.HighGain += (highGain - voice.equalizer.HighGain) * fade
	end
end

function Weather.gradeStep(state, delta)
	local v = state.values
	local grade = v.grade and state.grades[state.recipes[v.kind].grade or ""] or nil
	local target = grade and v.amount > 0 and 0.35 + 0.65 * v.amount / 100 or 0
	state.gradeLevel += (target - state.gradeLevel) * (1 - math.exp(-delta * 1.5))
	if not grade then state.grade.Enabled = false; return end
	state.grade.Enabled = true
	state.grade.TintColor = state.context.environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(grade.tint, state.gradeLevel)
	state.grade.Saturation, state.grade.Contrast, state.grade.Brightness = grade.saturation * state.gradeLevel,
		grade.contrast * state.gradeLevel, grade.brightness * state.gradeLevel
end

function Weather.groundY(state, focus)
	local now = os.clock()
	if state.groundAt and now - state.groundAt < 0.5 and state.groundY then return state.groundY end
	local vector = state.context.environment.Vector3
	local hit = Weather.cast(state, focus + vector.new(0, 4, 0), vector.new(0, -60, 0))
	state.groundAt, state.groundY = now, hit and hit.Position.Y or focus.Y - 3
	return state.groundY
end

function Weather.step(state, delta)
	local context, v = state.context, state.values
	if not v.on or not context.isAlive() then if state.folder then Weather.stop(state) end; return end
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then if state.folder then Weather.stop(state) end; return end
	delta = tonumber(delta) or 1 / 60
	if delta ~= delta or math.abs(delta) == math.huge then delta = 1 / 60 end
	state.frameElapsed += math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)
	if state.frameElapsed < 1 / 30 then return end
	delta, state.frameElapsed = state.frameElapsed, 0
	state.phase += delta
	Weather.validate(state)
	state.envClock += delta
	if state.envClock > 1 then
		state.envClock = 0
		local lighting = context.lighting
		local time, ambient = lighting.ClockTime, lighting.OutdoorAmbient
		local day = math.clamp((time - 5.5) / 1.5, 0, 1) * math.clamp((19.5 - time) / 1.5, 0, 1)
		local dark = math.clamp(1 - (ambient.R + ambient.G + ambient.B) / 3 / 0.55, 0, 1)
		local nextDark = math.max(1 - day, dark * 0.8)
		if math.abs(state.envDark - nextDark) > 0.08 then state.dirty = true end
		state.envDark = nextDark
	end
	if state.camera ~= camera or not state.folder or state.folder.Parent ~= camera or state.builtKind ~= v.kind
		or state.builtQuality ~= v.quality or state.builtSound ~= v.sound then Weather.build(state, camera) end
	local env, frame, character = context.environment, camera.CFrame, context.player.Character
	local focus = frame.Position
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if root and (root.Position - focus).Magnitude < 70 then focus = root.Position end
	local look = env.Vector3.new(frame.LookVector.X, 0, frame.LookVector.Z)
	if look.Magnitude > 0.05 then focus += look.Unit * 4 end
	if state.character ~= character or state.position and (focus - state.position).Magnitude > 48 then
		Weather.clearParticles(state)
		state.visibility, state.probeElapsed = 0, 1
		Weather.filter(state)
	end
	state.character, state.position = character, focus
	if state.dirty then Weather.configure(state); state.squash = nil end
	Weather.updateSquash(state)
	local ground = Weather.groundY(state, focus)
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do
		local height = node.layer.base == "ground" and ground + node.layer.y or focus.Y + node.layer.y
		node.part.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(focus.X + node.gx * node.tile, height, focus.Z + node.gz * node.tile)
	end
	state.probeElapsed += delta
	if state.probeElapsed >= WEATHER_QUALITIES[v.quality].probeInterval then state.probeElapsed = 0; Weather.probe(state, focus) end
	local suspended = v.amount == 0
	if suspended and not state.suspended then Weather.clearParticles(state) end
	state.suspended = suspended
	local target = suspended and 0 or 1
	state.visibility += (target - state.visibility) * (1 - math.exp(-delta * 5))
	if suspended then state.visibility = 0 end
	local angle = math.rad(v.windAngle)
	local direction = env.Vector3.new(math.cos(angle), 0, math.sin(angle))
	local sideways = env.Vector3.new(-direction.Z, 0, direction.X)
	local wind = v.wind * 0.3 * Weather.gust(state) * (v.speed / 100) ^ 2
	for _, node in ipairs(state.nodes) do
		local layer = node.layer
		local sway = math.sin(state.phase * 0.9 + node.seed) * 0.6 + math.sin(state.phase * 2.3 + node.seed * 1.7 + 1.7) * 0.4
		local along = math.cos(state.phase * 1.3 + node.seed * 0.7) * 0.5
		local force = layer.noWind and env.Vector3.zero or direction * wind * (layer.windK or 1)
		if layer.still then force = direction * (layer.drift or 0.3) * v.wind * 0.1
		else force += sideways * (layer.sway or 0) * sway + direction * (layer.sway or 0) * along * 0.5 + env.Vector3.new(0, (layer.lift or 0) - (layer.fall or 0), 0) end
		node.emitter.Acceleration = force
		node.emitter.Rate = (node.blocked or state.covered and not layer.noShelter) and 0 or node.rate * state.visibility
	end
	Weather.audio(state, delta)
	Weather.gradeStep(state, delta)
end

local WEATHER_SCENES = {
	{name = "Misty Morning", sky = "fog", look = "Overcast", weather = {kind = "Mist", amount = 70, wind = 2}},
	{name = "Snow Day", sky = "whiteout", look = "Overcast", weather = {kind = "Snow", amount = 55, wind = 4}},
	{name = "Blizzard", sky = "whiteout", look = "Overcast", weather = {kind = "Snow", amount = 100, wind = 16, speed = 130}},
	{name = "Winter Night", sky = "aurora1", look = "Night", weather = {kind = "Snow", amount = 45, wind = 3}},
	{name = "Cherry Blossom", sky = "rose", look = "Dusk", weather = {kind = "Blossom", amount = 62, wind = 5}},
	{name = "Autumn", sky = "golden", look = "Golden Hour", weather = {kind = "Leaves", amount = 58, wind = 7}},
	{name = "Golden Pollen", sky = "sunset", look = "Golden Hour", weather = {kind = "Pollen", amount = 62, wind = 2}},
	{name = "Firefly Night", sky = "mountains", look = "Night", weather = {kind = "Fireflies", amount = 65, wind = 1}},
	{name = "Bonfire Night", sky = "dusk", look = "Night", weather = {kind = "Embers", amount = 55, wind = 3}},
	{name = "Volcano", sky = "fiery", look = "Dusk", weather = {kind = "Ash", amount = 70, wind = 5}},
	{name = "Northern Lights", sky = "aurora2", look = "Night", fx = {"stars", "meteors"}},
	{name = "Clear Day", sky = "clear", look = "Natural"},
	{name = "Deep Space", sky = "nebula", look = "Night", fx = {"stars", "comets"}},
	{name = "Starry Night", sky = "mountains", look = "Night", fx = {"stars", "meteors"}},
	{name = "Meteor Shower", sky = "nebula", look = "Night", fx = {"stars", "meteors", "comets"}},
	{name = "Magic Night", sky = "aurora3", look = "Night", fx = {"stars", "runes", "dust", "net"}},
	{name = "Constellation Night", sky = "mountains", look = "Night", fx = {"net"}},
}

function Weather.syncUI(state)
	for key, control in pairs(state.controls) do
		if control.SetSilent then pcall(control.SetSilent, state.values[key]) end
	end
	if state.paintScenes then state.paintScenes() end
	if state.paintSkies then state.paintSkies() end
end

function Weather.changed(state)
	Weather.validate(state)
	state.dirty, state.probeElapsed = true, 1
	if not state.values.on then Weather.stop(state) end
end

function Weather.setSky(state, key)
	local api = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI
	if not api then return end
	if key and key ~= "" then api.activate(key, true) else api.clear() end
	if state.paintSkies then state.paintSkies() end
end

function Weather.takeLook(state, name)
	local world = state.context.visualWorld
	if not state.lookByWeather then state.previousLook = {preset = world.values.preset, on = world.values.on} end
	VisualWorld.preset(world, name)
	state.lookByWeather = true
end

function Weather.restoreLook(state)
	if not state.lookByWeather then return end
	state.lookByWeather = false
	local previous = state.previousLook
	state.previousLook = nil
	if previous and previous.on and previous.preset ~= "Original" then VisualWorld.preset(state.context.visualWorld, previous.preset); return end
	VisualWorld.preset(state.context.visualWorld, "Original")
end

function Weather.pickSky(state, key)
	local api = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI
	if api and api.active() == key then
		Weather.setSky(state, nil)
		if state.values.matchLight then Weather.restoreLook(state) end
		return
	end
	Weather.setSky(state, key)
	if not state.values.matchLight then return end
	for _, sky in ipairs(state.skies) do if sky.key == key and sky.look then Weather.takeLook(state, sky.look); return end end
end

function Weather.scene(state, name)
	local scene
	for _, item in ipairs(WEATHER_SCENES) do if item.name == name then scene = item; break end end
	if not scene then return end
	Weather.stop(state)
	local keep = {quality = true, shelter = true, sound = true, soundVolume = true, wet = true, wetness = true,
		grade = true, matchLight = true, radius = true, scale = true, ripples = true, gusts = true}
	for key, value in pairs(state.defaults) do
		if not keep[key] and not key:find("olour", 1, true) then state.values[key] = value end
	end
	state.values.scene, state.values.on = name, scene.weather ~= nil
	for key, value in pairs(scene.weather or {}) do state.values[key] = value end
	Weather.changed(state)
	Weather.setSky(state, scene.sky)
	if scene.look then Weather.takeLook(state, scene.look) end
	local skyFx = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyFx
	if type(skyFx) == "table" then
		if scene.fx then pcall(skyFx.scene, scene.fx) else pcall(skyFx.sceneEnd) end
	end
	Weather.syncUI(state)
end

function Weather.clearScene(state)
	local skyFx = state.context.environment.getgenv().__pulseSkyFx
	if type(skyFx) == "table" then pcall(skyFx.sceneEnd) end
	state.values.scene, state.values.on = "", false
	Weather.changed(state)
	Weather.setSky(state, nil)
	Weather.restoreLook(state)
	Weather.syncUI(state)
end

function Weather.applyExtra(state, preset)
	local extra
	for _, item in ipairs(state.context.visualWorld.presets) do if item.name == preset then extra = item.extra; break end end
	if not extra then
		if not state.extrasOn then return end
		state.extrasOn = false
		state.values.scene, state.values.on = "", false
		Weather.changed(state)
		if state.extrasSky then Weather.setSky(state, nil) end
		state.extrasSky = nil
		Weather.syncUI(state)
		return
	end
	Weather.stop(state)
	local keep = {quality = true, shelter = true, sound = true, soundVolume = true, wet = true, wetness = true,
		grade = true, matchLight = true, radius = true, scale = true, ripples = true, gusts = true}
	for key, value in pairs(state.defaults) do
		if not keep[key] and not key:find("olour", 1, true) then state.values[key] = value end
	end
	state.values.scene, state.values.on = "", type(extra.weather) == "table"
	for key, value in pairs(extra.weather or {}) do state.values[key] = value end
	Weather.changed(state)
	if extra.sky then Weather.setSky(state, extra.sky); state.extrasSky = extra.sky end
	state.extrasOn = true
	Weather.syncUI(state)
end

function Weather.sceneGallery(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local container = context.section:Container()
	if not container then return false end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, container)
	create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 34), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
	local swatches = {
		["Misty Morning"] = {{226, 232, 238}, {150, 164, 178}}, ["Snow Day"] = {{250, 252, 255}, {150, 180, 220}},
		Blizzard = {{240, 246, 255}, {150, 160, 178}}, ["Winter Night"] = {{80, 255, 170}, {30, 40, 90}},
		["Cherry Blossom"] = {{255, 196, 214}, {255, 140, 176}}, Autumn = {{255, 176, 60}, {176, 52, 24}},
		["Golden Pollen"] = {{255, 232, 150}, {255, 150, 60}}, ["Firefly Night"] = {{210, 255, 100}, {20, 30, 70}},
		["Bonfire Night"] = {{255, 220, 120}, {220, 60, 10}}, Volcano = {{120, 116, 118}, {255, 96, 30}},
		["Northern Lights"] = {{60, 255, 150}, {150, 90, 255}}, ["Clear Day"] = {{120, 190, 255}, {255, 255, 255}},
		["Deep Space"] = {{150, 70, 230}, {20, 12, 50}}, ["Starry Night"] = {{230, 236, 255}, {16, 22, 60}},
		["Meteor Shower"] = {{150, 210, 255}, {40, 20, 80}}, ["Magic Night"] = {{190, 120, 255}, {255, 200, 110}},
		["Constellation Night"] = {{150, 205, 255}, {12, 16, 40}},
	}
	local tiles = {}
	for index, scene in ipairs(WEATHER_SCENES) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = index}, frame)
		context.corner(button, 10)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.86, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		local swatch = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = env.UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0),
			Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)}, button)
		context.corner(swatch, 8)
		local colors = swatches[scene.name]
		create("UIGradient", {Color = env.ColorSequence.new(env.Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(colors[1])), env.Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(colors[2]))), Rotation = 45}, swatch)
		local label = context.nova.label({Parent = button, Text = context.translation.tr(scene.name), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(),
			Size = 12, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0), Truncate = true, Color = "Muted"})
		context.translation.bind(label, scene.name)
		local tile = {key = scene.name, button = button, stroke = stroke, label = label, hover = false}
		table.insert(tiles, tile)
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function()
			if state.values.scene == scene.name then Weather.clearScene(state) else Weather.scene(state, scene.name) end
		end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() tile.hover = true; state.paintScenes() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() tile.hover = false; state.paintScenes() end)
	end
	state.paintScenes = function()
		for _, tile in ipairs(tiles) do
			local selected = state.values.scene == tile.key
			context.nova.paint(tile.button, "BackgroundColor3", selected and "Accent" or "Surface2", "Quick")
			tile.button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.82 or 0
			context.nova.paint(tile.stroke, "Color", (selected or tile.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			tile.stroke.Transparency, tile.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0.1 or (tile.hover and 0.45 or 0.86), selected and 1.5 or 1
			context.nova.paint(tile.label, "TextColor3", (selected or tile.hover) and "Text" or "Muted", "Quick")
		end
	end
	state.paintScenes()
	return true
end

function Weather.skyGallery(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
	local container = context.section:Container()
	if not container then return false end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 1}, container)
	create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 66), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
	local tiles, entries = {}, {{key = "", name = "Default sky"}}
	for _, sky in ipairs(state.skies) do table.insert(entries, sky) end
	for index, sky in ipairs(entries) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = index}, frame)
		context.corner(button, 10)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(button, "BackgroundColor3", "Surface2")
		local picture = create("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ScaleType = env.Enum.ScaleType.Crop}, button)
		context.corner(picture, 10)
		if sky.key ~= "" then
			env.task.defer(function()
				local path = "PulseHub/textures/weather/sky_" .. sky.key .. "-" .. tostring(sky.tlen) .. ".jpg"
				local ok, image = pcall(env.getcustomasset, path)
				if ok and picture.Parent then picture.Image = image end
			end)
		end
		local shade = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = env.UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -5),
			Size = env.UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 18), BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}, button)
		context.corner(shade, 6)
		local label = context.nova.label({Parent = shade, Text = context.translation.tr(sky.name), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(),
			Size = 11, Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Truncate = true, Color = "Text"})
		context.translation.bind(label, sky.name)
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.8, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		local tile = {key = sky.key, stroke = stroke, shade = shade, hover = false}
		table.insert(tiles, tile)
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function()
			if sky.key == "" then
				Weather.setSky(state, nil)
				if state.values.matchLight then Weather.restoreLook(state) end
			else Weather.pickSky(state, sky.key) end
			state.values.scene = ""
			if state.paintScenes then state.paintScenes() end
		end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() tile.hover = true; state.paintSkies() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() tile.hover = false; state.paintSkies() end)
	end
	state.paintSkies = function()
		local api = env.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI
		local active = api and api.active() or ""
		for _, tile in ipairs(tiles) do
			local selected = tile.key == active
			context.nova.paint(tile.stroke, "Color", (selected or tile.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			tile.stroke.Transparency, tile.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0 or (tile.hover and 0.4 or 0.8), selected and 2 or 1
			tile.shade.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.62 or 0.4
		end
	end
	state.paintSkies()
	return true
end

function Weather.install(context)
	local env, section = context.environment, context.section
	local defaults = Weather.defaults(env)
	local textures, sounds, recipes, grades = weatherData(env)
	local state = {context = context, defaults = defaults, values = table.clone(defaults), textures = textures, sounds = sounds,
		recipes = recipes, grades = grades, skies = weatherSkies(env), controls = {}, nodes = {}, voices = {},
		invisible = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), phase = 0, envClock = 0, envDark = 0, visibility = 0,
		frameElapsed = 0, probeElapsed = 1, audioLevel = 0, coverBlend = 0, gradeLevel = 0, dirty = true, batch = true}
	local v = state.values
	local function changed(key, value)
		v[key] = value
		if not state.batch then Weather.changed(state) end
	end
	local function toggle(text, key, settings)
		state.controls[key] = section:Toggle({Text = text, Default = v[key], Settings = settings, Callback = function(value) changed(key, value) end})
	end
	local function slider(text, key, min, max, decimals, suffix)
		state.controls[key] = section:Slider({Text = text, Min = min, Max = max, Decimals = decimals or 0, Suffix = suffix,
			Default = v[key], Callback = function(value) changed(key, value) end})
	end
	local function dropdown(text, key, values)
		local options = {}
		for _, value in ipairs(values) do table.insert(options, {code = value, label = value}) end
		state.controls[key] = section:Dropdown({Text = text, Default = v[key], Options = options, Callback = function(value)
			if value ~= nil then changed(key, value) end
		end})
	end
	local function color(text, key)
		state.controls[key] = section:Colorpicker({Text = text, Default = v[key], Callback = function(value)
			if env.typeof(value) == "Color3" then changed(key, value) end
		end})
	end
	section:Sub("Weather")
	section:Columns()
	section:Group({Title = "Weather", Side = "Left"})
	toggle("Enabled", "on")
	dropdown("Weather type", "kind", WEATHER_ORDER)
	slider("Amount", "amount", 0, 100, 0, "%")
	slider("Visibility", "opacity", 20, 100, 0, "%")
	slider("Particle size", "scale", 0.5, 1.8, 2, "x")
	section:Group({Title = "Scenes", Side = "Right"})
	local scenesReady, scenesShown = pcall(Weather.sceneGallery, state)
	if not scenesReady or not scenesShown then
		local scenes = {{code = "", label = "Clear scene"}}
		for _, scene in ipairs(WEATHER_SCENES) do table.insert(scenes, {code = scene.name, label = scene.name}) end
		local sceneControl = section:Dropdown({Text = "Scene", Options = scenes, Default = "", NoSave = true, Callback = function(name)
			if state.batch then return end
			if name == "" then Weather.clearScene(state) else Weather.scene(state, name) end
		end})
		state.paintScenes = function() if sceneControl.SetSilent then sceneControl.SetSilent(v.scene) end end
	end
	section:Button({Text = "Reset all", Callback = function()
		Weather.clearScene(state)
		for key, value in pairs(state.defaults) do v[key] = value end
		Weather.changed(state)
		Weather.syncUI(state)
	end})
	section:Group({Title = "Wind", Side = "Left"})
	slider("Wind strength", "wind", 0, 18, 1)
	slider("Wind direction", "windAngle", 0, 360, 0, "В°")
	slider("Falling speed", "speed", 60, 150, 0, "%")
	toggle("Gentle gusts", "gusts")
	section:Group({Title = "Sky", Side = "Right"})
	toggle("Match lighting", "matchLight")
	local skiesReady, skiesShown = pcall(Weather.skyGallery, state)
	if not skiesReady or not skiesShown then
		local skies = {{code = "", label = "Default sky"}}
		for _, sky in ipairs(state.skies) do table.insert(skies, {code = sky.key, label = sky.name}) end
		local skyControl = section:Dropdown({Text = "Sky", Options = skies, Default = "", NoSave = true, Callback = function(key)
			if state.batch then return end
			if key == "" then Weather.setSky(state, nil); if v.matchLight then Weather.restoreLook(state) end
			else Weather.pickSky(state, key) end
			v.scene = ""
			if state.paintScenes then state.paintScenes() end
		end})
		state.paintSkies = function()
			local api = env.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI
			if skyControl.SetSilent then skyControl.SetSilent(api and api.active() or "") end
		end
	end
	section:Group({Title = "Sound", Side = "Left"})
	toggle("Weather sound", "sound", function() slider("Weather volume", "soundVolume", 0, 100, 0, "%") end)
	toggle("Weather tint", "grade")
	section:Group({Title = "Detail", Side = "Right"})
	dropdown("Particle budget", "quality", {"Light", "Balanced", "High", "Ultra"})
	slider("Coverage", "radius", 12, 50, 0, " st")
	toggle("Pause indoors", "shelter")
	section:Group({Title = "Particle colors", Side = "Right"})
	for _, entry in ipairs({{"Snow color", "snowColour"}, {"Petal tint", "petalColour"}, {"Leaf tint", "leafColour"},
		{"Mist color", "mistColour"}, {"Ember color", "emberColour"}, {"Ash color", "ashColour"},
		{"Firefly color", "fireflyColour"}, {"Pollen color", "pollenColour"}}) do color(entry[1], entry[2]) end
	state.batch = false
	local api = env.getgenv().__pulseSkyAPI
	if api then for _, sky in ipairs(state.skies) do api.register(sky.key, sky.spec) end end
	local savedSky = context.configuration.visuals and context.configuration.visuals.sky
	if api and savedSky and savedSky ~= "" then env.task.defer(function()
		if context.isAlive() then api.activate(savedSky, true); state.paintSkies() end
	end) end
	context.visualWorld.context.applyWeatherExtra = function(name) Weather.applyExtra(state, name) end
	env.getgenv().__pulseWeather = state
	if context.runService.RenderStepped then context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function(delta)
		if state.failureAt and os.clock() < state.failureAt then return end
		local ok, message = pcall(Weather.step, state, delta)
		if not ok then
			state.failureAt = os.clock() + 3
			Weather.stop(state)
			env.warn("[MYTHIC HUB] weather step failed: " .. tostring(message))
		end
	end) end
	return state, function()
		Weather.stop(state)
		if env.getgenv().__pulseWeather == state then env.getgenv().__pulseWeather = nil end
	end
end

local SkyFx = {}
local SKY_FX_ORDER = {"stars", "meteors", "comets", "net", "runes", "dust"}
local SKY_FX_TEXTURES = {dot = "rbxassetid://16512006463", soft = "rbxassetid://1075864321", spark = "rbxassetid://14590086212",
	sharp = "rbxassetid://17391818367", runes = "rbxassetid://11910560348", glyphs = "rbxassetid://10713705343",
	const1 = "rbxassetid://14101033134", const2 = "rbxassetid://14101036795", beam = "rbxassetid://12781750620", rays = "rbxassetid://4553095322"}

function SkyFx.defaults(env)
	local rgb = env.Color3.fromRGB
	return {general = {bright = 100}, stars = {on = false, density = 60, size = 100, twinkle = 60, tint = "Natural"},
		meteors = {on = false, rate = 45, length = 100, color = rgb(190, 225, 255)},
		comets = {on = false, rate = 40, size = 100, color = rgb(120, 210, 255)},
		net = {on = false, nodes = 80, reach = 50, speed = 40, place = "Sky", color = rgb(150, 205, 255), figures = true, pulses = true, rainbow = false, cursor = true},
		runes = {on = false, amount = 50, size = 100, set = "Runes", color = rgb(130, 220, 255)},
		dust = {on = false, amount = 50, palette = "Gold"}}
end

function SkyFx.palettes(env)
	local rgb = env.Color3.fromRGB
	return {stars = {Natural = {rgb(232, 240, 255), rgb(170, 200, 255), rgb(255, 226, 190)},
		Cold = {rgb(205, 225, 255), rgb(140, 185, 255), rgb(190, 240, 255)},
		Warm = {rgb(255, 238, 214), rgb(255, 205, 150), rgb(255, 180, 140)},
		Pastel = {rgb(255, 205, 236), rgb(195, 255, 232), rgb(214, 196, 255)}},
		dust = {Gold = {rgb(255, 220, 130), rgb(255, 190, 90)}, Silver = {rgb(235, 242, 255), rgb(190, 210, 255)},
		Pink = {rgb(255, 170, 220), rgb(255, 120, 190)}, Aqua = {rgb(140, 255, 235), rgb(110, 200, 255)}}}
end

function SkyFx.create(state, class, parent, properties)
	local object = state.context.environment.Instance.new(class)
	for key, value in pairs(properties or {}) do object[key] = value end
	object.Parent = parent
	return object
end

function SkyFx.part(state, parent, name)
	local env = state.context.environment
	return SkyFx.create(state, "Part", parent, {Name = name, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanTouch = false,
		CanQuery = false, CastShadow = false, Transparency = 1, Locked = true, Size = env.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)})
end

function SkyFx.emitter(state, parent, texture, life, speed, brightness, locked)
	local env = state.context.environment
	return SkyFx.create(state, "ParticleEmitter", parent, {Enabled = false, Rate = 0, Texture = texture,
		LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0, Brightness = brightness, Lifetime = env.NumberRange.new(life[1], life[2] or life[1]),
		Speed = env.NumberRange.new(speed and speed[1] or 0, speed and (speed[2] or speed[1]) or 0),
		LockedToPart = locked == true, Orientation = env.Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera})
end

function SkyFx.sequence(state, points)
	local env, result = state.context.environment, {}
	for _, point in ipairs(points) do table.insert(result, env.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(point[1], point[2], point[3] or 0)) end
	return env.NumberSequence.new(result)
end

function SkyFx.rainbow(state, offset)
	local env, result = state.context.environment, {}
	for index = 0, 6 do table.insert(result, env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(index / 6, env.Color3.fromHSV((offset + index / 6) % 1, 0.55, 1))) end
	return env.ColorSequence.new(result)
end

function SkyFx.particle(state, parent, texture, life, speed, brightness, locked, sphere)
	local emitter = SkyFx.emitter(state, parent, texture, life, speed, brightness, locked)
	local env = state.context.environment
	emitter.Shape = sphere and env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Sphere or env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
	emitter.ShapeStyle = sphere and env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Surface or env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
	if sphere then
		emitter.ShapeInOut, emitter.EmissionDirection = env.Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward, env.Enum.NormalId.Top
		pcall(function() emitter.ShapePartial = 0.5 end)
	end
	return emitter
end

function SkyFx.buildParticles(state, name, folder)
	local env, texture = state.context.environment, SKY_FX_TEXTURES
	local part = SkyFx.part(state, folder, name)
	local fx = {folder = folder, part = part, dirty = true, filled = false}
	if name == "stars" then
		fx.small = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.dot, {4, 9}, nil, 2.6, true, true)
		fx.tint = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.dot, {4, 8}, nil, 2.8, true, true)
		fx.big = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.spark, {5, 10}, nil, 3, true, true)
		return fx
	end
	if name == "runes" then
		part.Size = env.Vector3.new(80, 22, 80)
		fx.main = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.runes, {6, 10}, {0.5, 1.5}, 2.6)
		fx.main.Drag, fx.main.EmissionDirection, fx.main.SpreadAngle = 0.3, env.Enum.NormalId.Top, env.Vector2.new(25, 25)
		fx.main.FlipbookLayout, fx.main.FlipbookMode = env.Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4, env.Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot
		fx.main.FlipbookFramerate, fx.main.FlipbookStartRandom = env.NumberRange.new(0, 0), true
		fx.glow = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.dot, {3, 6}, {0.3, 1}, 2)
		fx.glow.EmissionDirection = env.Enum.NormalId.Top
		return fx
	end
	part.Size = env.Vector3.new(90, 30, 90)
	fx.main = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.spark, {4, 8}, {0.1, 0.5}, 2.6)
	fx.glow = SkyFx.particle(state, part, texture.dot, {3, 7}, {0.1, 0.4}, 2.2)
	fx.main.SpreadAngle, fx.glow.SpreadAngle = env.Vector2.new(180, 180), env.Vector2.new(180, 180)
	fx.main.Acceleration, fx.glow.Acceleration = env.Vector3.new(0, -0.15, 0), env.Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
	return fx
end

function SkyFx.applyStars(state, fx)
	local env, cfg, radius = state.context.environment, state.cfg.stars, state.radius
	local brightness, size = state.cfg.general.bright / 100, cfg.size / 100
	fx.part.Size = env.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.min(radius * 2, 2040)
	local twinkle = 0.08 + 0.62 * cfg.twinkle / 100
	local transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.1, 0.04}, {0.22, twinkle}, {0.34, 0.04}, {0.46, twinkle * 0.8},
		{0.58, 0.04}, {0.7, twinkle}, {0.82, 0.08}, {1, 1}})
	fx.small.Transparency, fx.tint.Transparency = transparency, transparency
	fx.small.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, radius * 0.03 * size, radius * 0.012 * size}, {1, radius * 0.03 * size, radius * 0.012 * size}})
	fx.tint.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, radius * 0.036 * size, radius * 0.012 * size}, {1, radius * 0.036 * size, radius * 0.012 * size}})
	local large = radius * 0.055 * size
	fx.big.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0}, {0.14, large}, {0.3, large * 0.55}, {0.46, large}, {0.62, large * 0.6}, {0.8, large}, {1, 0}})
	fx.big.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.12, 0.05}, {0.88, 0.1}, {1, 1}})
	fx.small.Brightness, fx.tint.Brightness, fx.big.Brightness = 2.6 * brightness, 2.8 * brightness, 3 * brightness
	fx.small.Rate, fx.tint.Rate, fx.big.Rate = cfg.density * 1.25, cfg.density * 0.36, cfg.density * 0.07
	fx.small.Enabled, fx.tint.Enabled, fx.big.Enabled = cfg.density > 0, cfg.density > 0, cfg.density > 0
	if cfg.tint == "Rainbow" then
		fx.small.Color, fx.tint.Color, fx.big.Color = SkyFx.rainbow(state, 0), SkyFx.rainbow(state, 0.5), SkyFx.rainbow(state, 0.25)
	else
		local colors = state.palettes.stars[cfg.tint] or state.palettes.stars.Natural
		fx.small.Color, fx.tint.Color, fx.big.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(colors[1]), env.ColorSequence.new(colors[2], colors[3]), env.ColorSequence.new(colors[1])
	end
	if not fx.filled and cfg.density > 0 then
		fx.filled = true
		fx.small:Emit(math.floor(cfg.density * 3.6))
		fx.tint:Emit(math.floor(cfg.density))
		fx.big:Emit(math.floor(cfg.density * 0.2))
	end
end

function SkyFx.applyRunes(state, fx)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.runes
	local size, brightness = cfg.size / 100, state.cfg.general.bright / 100
	fx.main.Texture = cfg.set == "Glyphs" and SKY_FX_TEXTURES.glyphs or SKY_FX_TEXTURES.runes
	fx.main.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1.02 * size}, {0.15, 1.7 * size}, {0.85, 1.7 * size}, {1, 1.36 * size}})
	fx.main.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.15, 0.05}, {0.75, 0.15}, {1, 1}})
	fx.glow.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0.5 * size}, {1, 0}})
	fx.glow.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0.3}, {1, 1}})
	fx.main.Color, fx.glow.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color), env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color)
	fx.main.Brightness, fx.glow.Brightness = 2.6 * brightness, 2 * brightness
	fx.main.Rate, fx.glow.Rate = cfg.amount * 0.32, cfg.amount * 0.3
	fx.main.Enabled, fx.glow.Enabled = cfg.amount > 0, cfg.amount > 0
	if not fx.filled and cfg.amount > 0 then fx.filled = true; fx.main:Emit(math.floor(cfg.amount * 1.6)) end
end

function SkyFx.applyDust(state, fx)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.dust
	fx.main.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0}, {0.15, 0.75}, {0.3, 0.2625}, {0.45, 0.75}, {0.6, 0.3}, {0.78, 0.75}, {1, 0}})
	fx.main.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.12, 0}, {0.85, 0.1}, {1, 1}})
	fx.glow.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0.38}, {1, 0.14}})
	fx.glow.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.15, 0.1}, {0.5, 0.5}, {0.75, 0.1}, {1, 1}})
	if cfg.palette == "Rainbow" then fx.main.Color, fx.glow.Color = SkyFx.rainbow(state, 0), SkyFx.rainbow(state, 0.4)
	else
		local colors = state.palettes.dust[cfg.palette] or state.palettes.dust.Gold
		fx.main.Color, fx.glow.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(colors[1]), env.ColorSequence.new(colors[2])
	end
	local brightness = state.cfg.general.bright / 100
	fx.main.Brightness, fx.glow.Brightness = 3.2 * brightness, 2.6 * brightness
	fx.main.Rate, fx.glow.Rate = cfg.amount * 0.9, cfg.amount * 1.1
	fx.main.Enabled, fx.glow.Enabled = cfg.amount > 0, cfg.amount > 0
	if not fx.filled and cfg.amount > 0 then
		fx.filled = true
		fx.main:Emit(math.floor(cfg.amount * 3))
		fx.glow:Emit(math.floor(cfg.amount * 3))
	end
end

function SkyFx.beam(state, parent, first, second, brightness)
	local env = state.context.environment
	return SkyFx.create(state, "Beam", parent, {Attachment0 = first, Attachment1 = second, FaceCamera = true, LightEmission = 1,
		LightInfluence = 0, Brightness = brightness, Segments = 1, Width0 = 0, Width1 = 1,
		Texture = SKY_FX_TEXTURES.beam, TextureMode = env.Enum.TextureMode.Stretch, Enabled = false})
end

function SkyFx.buildTrails(state, name, folder)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local anchor = SkyFx.part(state, folder, name .. "Anchor")
	anchor.CFrame = env.CFrame.new()
	local fx = {folder = folder, anchor = anchor, pool = {}, dirty = true, nextSpawn = state.time + (name == "meteors" and 0.5 or 1.2)}
	local count = name == "meteors" and 7 or 2
	for _ = 1, count do
		local first, second = SkyFx.create(state, "Attachment", anchor), SkyFx.create(state, "Attachment", anchor)
		local trail = {first = first, second = second, beams = {}, active = false}
		if name == "comets" then trail.ion = SkyFx.create(state, "Attachment", anchor) end
		local beamCount = name == "meteors" and 2 or 5
		for index = 1, beamCount do
			local beam = SkyFx.beam(state, anchor, first, index > 3 and trail.ion or second, index == 1 and 3.2 or 1.4)
			if name == "comets" then beam.Segments = index <= 3 and 24 or 12 end
			table.insert(trail.beams, beam)
		end
		trail.head = SkyFx.part(state, folder, name .. "Head")
		trail.head.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
		trail.glow = SkyFx.emitter(state, trail.head, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.soft, {name == "meteors" and 30 or 60}, nil, 1.4, true)
		trail.star = SkyFx.emitter(state, trail.head, name == "meteors" and SKY_FX_TEXTURES.spark or SKY_FX_TEXTURES.dot,
			{name == "meteors" and 30 or 60}, nil, 3, true)
		trail.dust = SkyFx.emitter(state, trail.head, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.dot, {0.3, 0.8}, {0.2, 1}, 2.2)
		trail.dust.SpreadAngle = env.Vector2.new(180, 180)
		if name == "comets" then
			trail.flare = SkyFx.emitter(state, trail.head, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.spark, {1.6, 2.4}, nil, 2.4, true)
			trail.dust2 = SkyFx.emitter(state, trail.head, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.dot, {2, 4}, {0.2, 1.5}, 2)
			trail.dust2.SpreadAngle = env.Vector2.new(180, 180)
		end
		table.insert(fx.pool, trail)
	end
	return fx
end

function SkyFx.applyTrails(state, name, fx)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg[name]
	local scale = name == "comets" and cfg.size / 100 or 1
	for _, trail in ipairs(fx.pool) do
		for _, beam in ipairs(trail.beams) do beam.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color) end
		local white = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		if name == "meteors" then
			trail.beams[1].Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cfg.color),
				env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, cfg.color:Lerp(white, 0.5)), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, white)})
		else
			trail.beams[1].Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, white),
				env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.12, cfg.color:Lerp(white, 0.35)), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cfg.color)})
			trail.beams[2].Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color:Lerp(white, 0.3), cfg.color)
			trail.beams[4].Color = env.ColorSequence.new(white, env.Color3.fromRGB(120, 175, 255))
			trail.beams[5].Color = env.ColorSequence.new(env.Color3.fromRGB(120, 175, 255))
		end
		trail.glow.Color, trail.dust.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color), env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color)
		trail.glow.Size, trail.star.Size = env.NumberSequence.new(state.radius * 0.032 * scale), env.NumberSequence.new(state.radius * 0.03 * scale)
		trail.dust.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, state.radius * 0.007 * scale}, {1, 0}})
		trail.dust.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0.1}, {1, 1}})
		if name == "comets" then
			trail.flare.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, state.radius * 0.042 * scale}, {0.5, state.radius * 0.07 * scale}, {1, state.radius * 0.042 * scale}})
			trail.flare.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.3, 0.2}, {0.7, 0.2}, {1, 1}})
			trail.dust2.Size = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, state.radius * 0.006 * scale}, {1, 0}})
			trail.dust2.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 0.2}, {1, 1}})
			trail.dust2.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color)
		end
	end
end

function SkyFx.direction(state, azimuth, elevation)
	local cos = math.cos(elevation)
	return state.context.environment.Vector3.new(cos * math.cos(azimuth), math.sin(elevation), cos * math.sin(azimuth))
end

function SkyFx.arc(first, second, t)
	local angle = math.acos(math.clamp(first:Dot(second), -1, 1))
	if angle < 0.0001 then return first end
	local sine = math.sin(angle)
	return first * (math.sin((1 - t) * angle) / sine) + second * (math.sin(t * angle) / sine)
end

function SkyFx.spawnTrail(state, name, fx, look)
	local trail
	for _, candidate in ipairs(fx.pool) do if not candidate.active then trail = candidate; break end end
	if not trail then return end
	local comet = name == "comets"
	local azimuth = math.atan2(look.Z, look.X) + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * (comet and 0.96 or 1.05)
	if not comet and math.random() >= 0.8 then azimuth = math.random() * math.pi * 2 end
	local elevation = math.rad(comet and 26 + math.random() * 26 or 30 + math.random() * 42)
	local direction = math.random() < 0.5 and -1 or 1
	trail.start = SkyFx.direction(state, azimuth, elevation)
	trail.finish = SkyFx.direction(state, azimuth + direction * math.rad(comet and 26 + math.random() * 18 or 16 + math.random() * 24),
		elevation + math.rad(comet and -12 + math.random() * 20 or -14 - math.random() * 14))
	trail.started, trail.duration, trail.active = state.time, comet and 18 + math.random() * 10 or 0.6 + math.random() * 0.7, true
	trail.tail = comet and 0.3 or 0.3 * state.cfg.meteors.length / 100 * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.45)
	for _, beam in ipairs(trail.beams) do beam.Enabled = true end
	trail.glow:Emit(1)
	trail.star:Emit(1)
	trail.dust.Rate, trail.dust.Enabled = comet and 26 or 80, true
	if comet then
		trail.flare.Rate, trail.flare.Enabled = 1.4, true
		trail.dust2.Rate, trail.dust2.Enabled = 34, true
	end
end

function SkyFx.stepTrails(state, name, fx, position, look)
	local cfg = state.cfg[name]
	local comet = name == "comets"
	if state.time >= fx.nextSpawn then
		SkyFx.spawnTrail(state, name, fx, look)
		local delay = comet and 6 + 70 * (1 - cfg.rate / 100) ^ 2 or 0.3 + 9 * (1 - cfg.rate / 100) ^ 2
		fx.nextSpawn = state.time + delay * (comet and 0.5 + math.random() or 0.35 + 1.3 * math.random())
	end
	local brightness = state.cfg.general.bright / 100
	for _, trail in ipairs(fx.pool) do
		if not trail.active then continue end
		local progress = (state.time - trail.started) / trail.duration
		if progress >= 1 + (comet and 0 or trail.tail) then
			trail.active, trail.dust.Enabled = false, false
			if comet then trail.flare.Enabled, trail.dust2.Enabled = false, false end
			for _, beam in ipairs(trail.beams) do beam.Enabled = false end
			trail.glow:Clear()
			trail.star:Clear()
			trail.head.CFrame = state.context.environment.CFrame.new(0, -10000, 0)
			continue
		end
		local direction = SkyFx.arc(trail.start, trail.finish, math.min(progress, 1))
		local head = position + direction * state.radius
		local fade
		if comet then
			local ahead = SkyFx.arc(trail.start, trail.finish, math.min(progress + 0.01, 1)) - direction
			local tangent = ahead.Magnitude > 1e-6 and ahead.Unit or state.context.environment.Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
			local side = tangent:Cross(direction).Unit
			local size = cfg.size / 100
			local dust = (direction - tangent * 0.3 * size + side * 0.04 * size).Unit
			local ion = (direction - tangent * 0.42 * size - side * 0.02 * size).Unit
			trail.first.CFrame = state.context.environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(head, side, direction)
			trail.second.CFrame = state.context.environment.CFrame.fromMatrix(position + dust * state.radius, side, dust)
			trail.ion.Position = position + ion * state.radius
			fade = math.clamp(math.min(progress * trail.duration / 2.5, (1 - progress) * trail.duration / 3), 0, 1)
			local widths = {{0.022, 0.08}, {0.04, 0.15}, {0.065, 0.24}, {0.012, 0.03}, {0.026, 0.07}}
			local alphas = {0.75, 0.45, 0.25, 0.7, 0.3}
			local levels = {1.4, 0.9, 0.6, 1.8, 0.8}
			for index, beam in ipairs(trail.beams) do
				beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = state.radius * size * widths[index][1], state.radius * size * widths[index][2]
				beam.CurveSize0, beam.CurveSize1 = index <= 3 and state.radius * 0.05 * size or 0, 0
				beam.Brightness = levels[index] * brightness
				beam.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1 - alphas[index] * fade}, {1, 1}})
			end
			trail.flare.Brightness, trail.dust2.Brightness = 2.4 * brightness * fade, 2 * brightness * fade
		else
			local tail = position + SkyFx.arc(trail.start, trail.finish, math.clamp(progress - trail.tail, 0, 1)) * state.radius
			trail.first.Position, trail.second.Position = tail, head
			fade = math.clamp(math.min(progress / 0.12, (1 + trail.tail - progress) / (trail.tail + 0.2)), 0, 1)
			for index, beam in ipairs(trail.beams) do
				beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = 0, state.radius * (index == 1 and 0.016 or 0.055)
				beam.Brightness = (index == 1 and 3.2 or 1.4) * brightness
				beam.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {1, 1 - fade * (index == 1 and 1 or 0.4)}})
			end
		end
		trail.head.CFrame = state.context.environment.CFrame.new(head)
		trail.glow.Brightness, trail.star.Brightness = 1.4 * brightness * fade, 3 * brightness * fade
		if progress > 0.95 and not comet then trail.dust.Enabled = false end
	end
end

function SkyFx.buildNet(state, folder)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.net
	local fx = {folder = folder, nodes = {}, links = {}, dirty = true, count = math.clamp(math.floor(cfg.nodes), 8, 140), place = cfg.place, linkClock = 1}
	local look = state.camera.CFrame.LookVector
	local forward = math.atan2(look.Z, look.X)
	for index = 1, fx.count do
		local part = SkyFx.part(state, folder, "Node")
		local attachment = SkyFx.create(state, "Attachment", part)
		local emitter = SkyFx.emitter(state, part, index % 6 == 0 and SKY_FX_TEXTURES.spark or SKY_FX_TEXTURES.dot, {12}, nil, 2.8, true)
		local node = {part = part, attachment = attachment, emitter = emitter, big = index % 6 == 0,
			azimuth = math.random() < 0.75 and forward + (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 1.25 or math.random() * math.pi * 2,
			elevation = math.rad(14 + math.random() * 58),
			drift = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.022, vertical = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.014,
			relative = env.Vector3.new((math.random() * 2 - 1) * 44, -10 + math.random() * 24, (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 44),
			velocity = env.Vector3.new(math.random() * 2 - 1, (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.4, math.random() * 2 - 1),
			nextEmit = state.time + math.random() * 6, hue = math.random()}
		table.insert(fx.nodes, node)
	end
	fx.cursorAnchor = SkyFx.part(state, folder, "CursorAnchor")
	fx.cursorAnchor.CFrame = env.CFrame.new()
	fx.cursor = SkyFx.create(state, "Attachment", fx.cursorAnchor)
	for _ = 1, fx.count * 2 + 6 do
		local beam = SkyFx.beam(state, folder, nil, nil, 1.8)
		local pulse = SkyFx.beam(state, folder, nil, nil, 3)
		pulse.Texture, pulse.TextureMode = SKY_FX_TEXTURES.rays, env.Enum.TextureMode.Wrap
		table.insert(fx.links, {beam = beam, pulse = pulse})
	end
	fx.shell = SkyFx.part(state, folder, "Constellations")
	fx.figures = {SkyFx.particle(state, fx.shell, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.const1, {9, 13}, nil, 1.8, true, true),
		SkyFx.particle(state, fx.shell, SKY_FX_TEXTURES.const2, {9, 13}, nil, 1.8, true, true)}
	return fx
end

function SkyFx.applyNet(state, fx)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.net
	local sky, brightness = cfg.place ~= "Around me", state.cfg.general.bright / 100
	for _, node in ipairs(fx.nodes) do
		local size = sky and state.radius * (node.big and 0.06 or 0.036) or (node.big and 1.7 or 1)
		node.emitter.Size = env.NumberSequence.new(size)
		node.emitter.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color:Lerp(env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.45))
		node.emitter.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.15, 0}, {0.85, 0}, {1, 1}})
		node.emitter.Brightness = 2.8 * brightness
		if not node.seeded then node.seeded = true; node.emitter:Emit(1) end
	end
	for _, link in ipairs(fx.links) do
		local width = sky and state.radius * 0.0075 or 0.16
		link.beam.Width0, link.beam.Width1 = width, width
		link.pulse.Width0, link.pulse.Width1 = width * 1.9, width * 1.9
		link.pulse.TextureLength, link.pulse.TextureSpeed = sky and state.radius * 0.2 or 7, 0.35 + 0.6 * cfg.speed / 100
		link.beam.Color, link.pulse.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color), env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color)
		link.beam.Brightness, link.pulse.Brightness = 1.8 * brightness, 5 * brightness
	end
	fx.shell.Size = env.Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * math.min(state.radius * 1.9, 2040)
	for _, emitter in ipairs(fx.figures) do
		emitter.Size, emitter.Color = env.NumberSequence.new(state.radius * 0.2), env.ColorSequence.new(cfg.color:Lerp(env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.4))
		emitter.Transparency = SkyFx.sequence(state, {{0, 1}, {0.25, 0.3}, {0.75, 0.3}, {1, 1}})
		emitter.Brightness, emitter.Rate, emitter.Enabled = 1.8 * brightness, 0.06, sky and cfg.figures
	end
	fx.linkClock = 1
end

function SkyFx.linkNet(state, fx)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.net
	local sky = cfg.place ~= "Around me"
	local maxDistance = sky and 2 * state.radius * 0.92 * math.sin(math.rad(5 + 20 * cfg.reach / 100) / 2) or 10 + 24 * cfg.reach / 100
	local used, degrees = 0, {}
	local function connect(firstNode, attachment)
		if used >= #fx.links then return end
		used += 1
		local link = fx.links[used]
		link.beam.Attachment0, link.beam.Attachment1 = firstNode.attachment, attachment
		link.pulse.Attachment0, link.pulse.Attachment1 = firstNode.attachment, attachment
		link.beam.Enabled, link.pulse.Enabled = true, cfg.pulses
		local color = cfg.rainbow and env.Color3.fromHSV((firstNode.hue + state.time * 0.05) % 1, 0.6, 1) or cfg.color
		link.beam.Color, link.pulse.Color = env.ColorSequence.new(color), env.ColorSequence.new(color)
	end
	for first = 1, #fx.nodes do
		for second = first + 1, #fx.nodes do
			if (degrees[first] or 0) >= 4 then break end
			if (degrees[second] or 0) >= 4 then continue end
			local firstNode, secondNode = fx.nodes[first], fx.nodes[second]
			if (firstNode.position - secondNode.position).Magnitude > maxDistance then continue end
			degrees[first], degrees[second] = (degrees[first] or 0) + 1, (degrees[second] or 0) + 1
			connect(firstNode, secondNode.attachment)
		end
	end
	if fx.cursorPosition then
		local nearest = {}
		for _, node in ipairs(fx.nodes) do
			local distance = (node.position - fx.cursorPosition).Magnitude
			if distance <= maxDistance * 1.3 then table.insert(nearest, {node = node, distance = distance}) end
		end
		table.sort(nearest, function(a, b) return a.distance < b.distance end)
		for index = 1, math.min(6, #nearest) do connect(nearest[index].node, fx.cursor) end
	end
	for index = used + 1, #fx.links do fx.links[index].beam.Enabled, fx.links[index].pulse.Enabled = false, false end
end

function SkyFx.cursor(state, fx, position)
	if not state.cfg.net.cursor then return nil end
	local camera, input = state.camera, state.context.inputService
	local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
	local x, y = viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2
	if input and input.MouseEnabled then
		local mouse = input:GetMouseLocation()
		x, y = mouse.X, mouse.Y
	end
	local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(x, y)
	if state.cfg.net.place == "Around me" then return ray.Origin + ray.Direction.Unit * 26 end
	if ray.Direction.Y <= -0.05 then return nil end
	return position + ray.Direction.Unit * state.radius * 0.92
end

function SkyFx.stepNet(state, fx, position, playerPosition, delta)
	local env, cfg = state.context.environment, state.cfg.net
	local sky, speed = cfg.place ~= "Around me", cfg.speed / 100
	fx.shell.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(position)
	fx.cursorPosition = SkyFx.cursor(state, fx, position)
	if fx.cursorPosition then fx.cursor.Position = fx.cursorPosition end
	for _, node in ipairs(fx.nodes) do
		if sky then
			node.azimuth += node.drift * delta * speed
			node.elevation += node.vertical * delta * speed
			if node.elevation < math.rad(12) then node.vertical = math.abs(node.vertical) end
			if node.elevation > math.rad(74) then node.vertical = -math.abs(node.vertical) end
			node.position = position + SkyFx.direction(state, node.azimuth, node.elevation) * state.radius * 0.92
		else
			node.relative += node.velocity * delta * speed * 2.5
			local relative, velocity = node.relative, node.velocity
			if math.abs(relative.X) > 44 then velocity = env.Vector3.new(-math.sign(relative.X) * math.abs(velocity.X), velocity.Y, velocity.Z) end
			if relative.Y < -10 or relative.Y > 14 then velocity = env.Vector3.new(velocity.X, (relative.Y < 0 and 1 or -1) * math.abs(velocity.Y), velocity.Z) end
			if math.abs(relative.Z) > 44 then velocity = env.Vector3.new(velocity.X, velocity.Y, -math.sign(relative.Z) * math.abs(velocity.Z)) end
			node.velocity = velocity
			node.position = playerPosition + env.Vector3.new(0, 12, 0) + node.relative
		end
		node.part.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(node.position)
		if state.time >= node.nextEmit then node.emitter:Emit(1); node.nextEmit = state.time + 5 + math.random() * 3 end
	end
	fx.linkClock += delta
	if fx.linkClock >= 0.05 then fx.linkClock = 0; SkyFx.linkNet(state, fx) end
end

function SkyFx.drop(state, name)
	local effect = state.effects[name]
	state.effects[name] = nil
	if effect and effect.folder then pcall(function() effect.folder:Destroy() end) end
end

function SkyFx.clear(state)
	for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do SkyFx.drop(state, name) end
	if state.folder then pcall(function() state.folder:Destroy() end) end
	state.folder, state.camera = nil, nil
end

function SkyFx.dirty(state, name)
	if name == "general" then
		for _, effect in pairs(state.effects) do effect.dirty = true end
		return
	end
	if state.effects[name] then state.effects[name].dirty = true end
end

function SkyFx.set(state, name, key, value)
	local settings = state.cfg[name]
	if not settings then return end
	settings[key] = value
	SkyFx.dirty(state, name)
end

function SkyFx.setOn(state, name, enabled)
	local handle = state.controls[name]
	if handle and handle.Set then handle.Set(enabled); return end
	SkyFx.set(state, name, "on", enabled)
end

function SkyFx.scene(state, names)
	if not state.sceneSaved then
		state.sceneSaved = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do state.sceneSaved[name] = state.cfg[name].on end
	end
	local selected = {}
	for _, name in ipairs(names or {}) do selected[name] = true end
	for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do SkyFx.setOn(state, name, selected[name] == true) end
	if state.paint then state.paint() end
end

function SkyFx.sceneEnd(state)
	local saved = state.sceneSaved
	state.sceneSaved = nil
	if not saved then return end
	for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do SkyFx.setOn(state, name, saved[name] == true) end
	if state.paint then state.paint() end
end

function SkyFx.build(state, name)
	local folder = SkyFx.create(state, "Folder", state.folder, {Name = name})
	local ok, effect = pcall(function()
		if name == "stars" or name == "runes" or name == "dust" then return SkyFx.buildParticles(state, name, folder) end
		if name == "net" then return SkyFx.buildNet(state, folder) end
		return SkyFx.buildTrails(state, name, folder)
	end)
	if ok then return effect end
	folder:Destroy()
	error(effect, 0)
end

function SkyFx.apply(state, name, effect)
	if name == "stars" then return SkyFx.applyStars(state, effect) end
	if name == "runes" then return SkyFx.applyRunes(state, effect) end
	if name == "dust" then return SkyFx.applyDust(state, effect) end
	if name == "net" then return SkyFx.applyNet(state, effect) end
	return SkyFx.applyTrails(state, name, effect)
end

function SkyFx.applySquash(state, name, effect)
	local env = state.context.environment
	local squash = env.NumberSequence.new(state.squash)
	for _, object in ipairs(effect.folder:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then pcall(function() object.Squash = squash end) end
	end
	local rotated = state.squash <= 0.05
	if name == "stars" then
		effect.big.Rotation = rotated and env.NumberRange.new(-20, 20) or env.NumberRange.new(0, 0)
		effect.big.RotSpeed = rotated and env.NumberRange.new(-12, 12) or env.NumberRange.new(0, 0)
	end
	if name == "runes" then effect.main.Rotation = rotated and env.NumberRange.new(-10, 10) or env.NumberRange.new(0, 0) end
	if name == "dust" then
		effect.main.Rotation = rotated and env.NumberRange.new(0, 45) or env.NumberRange.new(0, 0)
		effect.main.RotSpeed = rotated and env.NumberRange.new(-30, 30) or env.NumberRange.new(0, 0)
	end
end

function SkyFx.stepEffect(state, name, effect, position, look, playerPosition, delta)
	local env = state.context.environment
	if name == "stars" then effect.part.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(position); return end
	if name == "runes" then effect.part.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(playerPosition + env.Vector3.new(0, 9, 0)); return end
	if name == "dust" then effect.part.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(playerPosition + env.Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)); return end
	if name == "net" then return SkyFx.stepNet(state, effect, position, playerPosition, delta) end
	return SkyFx.stepTrails(state, name, effect, position, look)
end

function SkyFx.step(state, delta)
	local context = state.context
	if not context.isAlive() then SkyFx.clear(state); return end
	local active = false
	for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do if state.cfg[name].on then active = true; break end end
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not active or not camera then if state.folder then SkyFx.clear(state) end; return end
	if not state.folder or state.camera ~= camera or state.folder.Parent ~= camera then
		SkyFx.clear(state)
		state.folder = SkyFx.create(state, "Folder", camera, {Name = "PulseSkyFX"})
		state.camera = camera
	end
	delta = math.clamp(tonumber(delta) or 1 / 60, 0, 0.1)
	state.time += delta
	state.environmentClock += delta
	if state.environmentClock >= 0.5 then
		state.environmentClock = 0
		local atmosphere = context.lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
		local radius = math.clamp(atmosphere and 72 / math.max(atmosphere.Density, 0.04) or context.lighting.FogEnd * 0.5, 110, 520)
		if math.abs(radius - state.radius) / state.radius > 0.08 then state.radius = radius; SkyFx.dirty(state, "general") end
		local stretch = context.configuration.ui and tonumber(context.configuration.ui.stretch) or 0
		local squash = stretch > 0 and math.floor(-math.log(math.max(0.1, 1 - stretch / 100 * 0.6), 2) * 100 + 0.5) / 100 or 0
		if math.abs(squash - state.squash) > 0.02 then state.squash = squash; SkyFx.dirty(state, "general") end
	end
	local frame, character = camera.CFrame, context.player.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local playerPosition = root and root.Position or frame.Position
	for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do
		local cfg, effect = state.cfg[name], state.effects[name]
		if not cfg.on then if effect then SkyFx.drop(state, name) end; continue end
		if name == "net" and effect and (effect.count ~= math.clamp(math.floor(cfg.nodes), 8, 140) or effect.place ~= cfg.place) then
			SkyFx.drop(state, name)
			effect = nil
		end
		if not effect and state.time >= (state.failures[name] or 0) then
			local ok, result = pcall(SkyFx.build, state, name)
			if ok then effect = result; state.effects[name] = effect
			else state.failures[name] = state.time + 5; context.environment.warn("[MYTHIC HUB] sky fx " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(result)) end
		end
		if not effect then continue end
		if effect.dirty then
			effect.dirty = false
			local ok, message = pcall(function()
				SkyFx.apply(state, name, effect)
				SkyFx.applySquash(state, name, effect)
			end)
			if not ok then context.environment.warn("[MYTHIC HUB] sky fx " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(message)) end
		end
		local ok, message = pcall(SkyFx.stepEffect, state, name, effect, frame.Position, frame.LookVector, playerPosition, delta)
		if not ok then
			state.failures[name] = state.time + 5
			SkyFx.drop(state, name)
			context.environment.warn("[MYTHIC HUB] sky fx " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(message))
		end
	end
end

function SkyFx.gallery(state)
	local context, env = state.context, state.context.environment
